### [1] [Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421)
*Ilker Gurcan,Kadir Ustun,Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种算子理论框架，仅从采样射流空间数据中发现微分方程的内在李点对称生成元，无需符号确定方程或控制系统的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性对模型降维与科学机器学习有重要价值，但传统发现方法依赖符号确定方程与系统闭式表示，难以用于仅能获得数值解或近似系统的数据场景。

Method: 将内在李点对称发现表述为射流空间采样数据上的算子理论问题：由采样的确定算子识别可容许生成元，并将其与冗余、依赖解及几何平凡方向分离。框架结合延拓、序贯子空间限制，以及关于可训练系数线性的固定非线性字典；并引入秩稳定预检验，用于诊断所表示的内在对称代数在字典细化下不稳定的系统。

Result: 在具有解析已知11维所表示内在对称代数的受控微分方程基准上，方法恢复了完整的所表示生成元集合，以数值精度重现其李括号结构，并能外推到训练域之外近似已知生成元。

Conclusion: 可从精确或近似微分系统的数据中显式恢复内在连续对称生成元。

Abstract: Discovering continuous symmetries directly from differential-equation data can reveal geometric structure for model reduction and scientific machine learning without symbolic determining equations or a closed-form representation of the governing system. We formulate intrinsic Lie point-symmetry discovery as an operator-theoretic problem on sampled jet-space data. Admissible generators are identified from a sampled determining operator and separated from redundant, solution-dependent, and geometrically trivial directions. The framework combines prolongation, sequential subspace restrictions, and a fixed nonlinear dictionary linear in trainable coefficients. A rank-stabilization preflight test diagnoses systems whose represented intrinsic symmetry algebra does not stabilize under dictionary refinement. On a controlled differential-equation benchmark with an analytically known 11-dimensional represented intrinsic symmetry algebra, the method recovers the complete represented generator set, reproduces its Lie-bracket structure to numerical precision, and approximates a known generator beyond the training domain. Here we show that explicit intrinsic continuous-symmetry generators can be recovered from data obtained from exact or approximate differential systems.

</details>


### [2] [SteelAgent: LLM-Orchestrated, Physics-Informed Steel Property Prediction with Audited Generalization and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06953)
*Aleksandr Volkov,Roman Sultimov,Mikhail Kuzin,Yury Maximov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随机划分下的钢材性能模型评价会系统性高估外推能力；留出单元（steel family/source）改变后，Gradient Boosting 的池化 out-of-fold R² 从 0.859 降至 0.113，名义90%预测区间覆盖从91.0%降至37.6%；物理特征效应亦随评价设定变号。作者据此主张在报告性能估计或合规分数时须同时声明评价总体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钢材性能/强度模型常在随机测试划分上表现良好，但对陌生合金外推能力差；SteelAgent 等工具将冶炼描述符、ML 模型与语言模型接口耦合，用于性能预测、不确定性报告与逆向设计，因此需要一个能反映真实泛化与校准的评价规范。

Method: 基于 SteelBench 抗拉强度数据，比较不同留出单元（随机划分 vs. 按 steel family 留出 vs. 留出 source / 四牌号实验室分析）下的评价结论；模型含 Gradient Boosting 等 ML 方法，特征含物理导出描述符；指标包括池化 out-of-fold R²、名义90%预测区间的经验覆盖率、组级误差，并辅以合成回归实验演示组外校准退化。

Result: Gradient Boosting 池化 out-of-fold R²：随机划分为0.859，按钢族留出降至0.113；名义90%区间经验覆盖率由91.0%（随机）降至37.6%（留出 source）；物理特征贡献方向随评价改变——17族留出分析中使 R² 提高0.2534，而在独立四牌号实验室分析中使其下降0.358。

Conclusion: 基于随机划分的性能与校准结论不可外推至陌生合金群体；评价单元的选择会改变模型优劣与特征有效性的判断。应报告组级误差和合成回归校准检验，并在给出性能估计或合规评分时同时报告评价总体（evaluation population）。

Abstract: Steel property models can perform well on random test splits even when they extrapolate poorly to unfamiliar alloys. SteelAgent couples metallurgical descriptors and machine-learning models with a language-model interface for property prediction, uncertainty reporting and inverse design. We examine how the choice of held-out unit changes the conclusions drawn from SteelBench tensile-strength evaluations. For gradient boosting, pooled out-of-fold $R^2$ fell from 0.859 on a random split to 0.113 when steel families were held out. Empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals ranged from 91.0% under random splitting to 37.6% on held-out sources. The effect of physics-derived features depended on the evaluation. They increased gradient-boosting $R^2$ by 0.2534 in a 17-family holdout analysis and decreased it by 0.358 in a separate four-grade laboratory analysis. We report group-level errors and a synthetic regression experiment illustrating how calibration can deteriorate on held-out groups. These results motivate reporting the evaluation population alongside a property estimate or compliance score.

</details>


### [3] [Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956)
*Tianming Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Octopus 是一个单模型多智能体强化学习框架，所有子线程共享同一主干并经由 GPU 常驻的 KV 状态 DAG 协作，通过零拷贝 Radix 分支、仅注意力 KV 挂载与 DAG-GRPO 打包训练，在 BrowseComp-Plus 上达 49.6% 准确率并显著提升吞吐、降低显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 框架存在三类低效与建模缺陷：分支间重复前缀 KV 缓存、以重新预填充文本摘要的方式交换发现结果、以及在 RL 中扁平化或丢弃跨分支依赖；同时单智能体系统将每步检索与验证追加进单一增长轨迹，难以并行探索。

Method: 以单一骨干模型承载全部线程，通过五个原语（fork、read_thread、wait_threads、rewind、commit）在 GPU 常驻的 KV 有向无环图上协调。三个协同设计机制：(1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking，将分支生成为共享物理 KV 页上的 O(1) 游标视图；(2) Attention-Only KV Mounting，将兄弟线程的终态 KV 块直接挂载进全注意力层、绕过线性循环状态（Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2），零额外 KV 分配；(3) Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer，将所有回合分支打包进单次块稀疏注意力前向，并以留一分支的边际奖励分配反事实分支信用。基于 Qwen3.5-4B、稀疏 BM25 语料端到端 GRPO 训练。

Result: BrowseComp-Plus 准确率 49.6%（较 143K 上下文的单智能体 GRPO 提升 17.5 点，较文本摘要式多智能体 RL 提升 12.5 点），OpenResearcher 达 51.2%，超越参数量最多大 250 倍的稠密检索智能体；训练吞吐提升 3.3 倍，峰值 KV 显存降低 2.8 倍。Attention-Only KV Mounting 将离任务劫持从 57.9% 降至 2.7%（约 21 倍削减），同时保留 92.2% 的全文互信息。

Conclusion: 通过将跨分支依赖显式建模为 KV 状态 DAG 并配以零拷贝分支、仅注意力挂载与打包的 DAG 式信用分配，Octopus 在单一模型内实现了高效、可训练的并行多智能体探索，兼顾精度、吞吐与显存效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL).
  We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read_thread, wait_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\times$ ($57.9\% \to 2.7\%$) while retaining $92.2\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards.
  Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\times$.

</details>


### [4] [Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979)
*Ismail Huseynov,Mohammad Orabe,Srikantan Nagarajan,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的不确定性量化与校准框架，比较BMN+sLORETA与SBL+S-FLEX两种经验贝叶斯源成像方法的可信区域校准性，并引入等渗回归后校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M/EEG源成像中后验可信区域极少经受实证校准评估，缺乏对其不确定性估计可靠性的系统验证。

Method: 针对具闭式条件高斯后验的贝叶斯最小范数（BMN，sLORETA深度加权，通过证据最大化推导源与噪声方差的联合更新）与稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL，稀疏基场展开S-FLEX）；仿真中考察固定/自由朝向、不同SNR、源稀疏度与个体头模型，采用oracle、基线估计与自适应学习噪声；并以经验覆盖率进行等渗回归事后校准。

Result: 校准前，SBL+S-FLEX的Wasserstein定位误差更低、后验不确定性更小、覆盖率更接近名义可信度；等渗校准对两者均有改善，保守的BMN+sLORETA增益更大，但跨失配SNR、稀疏度与头模型的迁移依赖具体条件；各噪声设定下趋势基本一致。真实听觉MEG数据中SBL+S-FLEX定位更聚焦、不确定性更紧。

Conclusion: 自适应噪声学习未能完全消除残余校准偏差，事后等渗校准有效；SBL+S-FLEX在定位精度与不确定性紧致度上优于BMN+sLORETA，有望提升双侧听觉源识别的统计功效。

Abstract: M/EEG source imaging requires accurate localization and reliable uncertainty estimates, yet posterior credible regions are rarely assessed for empirical calibration. We present a simulation-based framework for uncertainty quantification and calibration of empirical Bayesian inverse methods with closed-form conditional Gaussian posterior summaries. We compare Bayesian minimum norm (BMN) with sLORETA depth weighting and sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) with sparse basis field expansions (S-FLEX). S-FLEX allows retained coefficients to contribute nonzero marginal posterior variance at locations where voxel-wise SBL yields degenerate variances. For BMN, we derive joint source- and noise-variance updates through evidence maximization. We test whether adaptive noise learning yields near-calibrated uncertainties. Both methods retain residual miscalibration, motivating post-hoc isotonic recalibration of credible-region levels using empirical coverage. Simulations assess fixed- and free-orientation models across signal-to-noise ratios (SNR), source sparsity levels, and subject-specific head models, using oracle, baseline-estimated, and adaptively learned noise. Before recalibration, SBL+S-FLEX yields lower Wasserstein localization error, smaller posterior uncertainty, and coverage closer to nominal credibility than BMN+sLORETA. Isotonic recalibration improves both methods, with larger gains for conservative BMN+sLORETA posteriors; transfer across mismatched SNR, sparsity, and head models remains condition dependent. Noise specifications yield broadly consistent reconstruction and uncertainty trends within each estimator. In real auditory MEG data, SBL+S-FLEX provides more focal localization and tighter uncertainties than BMN+sLORETA, suggesting greater statistical power for identifying bilateral auditory sources.

</details>


### [5] [Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988)
*Rishit Jain,Rishit Kar,Chetashri Bhadane,Nilesh Patil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在MONSTER基准的六个大规模数据集上，以1%/10%/100%标签比例受控对比两个预测基础模型（Reverso、Chronos-Bolt）的冻结表征与四种强时序分类基线及同架构随机权重对照，发现预训练相对随机权重有效（6个数据集中5个提升，最高+23个百分点），但冻结表征在任何设置下都无法可靠超越最强非预训练基线（17个设置中15个更差、2个持平）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测基础模型常被当作冻结特征提取器用于时间序列分类，但其预训练表征是否优于无需预训练的强分类器尚不清楚；核心问题是：在标注数据稀缺时，这些表征是否提供了超越架构本身所能捕获的有用信息。

Method: 对Reverso与Chronos-Bolt在MONSTER基准六个大数据集上进行受控基准测试，使用1%、10%、100%训练标签；将冻结表征与四种成熟时序分类方法、以及同模型随机权重版本进行对比；并进一步开展幅度信息恢复、微调、预测误差特征等补充实验。

Result: 预训练相对随机权重在6个数据集中的5个提升分类精度，增益最高达23个百分点；但冻结表征未能在任何设置下可靠超越最强非预训练基线，17个设置中15个更差、2个持平；恢复幅度信息可弥补部分差距，而微调与预测误差特征无法弥合差距。

Conclusion: 评估预测基础模型用于分类时，必须引入强任务特定基线与随机权重对照；预训练带来的优势主要体现在相对同架构随机权重，而非相对强非预训练分类器。

Abstract: Forecasting foundation models are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for time series classification, but it remains unclear whether their pretrained representations offer an advantage over strong classifiers that do not require pretraining. This raises an important question: do these representations provide useful information beyond what the model architecture alone can capture, particularly when labelled data are scarce? We present a controlled benchmarking of two forecasting models, Reverso and Chronos-Bolt, on six large datasets from the MONSTER benchmark, using 1%, 10%, and 100% of the available training labels. We compare their frozen representations against four established time series classification methods and random-weight versions of the same forecasting models. Our results show that pretraining improves classification accuracy over random-weight counterparts on five of the six datasets, with gains of up to 23 percentage points. However, the frozen representations do not reliably outperform the strongest non-pretrained baseline in any setting: they perform worse in 15 of 17 settings and tie in the remaining two. Further experiments show that restoring amplitude information recovers part of the performance gap, while fine-tuning and forecast-error features do not close it. These findings highlight the importance of strong task specific baselines and random weight controls when evaluating forecasting foundation models for classification. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachet2006/frozen-forecasters-monster.

</details>


### [6] [Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Time Neutralising Trees（TNT）决策树架构，通过"时间中和"机制过滤不再相关的旧样本并在其重新相关时恢复使用，在24个USP DS真实/半真实数据流上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流具有无限性与非平稳性（概念漂移），现有"漂移检测+重置/重训"范式仍占主导；而相关工作表明，在允许存储实例的场景下传统批学习算法常优于流学习算法，因此有必要设计能利用历史样本、同时应对概念漂移的分类器。

Method: TNT 是一种决策树架构。训练时根据流的当前上下文过滤（"中和"）已不再相关的旧训练样本，当这些样本重新变得相关时再将其重新纳入训练，从而在概念漂移下保持鲁棒性；与相关算法在 USP DS 仓库的24个真实与半真实分类数据流上进行对比评测。

Result: 在涵盖多种概念漂移类型的广泛数据流上，结果强有力地表明 TNT 达到了同类最佳（best-in-class）性能。

Conclusion: 放宽"实例仅可见一次"假设、以样本相关性动态调节替代漂移检测与重置，是应对概念漂移的有效途径；TNT 为可存储实例场景下的流分类提供了鲁棒且性能领先的方案。

Abstract: Data Streams are characterised as a potentially infinite source of data subject to non-stationarity. This non-stationarity, also called Concept Drift, often results in deployed predictive models having to be perpetually retrained as the underlying data generating process of the stream changes over time. Drift Detectors are a class of methods used to identify these changes, triggering these retraining procedures. Historically, Stream Learning has operated under the assumption that instances are only seen once, and cannot be stored for later use. Contemporary work relaxes this assumption, yet drift detection and simple resetting procedures are still ubiquitous.
  Building on related work which has shown that traditional Batch Learning algorithms often outperform Stream Learning algorithms when instances can be stored, we introduce Time Neutralising Trees (TNT), a Decision Tree architecture that enables robust stream classification in settings with Concept Drift. During training, TNT will filter out ("neutralise") old training samples that are no longer relevant given the current context of the stream. If those instances become relevant again, TNT will then re-include them. We evaluate TNT, and related algorithms on 24 real and semi-real classification data streams from the USP DS repository. Results show strong evidence that TNT achieves best-in-class performance on a wide-range of data streams with varying concept drift types.

</details>


### [7] [LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025)
*Teresa Delgado,Hugo Hazard,Martin Benfeghoul,Jun Wang,Zafeirios Fountas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LowRAM，一种非对称 KV 缓存压缩流水线：利用同一注意力头内 value 可由 key 高度预测的性质做 value 摊销（per-head 轻量预测器 + 仅对重构误差最大的 token 存残差），配合跨层低秩 key 压缩，实现近无损的 4× 压缩，并在 4× 压缩下取得最高 5× 的峰值解码吞吐；与量化、驱逐组合可达 26.9× 压缩并保留 94.5% 基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码依赖 KV cache，其显存随上下文长度线性增长，限制了 batch size 与吞吐。已有训练后压缩方法主要利用 token、层、特征维度的冗余或降低数值精度，但将 key 与 value 视为同构处理，未挖掘其结构差异带来的新压缩维度。

Method: 核心观察：在每个注意力头内，value 基本可由其对应的 key 预测。据此提出 value cache amortisation——在测试时对每个头拟合轻量预测器，仅为重构最差的 token 存储残差，实现约 4× 近无损 value 压缩；预测器可通过元学习初始化以大幅降低 prefill 开销，或在允许一次性成本的场景（如 multi-query 推理）中从头拟合。key 端采用跨层低秩压缩。二者组合为 LowRAM 非对称流水线，每个解码步仅重构被选中的部分 KV 对以控制开销。

Result: 在三个模型家族上接近无损；4× 压缩下峰值解码吞吐最高提升 5×；与量化、驱逐方法可组合，长上下文下达到 26.9× 压缩并保持 94.5% 基线性能。

Conclusion: 将 key 与 value 视为结构上不同的对象，可支撑高效、激进且可组合的 KV 缓存压缩；value 摊销与 key 低秩压缩互为补充，形成统一的非对称压缩框架。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\times$ compression at $94.5\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

</details>


### [8] [Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065)
*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Yiming Zhang,Luke Snow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从随机动力系统视角统一刻画三类生成式AI模型：LLM作为高维非线性自回归模型、交互LLM网络的均值场ODEs、以及神经常微分方程(SDEs)的路径空间生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型（LLM及其网络、连续时间生成模型）缺乏统一的概率与动力系统理论刻画，本文旨在用随机动力系统框架为其提供可解释的概率建模与训练分析工具。

Method: 三点：(1) 将LLM表述为带注意力依赖的高维非线性自回归模型，把条件极大似然预训练与序列token生成联系起来；(2) 用均值场ODEs刻画交互LLM网络的集体行为；(3) 将神经SDEs作为连续时间生成模型，并基于rough-path分析提出Wiener空间cubature方法替代Monte Carlo估计进行训练。

Result: 均值场框架可解释涌现的优先连接(preferential attachment)与持续结构性偏差，包括类型依赖的“玻璃天花板”效应；Wiener空间cubature方法被论证为比Monte Carlo更高效的训练估计手段。

Conclusion: 三类设置分别覆盖概率预测、交互网络中的信息交换与涌现行为、以及路径空间生成建模，形成一个统一的随机动力系统分析框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic dynamical-systems perspective on three classes of generative AI models. First, we formulate large language models (LLMs) as high-dimensional nonlinear autoregressive models with attention-based dependencies. This formulation connects pretraining by conditional maximum likelihood to sequential token generation and provides a probabilistic interpretation of LLM operation. Second, we consider networks of interacting LLMs and characterize their collective behavior through mean-field ordinary differential equations. These dynamics provide a framework for understanding emergent preferential attachment and persistent structural biases, including type-dependent glass-ceiling effects. Third, we consider neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) as continuous-time generative models for stochastic trajectories, including those observed at irregular time intervals. We discuss their training with particular emphasis on Wiener-space cubature methods based on rough-path analysis as an efficient alternative to Monte Carlo estimation. Together, the three settings address probabilistic prediction, information exchange and emergent behavior in interacting networks, and path-space generative modeling.

</details>


### [9] [Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069)
*Jingjuan Huang,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限记忆POMDP下基于非线性神经值函数近似的强化学习，建立了新的有限状态MDP逐点近似误差界、神经critic的有限时间误差界，并结合策略镜像下降得到针对最优POMDP值的有限时间次线性regret，为该设定下首个基于神经函数近似的有限时间次线性regret结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP中RL因对历史的长程依赖而计算困难。在滤波稳定性假设下，可用有限记忆窗口内截断历史构造的superstate MDP近似原POMDP，且价值差距随窗口长度衰减。但已有有限时间收敛分析要么局限于过度简化的线性函数近似，要么依赖与POMDP轨迹不符的i.i.d.采样oracle，严重限制其实际适用性，因此需要研究非线性神经值函数近似下的POMDP强化学习。

Method: i) 建立新的有限状态MDP逐点近似误差界，作为POMDP中RL算法设计与分析的基础；ii) 将TD学习应用于POMDP生成的superstate转移，以superstate MDP的Bellman不动点为目标，证明非线性神经值函数近似下的有限时间critic误差界；iii) 将该神经critic近似误差界与策略镜像下降框架结合，导出有限时间次线性regret。

Result: 得到基于非线性神经值函数近似的有限时间critic误差界，并进一步获得针对最优POMDP值的有限时间次线性regret；据作者所知，这是有限记忆POMDP文献中首个基于神经函数近似的有限时间次线性regret结果，数值实验也验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 在滤波稳定性假设下，通过superstate MDP代理将POMDP的RL问题转化为可分析的形式，神经TD critic与策略镜像下降的结合可在不依赖i.i.d.采样oracle、不局限于线性近似的前提下给出有限时间次线性regret保证，拓展了既有理论结果的实用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) is computationally challenging due to the curse of long-range dependence on history. Fortunately, recent studies have shown that a range of POMDPs that satisfy the filter stability assumption can be well approximated by a superstate Markov Decision Process (MDP) based on a truncated history in a finite-memory window, with a value gap that decays with the window length. However, existing finite-time convergence analyses on such superstate MDP surrogates are either limited to over-simplified linear function approximation or rely on an i.i.d. sampling oracle that is violated by POMDP trajectories, both of which severely limit the applicability of these theoretical results in practice. This motivates us to bridge the gap by investigating RL in POMDPs with nonlinear neural value function approximation. Our main contributions are threefold: i) We first establish a new finite-state MDP point-wise approximation error bound, which lays the foundation for our RL algorithm design and analysis in POMDPs; ii) By applying Temporal-Difference (TD) learning to POMDP-generated superstate transitions and targeting the Bellman fixed point of the superstate MDP, we prove a finite-time critic error bound with nonlinear neural value function approximation; and iii) By combining the neural critic function approximation error bound with the policy mirror descent framework, we obtain a finite-time sublinear regret against the optimal POMDP value. To the best of our knowledge, such a finite-time sublinear regret based on neural function approximation is the first in the literature for finite-memory POMDPs. Our numerical experiments also verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [10] [Shaping & Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071)
*Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal,Matt Feiszli,Satchel Grant,Andrew Lee,Chris Earls,Fatih Dinc,Vasudev Shyam,Usha Bhalla,Leon Bergen,Siddharth Boppana,Ching Fang,Michael Byun,Tristan Gallent,Nicholas Wang,Tal Haklay,Dron Hazra,Neehar Kondapaneni,Connor Watts,Ryan Panwar,Thomas Dooms,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Mechanistic Shaping：在训练中通过内部表示对模型进行"规训"，使其减少特定概念的依赖/表达，同时保持任务性能；并系统研究被规训概念是否仍可被监测，给出监测器层级与三种规避机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐与规训手段多在输出层面约束模型，难以控制模型"依赖哪些概念做决策"（如简历筛选中不使用性别）。作者希望利用可解释性工具直接作用于内部表示，并回答关键疑问：对表示施加训练压力会改变表示本身，被规训的概念是否还能被监测、是否只是"逃离了某个探针"而非真正消失。

Method: 1) 统一框架 Mechanistic Shaping：训练时对模型内部表示施加约束，规范其表达的概念（如无有害内容）与所依赖的概念（如不以候选者性别为决策依据），度量可基于输出或内部表示。2) 在视觉、医学影像、语言三个领域，规模至 32B 参数，以及分类、SFT、DPO、GRPO 多种范式上验证。3) 引入监测器类别的层级（hierarchy of monitor classes），区分"概念逃出某一探针"与"概念真正消失"。4) 通过几何分析刻画三种规避机制：概念漂移到监测器之外、概念从该层被抹除、概念以非线性编码形式留存于高阶统计量中（用乘积码 product-code 构造解释）。5) 检验重拟合线性探针、梯度是否流经原探针等条件下的可监测性。

Result: Mechanistic Shaping 在多领域、多规模、多训练范式下显著减少目标行为，同时保持任务性能。可监测性方面：被规训概念总体上仍大体可监测——重拟合的线性探针在多数设置下能恢复该概念；当梯度不流经原始探针时，原探针保持可靠。三种规避机制可解释全部观测，且每种都存在匹配的监测器予以检出（其他机制可能在别的设置或更大规模出现）。

Conclusion: 可解释性可作为训练工具（interpretability as a tool for training），有效实现对模型概念表达与概念依赖的定向规训；被规训概念大多仍可监测，但"探针失效"不等于"概念消失"，需要按监测器层级严谨评估。作者据此呼吁建立与之匹配的、严谨的"基于可解释性的训练"科学。

Abstract: We propose Mechanistic Shaping, a unified framework for shaping models through their internal representations. Mechanistic Shaping is applied during training so that models follow specifications (rules) about which concepts they express, measured via their outputs or internal representations (e.g., no harmful content), and also which concepts they rely on, i.e., what they use to make their decisions (e.g., not a candidate's gender when screening a résumé). Across multiple domains (vision, medical imaging, language), scales (up to 32B parameters), and training paradigms (classification, SFT, DPO, GRPO), we find that Mechanistic Shaping substantially reduces targeted behaviors while preserving task performance. Since training against a representation can also modify it, we ask whether shaped concepts remain monitorable, a question that depends on the class of monitors considered. We therefore introduce a hierarchy of such classes, which separates a concept that escapes one probe from a concept that is truly gone. We characterize three geometric mechanisms of circumvention: the concept can drift away from the monitor, be erased from the layer, or persist in higher-order statistics as a nonlinear encoding, which we explain through a product-code construction. Although other mechanisms may arise in different settings or at larger scales, these three account for all of our observations, and each can be detected by a matching monitor. In practice, we find that shaped concepts largely remain monitorable: refitted linear probes recover the concept in most settings, and the original probe remains reliable whenever gradients do not flow through it. Taken together, these results point to the potential of using interpretability as a tool for training, and call for a correspondingly rigorous science of interpretability-based training.

</details>


### [11] [Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078)
*Yuval Dagan,Nadav Eisen,Liad Erez,Tomer Koren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了Optimistic Hedge的遗憾界与玩家数量的关系：双人博弈中存在学习率使其获得与时限无关的O(n log n)遗憾，而三人及以上博弈中不存在多项式于博弈规模且与时限无关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Daskalakis等证明固定学习率下Optimistic Hedge的遗憾随时限polylog增长，Ha又证明存在学习率使遗憾与T无关，但该界非构造性、对动作数n的依赖不明确，遗留"能否达到多项式于博弈规模的常数遗憾界"这一开放问题。

Method: 提出用于分析Optimistic Hedge的时间局部近似框架：从Daskalakis等的基于方差的RVU界出发，将时间局部窗口内的损失近似为性质良好的函数，使其全变差仅为累积方差的小比例；同时构造下界博弈实例给出不可行性证明。

Result: 双人博弈中存在学习率使遗憾为O(n log n)且与时限无关；三人博弈中，对任何不超过绝对常数的学习率及足够大时限T，存在每个玩家n个动作的博弈使某玩家遗憾达2^{Ω(n)}；更一般地，对每个m≥3构造出某玩家遗憾为2^{Ω(mn)}的m人博弈，学习率高于2^{-Ω(mn)}时该遗憾甚至随时限线性增长。此外该框架为Ha的O(√m log n log T)界给出显著简化的证明。

Conclusion: Optimistic Hedge能否获得多项式于博弈规模且与时限无关的遗憾界取决于玩家数量：双人情形可行，三人及以上情形不可行，从而完整回答了该开放问题。

Abstract: Optimistic Hedge, a multiplicative weight update with one step of extrapolation, is one of the most natural regret-minimizing dynamics for games, and characterizing its regret has been a longstanding open question. Daskalakis, Fishelson and Golowich proved that if all $m$ players in a finite general-sum game follow Optimistic Hedge with a fixed learning rate, each player's regret is polylogarithmic in the horizon $T$. Subsequently, Ha showed that an appropriate choice of learning rate makes each player's regret bounded independently of $T$. This bound, however, is non-constructive and its dependence on the number of actions $n$ is not explicit, leaving open whether Optimistic Hedge can achieve a constant regret bound which is polynomial in the size of the game.
  We show that the answer hinges on the number of players. In two-player games, there is a learning rate for which Optimistic Hedge guarantees $O(n\log n)$ regret independent of the horizon. In three-player games, however, no such bound is possible: for any learning rate up to an absolute constant and for sufficiently large horizon $T$, there is a game with $n$ actions per player in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(n)}$, and more generally, for every $m\ge3$ we construct $m$-player games in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(mn)}$; for learning rates above $2^{-Ω(mn)}$, that player's regret even grows linearly in $T$.
  Our upper bound is based on a temporal approximation framework for the analysis of Optimistic Hedge. Starting from the variance-based RVU bound of Daskalakis et al., we approximate the losses over time-localized windows by well-behaved functions, whose total variation is a small fraction of their cumulative variance. In $m$-player games, the same framework gives a significantly simplified proof of the recent $O(\sqrt{m}\log n\log T)$ regret bound due to Ha.

</details>


### [12] [Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093)
*Xue Zheng,Aylin Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S³-MoE，一种服务端稀疏专家分配框架，从部分观测估计客户端专属专家相关性并经 exploit–explore 机制精炼分配，并给出相关性估计误差与精确 Top-K 恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备联邦微调 LLM 面临数据异构与资源受限（内存、算力）问题；现有联邦 MoE 的部分专家方法依赖启发式全池观测或系统级负载均衡，缺乏从部分观测恢复客户端相关专家的保证。

Method: 服务器端稀疏专家分配框架：由部分观测估计客户端专属专家相关性，并通过 exploit–explore 机制迭代精炼分配；理论上建立相关性估计误差界与精确 Top-K 恢复保证。实验采用基于 CNN 的 MoE 在 CIFAR-100 上验证。

Result: S³-MoE 能恢复任务相关的客户端–专家偏好，并减少因客户端间专家选择相关性导致的冲突；在每客户端仅加载 5 个专家的情况下，平均最终准确率比 Full-20 FedAvg（每客户端可用全部 20 专家、本地 Top-5 路由）高 1.31 个百分点。

Conclusion: 服务端稀疏专家分配可在显著降低每客户端专家加载量的同时提升异构联邦 MoE 微调的准确率，且具备理论恢复保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly recognized for their remarkable capabilities; federated learning (FL) offers a promising framework for collaboratively fine-tuning LLMs on edge devices while achieving data privacy. However, edge devices are inherently heterogeneous: clients may exhibit diverse data distributions and resource constraints, including limited memory and computation budgets. Recent federated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) approaches address these challenges by using sparse expert activation or partial expert loading. However, existing partial-expert methods rely on heuristic full-pool observations or prioritize system-wide load balance, without guarantees for recovering client-specific relevant experts from partial observations. We propose $S^3$-MoE, a server-side sparse expert assignment framework that estimates client-specific expert relevance from partial observations and refines assignments through an exploit--explore mechanism. We also establish relevance-estimation error and exact Top-$K$ recovery guarantees. Experiments using a CNN-based MoE on CIFAR-100 show that $S^3$-MoE recovers task-dependent client--expert preferences and reduces conflicts caused by correlated expert selection across clients. Compared with Full-20 FedAvg, which gives each client access to all 20 experts for local Top-5 routing, $S^3$-MoE achieves 1.31 percentage points higher mean final accuracy while loading only $5$ experts per client.

</details>


### [13] [A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103)
*Scott Biggs,Bedionita Soro,Saumya Gupta,Bruno Andreis,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构建了按数据混合成分系统变化的GPT-2模型动物园（51M/124M/355M三档，各100个），并据此训练以数据混合配比为条件的Flow Matching权重生成器，在Gram-PCA压缩权重空间中实现对未见混合配比的零样本泛化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间学习从任务特定、视觉模型拓展到LLM时，缺乏精心构建并经过分析的LLM模型种群（population），制约了权重空间生成研究。

Method: 在Web文本、书籍、数学、代码、法语五种数据间系统改变混合配比，训练51M/124M/355M GPT-2种群（各N=100）；用余弦相似度、功能多样性、Leave-One-Mixture-Out建模和方差分解分析种群结构；将混合配比嵌入为条件，在Gram-PCA压缩权重空间中训练Flow Matching模型生成完整预训练权重。

Result: 动物园包含多样性模型，其变异由训练混合配比结构化决定；生成器在留出混合配比上实现零样本泛化，CRPS技能得分良好，生成模型在下游困惑度与通用能力基准分数上与动物园模型接近；并开源全部代码与产物。

Conclusion: 数据混合配比可有效参数化LLM权重分布，条件Flow Matching能在混合配比单纯形上"从无到有"生成性能相当的模型，为权重空间学习与生成在LLM领域的发展提供数据与工具基础。

Abstract: A challenge to Weight Space Learning as it expands from task specific and vision models to Large Language Models (LLMs) is the absence of a well curated and analyzed LLM population. To address this, we train such a model zoo for application to Weight Space Generation by systematically varying the data mixture composition between web text, books, mathematics, coding, and French across populations of 51M, 124M, and 355M parameter GPT-2 Models, each tier with count N = 100. We analyze these populations by cosine similarity, functional diversity, Leave-One-Mixture-Out modeling, and variance decomposition. We show that the zoo contains diverse models whose variation is structured by training mixture composition. We embed the training mixture composition to condition a Flow Matching model of complete pretrained LLM weights in Gram-PCA compressed weight space. By demonstrating zero-shot generalization to held out mixtures, measuring Continuous Ranked Probability Score skill, as well as finding minimal changes in downstream test-set perplexity and general capability benchmark scores, we show that our generator generalizes on the simplex of data mixtures to produce models that perform similarly to those in the zoo ex nihilo. Finally, we produce all source code and artifacts to support the continued expansion of Weight Space Learning and Generation into the LLM domain.

</details>


### [14] [Regulating Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand Operators with Fixed and Adaptive Penalties: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07122)
*Korbinian Kügler,Carolin Schmidt,Florian Dandl,Filipe Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究对自动驾驶出行即服务（AMoD）运营商的惩罚式监管：将静态固定罚金建模为MDP，将动态区域差异化罚金建模为部分可观测随机博弈，用图基多智能体强化学习求解；曼哈顿数据实验显示固定罚金把遭遇过高价格的乘客比例从22%降至1%，动态监管相比等均值固定罚金将监管者收益提升最多15.6%，但车队高利用率的高峰期运力缺口仍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利润最大化的AMoD运营商可能通过过高价格攫取消费者剩余，并把车辆调离低利润区域导致服务不足。作者希望研究基于罚金的监管能否在多模式出行系统中抑制动态溢价（surge pricing）并改善区域服务可用性，并比较静态与动态（闭环、区域差异化）监管的效果及适用边界。

Method: 将监管刻画为作用于“学习型运营商”的外层控制回路。静态监管（固定罚金）表述为运营商联合控制定价与调度的马尔可夫决策过程；动态监管表述为部分可观测随机博弈，监管者周期性在闭环中调整区域特定罚金。运营商与监管者策略均用基于图的多智能体强化学习学习。实验使用曼哈顿真实数据。

Result: 固定surge定价罚金使遭遇过高价格的出行者比例从22%降至1%。在区域服务可用性上，动态监管相比同等平均强度的固定罚金使监管者奖励提升最多15.6%。服务可用性改善在存在闲置车队容量时最强；在车队高度利用的高峰期服务缺口持续存在。学到的监管策略会在车队容量限制履约能力的区域降低罚金。

Conclusion: 惩罚式监管可有效抑制过高定价，闭环动态、区域差异化罚金优于等强度静态罚金，但受车队容量约束；研究揭示了监管目标、运营商盈利与出行者效用之间的权衡，并表明监管策略应随容量约束自适应调整。

Abstract: Profit-maximizing Autonomous Mobility-on-Demand (AMoD) operators may exploit consumer surplus through excessive prices and underserve less profitable regions by relocating vehicles away from them. We study how penalty-based regulation of an AMoD operator in a multimodal mobility system can limit surge pricing and improve regional service availability. We model regulation as an outer control loop that acts on a learning-based operator. Static regulation with fixed penalties is formulated as a Markov Decision Process for an operator that jointly controls pricing and rebalancing, and dynamic regulation as a Partially Observable Stochastic Game in which a regulator periodically adjusts region-specific penalties in closed loop. Operator and regulator policies are learned with graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning. In experiments with real-world Manhattan data, fixed surge pricing penalties reduce the share of travelers facing excessive prices from 22% to 1%. For regional service availability, dynamic regulation improves the regulator's reward by up to 15.6% over fixed penalties of equal mean intensity. The gains in service availability are strongest when spare fleet capacity exists, whereas service deficits persist in peak periods with highly utilized fleets. The learned regulator policy responds by reducing penalties where limited fleet capacity restricts compliance. The results identify trade-offs between regulatory targets, operator profitability, and traveler utility.

</details>


### [15] [Machine Learning for Live-Birth Prediction After IVF/ICSI From Structured Clinical Data: A Systematic Review, Meta-Analysis, and Critical Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07124)
*Abdul Raheem Shahzad,Muhammad Zaheer Khan,Umar Ajaib,Asad Ur Rehman,Juan Deng,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统评价了IVF/ICSI后活产预测的机器学习模型，内部验证合并AUC为0.814，外部验证降至0.756；48项研究中47项存在高或不明确偏倚风险，仅4项评估校准斜率，无模型有临床影响证据，证据确定性低至极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测IVF/ICSI后活产的机器学习模型报告的性能从接近随机到接近完美，差异悬殊，需系统梳理其真实泛化能力、偏倚来源与报告规范性。

Method: 按PRISMA 2020开展系统综述，检索OpenAlex、Scopus和Web of Science至2026年10月22日；用PROBAST+AI与TRIPOD+AI评估偏倚风险与报告质量；在logit尺度上用随机效应模型合并判别力（AUC）；定义决定模型输出含义的四个要素（决策点、终点、分析单位、数据来源），并按模型家族、决策点与验证设计对48项纳入研究进行映射；采用meta回归识别调节因素。

Result: 仅内部验证的模型合并AUC为0.814；在后续时段或另一中心验证的模型为0.756（95%预测区间0.546–0.889）；非高偏倚风险研究为0.764，两差异均不显著，异质性极高。未说明决策点的模型合并AUC达0.910，是meta回归中唯一显著调节因素。与同一数据上拟合的逻辑回归相比，最佳机器学习模型仅增益0.056 AUC（95% CI 0.017–0.090）。48项研究中47项为高或不明确偏倚风险，仅4项评估校准斜率，无模型具备临床影响证据。

Conclusion: 合并证据的确定性为低至极低；经外部验证后仍存在的性能增益来自将预测对齐到具体决策点与本地校准，而非更深的网络架构。作者提供了TRIPOD+AI映射清单、基准测试规范与研究议程。

Abstract: Machine-learning models predicting live birth after in vitro fertilisation (IVF) or intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) from routine clinical data report performance from near chance to near perfect. We systematically reviewed these models under PRISMA 2020, searching OpenAlex, Scopus and Web of Science to 22 October 2026, appraised them with PROBAST+AI and TRIPOD+AI, and pooled their discrimination with random-effects meta-analysis on the logit scale. We define what fixes the meaning of a model's output (decision point, endpoint, unit of analysis, data source) and map the 48 included studies by model family, decision point and validation design. Models evaluated only internally pooled to an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.814, against 0.756 (95% prediction interval 0.546-0.889) for models evaluated on a later period or another centre, and 0.764 for studies not at high risk of bias; neither difference was significant, and heterogeneity was extreme. Models whose decision point was not stated pooled to 0.910, the only significant moderator in meta-regression. Against logistic regression fitted to the same data, the best machine-learning model gained a pooled 0.056 AUC (95% CI 0.017-0.090). Of 48 studies, 47 were at high or unclear risk of bias, only four assessed the calibration slope, and no model has evidence of clinical impact. The certainty of the pooled evidence is low to very low; the gains that survive validation came from staging predictions to decision points and local calibration, not deeper architectures. We provide a TRIPOD+AI-mapped checklist, a benchmark specification and a research agenda.

</details>


### [16] [Data-Driven Modeling of Molten Chloride Corrosion Using Static Test Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07125)
*Md Mahmudul Hasan Pathik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 汇编291组文献熔盐氯化物腐蚀实验数据，构建可解释机器学习框架，提出"氯化盐制备指数(CSPI)"定量刻画盐处理/气氛/杂质状态，树集成模型交叉验证R²=0.85、MAE=0.41 mg cm⁻² day⁻¹，并给出校准良好的保形预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔盐氯化物腐蚀研究已持续数十年，但跨合金、跨盐体系、跨实验条件的可靠腐蚀速率预测仍很困难；文献中实验条件（盐纯化方式、气氛控制、杂质状态）多以定性或格式不一的方式报告，难以直接作为模型输入，导致历史数据难以被有效复用。

Method: 将文献中291组静态熔盐氯化物腐蚀实验整理为结构化数据集；提出氯化盐制备指数（CSPI），把盐处理方法、气氛控制与暴露前杂质状况映射到统一数值尺度；采用混合树基集成模型进行腐蚀速率回归；结合全局敏感性与SHAP分析解释特征贡献，并用保形预测（conformal prediction）给出不确定性区间。

Result: 混合树集成模型表现最稳定，交叉验证R²=0.85、MAE=0.41 mg cm⁻² day⁻¹；分级CSPI比"纯化/未纯化"二分标签保留更多信息，并成为模型的主导描述符；暴露时间、温度与合金成分是重要贡献特征，其趋势与已知熔盐氯化物腐蚀机理一致；保形预测区间校准良好。

Conclusion: 显式表征实验语境时，历史腐蚀数据可支撑定量、可解释且具不确定性量化的预测；同时指出需更规范的实验报告与针对性补充实验，以进一步提升熔盐腐蚀模型能力。

Abstract: Molten chloride corrosion has been studied for decades, yet reliable prediction across different alloys, salts, and test conditions remains difficult. In this work, 291 static molten chloride corrosion experiments reported in the literature were compiled into a structured dataset and used to develop an interpretable machine-learning framework for corrosion-rate predictions. To better represent the differences in salt condition across studies, a Chloride Salt Preparation Index (CSPI) was introduced to bring the reported salt-treatment method, atmosphere control, and pre-exposure impurity condition on a common numerical scale. A blended tree-based ensemble provided the most consistent predictive performance, achieving a cross-validated \(R^2\) of 0.85 and an MAE of 0.41 mg cm\(^{-2}\) day\(^{-1}\). The graded CSPI retained more useful information than a simple purified/unpurified classification and emerged as the dominant model descriptor. Global sensitivity and SHAP analyses further identified exposure time, temperature, and alloy chemistry as important contributors to the model predictions, with several observed trends consistent with the established molten-chloride corrosion behavior. The conformal prediction intervals were well calibrated, providing an estimate of prediction uncertainty in addition to the point prediction. Overall, the framework demonstrates that historical corrosion data can support quantitative, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware prediction when experimental context is represented explicitly, while also highlighting where more consistent reporting and targeted experiments are needed to improve future molten-salt corrosion models.

</details>


### [17] [Scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification for Cancer Histopathology: Calibrated Uncertainty Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07127)
*Yunru Zheng,Wun Ting Chan,Sampras Dsouza,Zhenyu Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可扩展的贝叶斯高斯过程分类框架，在冻结的病理基础模型嵌入上结合RPCholesky低秩分解、非中心参数化HMC采样与"枢轴即诱导点"预测方案，在约30万训练点上实现HMC后验采样，跨医院分布偏移下显著改善校准与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理诊断模型临床部署需要可靠的不确定性量化，但神经网络与变分高斯过程（SVGP）易产生过度自信预测，尤其在医院间迁移导致的分布偏移下；同时MCMC类贝叶斯方法此前难以扩展到大规模数据。

Method: 在冻结的Phikon病理基础模型嵌入上构建GPC：用加速的随机枢轴Cholesky（RPCholesky）做低秩核分解，在低秩潜在表示上用非中心参数化的HMC进行后验采样，并以枢轴点作为诱导点进行预测，使N≈300k规模下的HMC后验采样可行。

Result: 在PCam与CAMELYON17-WILDS两个乳腺癌转移检测基准上，相较SVGP与神经网络基线，校准误差（ECE）最多降低88%，假阴性率最多降低90%，且增益在分布偏移场景下最大。

Conclusion: 基于HMC的后验采样可为跨站点病理分类提供更可信的不确定性估计，在大规模数据上兼具可扩展性与校准优势。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty quantification is essential for clinical deployment of cancer diagnosis models, yet neural networks and variational Gaussian process methods can produce overconfident predictions, particularly under the distribution shifts that arise when models trained at one hospital are applied at another. We present a scalable Bayesian Gaussian Process Classification (GPC) framework that operates on frozen pathology foundation model embeddings (Phikon) and combines accelerated Randomly Pivoted Cholesky (RPCholesky) for low-rank kernel factorization, Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) with non-centered parameterization for posterior sampling, and a pivots-as-inducing-points scheme for prediction. This makes HMC posterior sampling in the low-rank latent representation tractable at N approximately 300,000 training points, enabling HC-based low-rank GPC on substantially larger datasets than prior MCMC-based demonstrations. We evaluate on two histopathology benchmarks for breast cancer metastasis detection: PCam and CAMELYON17-WILDS, where the latter tests calibration under hospital-level distribution shift. Compared to stochastic variational GP and neural network baselines, our method improves calibration and predictive performance - reducing expected calibration error by up to 88% and false negative rate by up to 90% - with the largest gains appearing under distribution shift, suggesting that HMC-based posterior sampling provides more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for cross-site pathology classification.

</details>


### [18] [Teaching Agents the "Principle", Not the "Reward"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07130)
*Shan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"规范性策略学习"(PPL)，用"行为原则"(如保持平衡、积累能量、平稳落地)通过关键量与参考值直接逐步指示智能体是否遵循原则及程度，使智能体无需任何奖励信号即可学到良好、可泛化的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在复杂任务中即使奖励精心设计、样本充足仍难以学到可泛化策略。作者将其归因于"奖励解码负担"：设计者把行为知识编码为奖励与惩罚，智能体须从冗长、含噪的标量序列中反推这些知识。

Method: 提出PPL，直接向智能体传授更高层的任务知识（行为原则），借助恰当的"关键量"与"参考量"，在每一步直接指示智能体是否遵循原则以及遵循程度，从而绕开奖励解码。

Result: 仅用简单启发式原则教学、不设任何奖励，PPL 智能体以远更少的样本超越基于奖励的方法；策略可直接迁移到更难设置并保持良好表现，展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: PPL 提供了新范式：即使系统动力学难以精确建模，仅凭对期望行为的粗略知识即足以让智能体学到良好、可泛化的策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved strong performance in many tasks by letting agents learn from nothing more than a reward signal. However, in more complex tasks, agents still struggle to learn good, generalizable policies, even with carefully designed rewards and abundant samples. We argue that this difficulty stems from the reward decoding burden: designers encode their knowledge of the desired behavior into rewards and penalties, and agents must infer this knowledge from long sequences of noisy scalars. To address this burden, we propose prescriptive policy learning (PPL), which directly teaches agents higher-level knowledge about the tasks, termed principles of behavior, such as how to maintain balance, how to accumulate energy, and how to land smoothly. Through appropriately selected key quantities and references, PPL can directly indicate at every step whether the agent follows the principles and to what extent. Our experiments show that agents can learn good, generalizable policies without any reward. Taught only simple heuristic principles, PPL agents outperform reward-based methods with far fewer samples. Their policies also transfer directly to harder settings and keep performing well, demonstrating their strong generalization ability. In summary, PPL provides a new approach in which rough knowledge of the desired behavior is enough for agents to learn good, generalizable policies, even when the system dynamics cannot be accurately modeled.

</details>


### [19] [Depth or Description? When a One-Step Latent Score Replaces Lookahead in Proactive Information Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07155)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 主动智能体在行动前的提问决策中，深度推理的必要性常取决于隐因子的描述方式；在合适的潜在空间上做一步打分即可替代大部分前瞻搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确前瞻需要枚举问题组合，代价高且依赖拟合模型；一步打分便宜但可能遗漏答案间的交互作用。作者希望找到一种既廉价又能捕捉必要深度的替代方案。

Method: 对比精确前瞻价值与一步打分，并考察打分所依据的表示：标签层面的一步打分 vs. 合适潜在隐因子上的一步打分；同时检验潜在表示与精确值的一致性，以及不同语言模型间学到的概率可迁移性。

Result: 基于标签的一步打分对答案交互是盲的；基于合适隐因子的打分则能捕捉交互，且在隐因子编码了决策时，能以一小部分搜索代价替代大部分深度。同一问题换一种描述可使打分失效而精确值不变。从一个语言模型答案学到的概率不可迁移到另一模型，除非规划同时考虑两者；从token读出的信息仅价值几个标签。

Conclusion: 应保持隐因子小巧并用精确值校验；打分表示的选择决定一步近似能否替代深度；跨模型概率需联合规划；token级信号增益有限。

Abstract: A proactive agent must decide what to ask before it acts. Exact lookahead values combinations of questions but is expensive and leans on a fitted model; a one-step score is cheap but can miss them. Our main finding: the need for depth often lies in how the hidden cause is described. A one-step score on the label can be blind to an interaction among answers. A score on a suitable latent cause is not, and where that latent encodes the decision it replaces most of depth at a fraction of the search. Described differently, the same problem can defeat the score while exact values do not move, so keep the latent small and check it against exact values. Probabilities learned from one language model's answers do not carry to another unless planning allows for both; those read from tokens are worth only a few labels.

</details>


### [20] [A Survey of Nonlinear Filtering Methods for Data Assimilation and Recent Advances in Generative AI-Enabled Score Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07160)
*Cath Pham,Kadesha Reynolds*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理集合分数滤波（EnSF）的数学基础，将其确立为非高斯、高维滤波的最先进方法，并考察了14项扩展工作，其中12项保留训练无关的蒙特卡洛分数估计核心，仅2项为实现行星尺度部署而偏离该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据同化需在非线性、混沌且含不确定性系统中求解最优滤波问题（给定观测序列估计隐状态的条件分布）。经典方法存在三类瓶颈：卡尔曼类方法假设高斯后验且非线性观测算子下产生偏差；粒子类方法在高维下因维数灾难而崩塌；核方法无法在维度增长时低成本维持密度近似。EnSF通过以分数函数编码滤波密度、并用训练无关的蒙特卡洛估计器近似该分数，同时规避这三点限制。

Method: 综述性工作：追溯EnSF的数学基础（分数匹配/扩散式密度编码、训练无关的蒙特卡洛分数估计），总结其在非线性观测下的滤波流程，并对14项扩展进行归类分析，按是否保留核心估计器划分层次。

Result: 论证EnSF在高维混沌系统中可稳定滤波，突破高斯假设、维数灾难与密度近似成本三重限制；14项扩展中12项维持训练无关蒙特卡洛估计器为核心，2项为行星尺度部署而改用其他机制。

Conclusion: EnSF被定位为非高斯、高维滤波的最先进范式，其扩展体系以训练无关的蒙特卡洛估计器为主线，兼具理论一致性与工程可扩展性；未来方向在于行星尺度部署与核心估计器的取舍平衡。

Abstract: Many real-world applications, such as numerical weather prediction or wildfire tracking, involve modeling highly nonlinear dynamical systems that are chaotic and influenced by various sources of uncertainty. To improve model prediction accuracy and reduce such uncertainties, data assimilation (DA) combines the dynamical model with partial, noisy observations to estimate the evolution of the system's state. Doing so depends on solving the optimal filtering problem: computing the conditional distribution of a hidden state given a sequence of observations. Classical approaches fail along three distinct axes: Kalman-based methods assume Gaussian posteriors and produce biased estimates under nonlinear observation operators; particle-based methods represent distributions with weighted samples and collapse in high dimensions due to the curse of dimensionality; and kernel-based methods move beyond discrete particle representations but cannot maintain their density approximation affordably as dimension grows. The Ensemble Score Filter (EnSF) avoids all three constraints by encoding the filtering density in a score function and approximating that score with a training-free Monte Carlo estimator, enabling stable filtering in high-dimensional, chaotic systems. This survey traces EnSF's mathematical foundations, establishes it as the state of the art for non-Gaussian, high-dimensional filtering, and examines fourteen extensions that expand its operational reach. Twelve of the fourteen extensions preserve this training-free Monte Carlo estimator at the core of the update, with only two departing from it to achieve planetary-scale deployment.

</details>


### [21] [Structural Conditional Independence Test via Neural Quantile Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07165)
*Khoa Nguyen,Bao Duong,Thin Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QRCIT——基于分位数形变的全局条件独立性检验，比较P(X|Z)与P(X|Y,Z)，并给出可检视的形变场；同时构建SDB基准评估结构化依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统条件独立性检验在依赖弱、局部化、或仅通过分布变化（而非全局均值偏移）体现时难以检测，且仅输出标量统计量或p值，无法刻画条件分布"何处"发生变化。

Method: 用共享的轻量残差分位数流模型先学习基线条件分位律P(X|Z)，再学习由Y诱导的附加形变，得到全局预测增益统计量；采用条件随机化进行校准；并输出随上下文与分位水平变化的可检视形变场。另提出结构依赖基准SDB，涵盖上下文局部、尾部专属、符号反转、移动脊、均值中性等模式。

Result: 在合成PNL与SDB基准上，QRCIT提升对结构化备择的检测力，并在多设置下评估了经验Type-I错误；较高分位分辨率在中等样本下提升合并SDB功效；限定于已知受影响区域的诊断可提升局部依赖检测；在Sachs、METABRIC乳腺癌与CCLE/PLX4720上性能具竞争力，且形变模式可解释。

Conclusion: 分位数分辨的条件律形变可作为检验与诊断结构化条件依赖的实用框架。

Abstract: Conditional independence testing is difficult when dependence is weak, localized, or expressed through distributional changes rather than a global mean shift. Existing tests usually return a scalar statistic or p-value, which summarizes evidence against independence but does not describe where the conditional law changes. We propose QRCIT, a quantile-resolution conditional independence test that compares P(X|Z) and P(X|Y,Z) through a quantile deformation learned by a shared model. A lightweight residual quantile-flow model first learns the baseline conditional quantile law and then learns the additional deformation induced by Y, yielding a global predictive-gain statistic with conditional-randomization calibration and an inspectable deformation field over context and quantile level. We also introduce the Structural Dependency Benchmark (SDB), which evaluates dependence structures motivated by real applications, such as context-local, tail-only, sign-reversing, moving-ridge, and mean-neutral patterns. Across synthetic post-nonlinear (PNL) and SDB benchmarks, QRCIT improves detection of structured alternatives, with empirical Type-I behavior evaluated across settings. Higher quantile resolution improves merged-SDB power at moderate sample sizes, while diagnostics restricted to known affected regions improve detection of localized dependence. On Sachs, METABRIC breast cancer, and CCLE/PLX4720, QRCIT provides competitive real-data performance and interpretable deformation patterns. These results support quantile-resolved conditional-law deformation as a practical framework for testing and diagnosing structured conditional dependence.

</details>


### [22] [Meta-Frieren: Beyond Single Level Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07169)
*Vahan Arsenyan,Arnak Dalalyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段在线优化框架 Meta-Frieren：元学习者先选“智能体-环境”对，再由所选智能体与环境交互获取奖励；给出 UCB 式元层选择准则，并以 GP-UCB 与 Feel-Good Thompson Sampling（FGTS）分别实例化，首次给出 FGTS 的高概率遗憾界，实验验证理论并成功用于多模型类的超参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段在线优化（先选智能体-环境对、后交互得奖励）在超参数优化等场景中普遍存在，但已有工作往往只覆盖特定设置、缺乏统一分析，且 FGTS 缺乏高概率遗憾保证。

Method: 构建通用元层框架 Meta-Frieren，推导出简单的 UCB 型元层选择准则，把已有保证作为特例纳入并推广到更广设置；在贝叶斯与频率学派假设下用 GP-UCB 实例化；推导 FGTS 的高概率遗憾界并推广到有限与参数化函数类，再集成进 Meta-Frieren。

Result: 元层 UCB 准则可恢复并推广先前的理论保证；据作者所知给出了首个 FGTS 高概率遗憾界，且在有限与参数化函数类下给出具体化结果；实验支持理论结论，并显示 Meta-Frieren 可用于跨多模型类的超参数优化。

Conclusion: Meta-Frieren 为两阶段在线优化提供了统一且通用的分析框架，兼具理论普适性与实践可用性，尤其拓展了 FGTS 的高概率理论并验证于超参数优化任务。

Abstract: We study a two-stage online optimization problem: at each round, a meta-learner first selects an agent-environment pair, then the chosen agent interacts with the environment, and receives a reward. We introduce Meta-Frieren, a general framework for maximizing cumulative reward in this setting. Our analysis yields a simple UCB-style criterion for meta-level selection and recovers prior guarantees as special cases while extending them to broader settings. We instantiate Meta-Frieren with GP-UCB under Bayesian and frequentist assumptions. We also derive, to our knowledge, the first high-probability regret bound for Feel-Good Thompson Sampling, specialize it to finite and parametric function classes, and integrate it into Meta-Frieren. Experiments support our theoretical results and demonstrate that Meta-Frieren can be successfully applied to hyperparameter optimization across multiple model classes.

</details>


### [23] [Backbone-Informed Adaptation after EEG Pretraining: A Capacity-Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07192)
*Mingyang Jiang,Yamin Li,Fan Ma,Daniel Moyer,Hua Xu,Catie Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在参数量近似匹配、预训练编码器固定的条件下，比较"骨干知情的原生适配器"与逐token MLP对照，发现前者无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型的表征融合了通道与时间信息，因此研究者常设计建模同类关系的适配器；但这种设计与其它候选适配器相比是否更优尚不清楚，且公平比较必须考虑可训练参数量。

Method: 提出原生（backbone-informed）适配器与逐token多层感知机对照进行对比，通过近似匹配可训练参数量、冻结预训练编码器以保证公平；在两个EEG基础模型上，覆盖情绪识别、睡眠分期与EEG事件分类任务。

Result: 原生适配器无一致优势：对其中一个骨干，两种设计的差异小于跨次运行的波动；对另一骨干，逐token对照在情绪识别与事件分类上更优；睡眠分期上原生适配器的优势在同时训练处理EEG片段序列的组件后减弱。

Conclusion: 两种适配器的相对表现取决于骨干、任务与下游训练协议；应把骨干知情适配器与容量匹配的替代方案进行对照评估。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models learn representations that combine information across channels and time. This structure motivates adapters that model the same relationships during downstream learning. However, it is not clear whether such a design outperforms other candidate adapters. Moreover, in order to arrive at a fair comparison between candidate adapters, we also need to consider their trainable parameters. Here, we ask whether a backbone-informed (native) adapter makes better use of a given capacity by comparing it against a token-wise multilayer perceptron control that transforms each feature vector independently. We approximately match their trainable parameter counts and keep the pretrained encoder fixed. Across two EEG foundation models and tasks covering emotion recognition, sleep staging, and EEG event classification, the native adapter shows no consistent advantage. For one backbone, differences between the designs are small relative to variation across runs. For the other, the token-wise control performs better on emotion recognition and EEG event classification. On sleep staging, the native adapter's advantage over the token-wise control is reduced when we also train the component that processes sequences of EEG segments. The relative performance of the two adapters depends on the backbone, task, and downstream training protocol. These findings motivate evaluating backbone-informed adapters against capacity-matched alternatives.

</details>


### [24] [TrustMix: Graceful Degradation for Pretrained Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07206)
*Juliusz Ziomek,Maike Osborne,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrustMix是一种免重训的事后(post-hoc)方法，将预训练模型的预测分布与用户指定的回退分布 p0(y|x) 按OOD偏离统计量进行混合，使模型外推行为显式化、可控，并在分布偏移下实现平滑退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络对训练域之外的预测必然依赖外推假设，但这些假设通常只隐含在架构中，不可见也不可控；作者希望用轻量后处理手段把这种外推行为变得显式且可由用户调节。

Method: TrustMix不改动基模型、无需重训：以OOD偏离统计量为门控信号，将预训练模型的预测分布与回退分布 p0(y|x) 做凸混合，OOD信号增大时预测分布向 p0 收敛；p0 可编码领域知识，也可采用领域无关的平稳性假设；方法同时适用于回归与分类。

Result: 在表格、图像（CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet）与自然语言处理任务的多种合成及真实分布偏移上评估，覆盖CNN与Transformer等架构：保持分布内竞争力，同时改善偏移下的预测不确定性——回归预测似然提升、分类校准改善；并可作为贝叶斯优化的代理模型提供有用的预测分布。

Conclusion: TrustMix以极低代价为预训练模型提供显式、可控、可退化的外推机制，兼顾分布内性能与分布外不确定性质量，并能支撑下游决策任务。

Abstract: Predicting beyond the training domain necessarily requires assumptions about how the ground truth extrapolates, yet in most neural networks these assumptions are made only implicit through the architecture. We propose TrustMix, a lightweight post-hoc method that makes a pretrained model's extrapolative behaviour explicit and controllable. TrustMix combines the predictive distribution produced by a pretrained model with a user-specified fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$, such that as the out-of-distribution (OOD) deviation statistic grows, we collapse towards $p_0(y|x)$. TrustMix provides graceful degradation: the predictive distribution converges toward the fallback distribution as the OOD signal grows. The fallback distribution $p_0(y|x)$ can reflect domain knowledge, if available, or rely on a domain-agnostic stationarity assumption. TrustMix does not require retraining of the base model and can be applied to both regression and classification problems. We evaluated TrustMix on several synthetic and real-world distribution shifts, including tabular, image, and natural language processing tasks. We tested TrustMix on datasets such as CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet and on numerous architectures including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Transformers. TrustMix remains competitive in-distribution while improving predictive uncertainty under distribution shift, with gains in predictive likelihood for regression and calibration for classification. Finally, we showed that TrustMix can provide useful predictive distributions for decision-making applications by utilizing it as a surrogate for Bayesian Optimisation.

</details>


### [25] [Structured Machine Theory of Mind from Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07208)
*Nigel F. Doering,Rahath Malladi,Arshia Sangwan,M Tanjid Hasan Tonmoy,Jacob Doan,David Danks,Tauhidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出结构化机器心智理论框架SMToM，在BDI因果结构下从轨迹中显式地、独立监督地推断信念与欲望表示，并以目标头作为瓶颈实现可编辑的心智状态与反事实干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模轨迹数据训练的预测模型只追求统计精度，未表示因果驱动行为的心智状态，因而只能预测而无法进行原则性干预——无法回答"若智能体的信念或偏好不同，行为会如何变化"。

Method: SMToM在BDI因果结构下对信念与欲望通道施加独立监督；核心架构为目标头（goal head），仅接收预测的心智状态通道与当前轨迹嵌入，对信念/欲望的反事实干预即为直接操作。在真值心智状态可构造已知的受控行人导航域上实例化模型（BDIBottleneck），并开展归因准确率、局部通道干预与轨迹级反事实诊断评估。

Result: BDIBottleneck优于仅用轨迹与无监督上下文神经基线；与匹配的辅助监督上下文基线相当，但额外提供后者所缺的可编辑心智状态接口。欲望反事实实验：替换推断偏好为其他活动类型可使预测目的地一致地转向相关位置；信念反事实实验：在信念状态中标记某地点不可用会降低其作为目的地的预测概率，但影响方式较复杂。

Conclusion: 在受控导航设定下，显式BDI结构监督可作为纵向轨迹数据可解释与反事实分析的可行基础。

Abstract: Predictive models of human behavior trained on large-scale trajectory data optimize for statistical accuracy without representing the mental states that causally generate behavior. Such models support prediction but not principled intervention: they cannot answer how an agent's behavior would change if its beliefs or preferences were different. We introduce Structured Machine Theory of Mind (SMToM), a framework that attributes explicit, independently supervised belief and desire representations from observed trajectories within a Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) causal structure. The central architectural element is a goal head that consumes only the predicted mental-state channels and a current-trajectory embedding; counterfactual intervention on beliefs and desires is a direct operation. We instantiate SMToM on a controlled pedestrian navigation domain where ground-truth mental states are known by construction, enabling evaluation of attribution accuracy, local channel interventions, and trajectory-level counterfactual diagnostics. The resulting model, BDIBottleneck, improves over trajectory-only and unsupervised-context neural baselines. It is also competitive with a matched auxiliary-supervised context baseline while providing an editable mental-state interface that the unbottlenecked baseline lacks. Counterfactual experiments on desires confirm that substituting an agent's inferred preferences with a different activity type coherently shifts predicted destinations toward relevant locations. Counterfactual experiments on beliefs confirm that marking a location as unavailable in the agent's belief state reduces its predicted probability as a destination, though in a complex way. These results demonstrate, in a controlled navigation setting, that explicit BDI-structured supervision is a viable foundation for interpretable and counterfactual analysis of longitudinal trajectory data.

</details>


### [26] [Canonical Correlation Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07212)
*Andrea Cavallo,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs)，将视图内协方差与跨视图协方差作为两类不同的关系性归纳偏置，用于双视图数据的下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格、图等关系性归纳偏置可减少参数并提升泛化，但需预先已知结构。单视图场景下协方差矩阵可作为有原则的替代（VNNs 已证明其基于 PCA、对有限样本误差稳定且参数高效），但该框架无法处理多视图数据——即同一现象由多视图观测、各有视图内协方差并有跨视图协方差相连的情形。

Method: CANEs 将视图内协方差与跨视图协方差分别作为两种关系性偏置构造网络架构；通过谱分析建立其与典型相关分析（CCA）的类比，但与 CCA 以求解共享视图表示为目标不同，CANEs 直接训练以求解下游任务。

Result: 理论上给出有限样本协方差扰动下的输出误差界：误差依赖于所学滤波器的可控 Lipschitz 常数，而非 CCA 稳定性所依赖的奇异值间隔，从而保证更好的鲁棒性。在脑影像与运动传感的七个数据集上，CANEs 匹配或优于单视图、双视图及基于 CCA 的基线，且常使用更少参数；在由更少样本重新估计协方差时性能仍保持。

Conclusion: 以协方差矩阵（视图内/跨视图）作为归纳偏置、并直接面向下游任务训练的 CANEs，是单视图 VNN 框架向多视图场景的自然且理论上可解释的推广，兼具参数效率与对协方差估计误差的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Relational inductive biases such as grids or graphs allow neural networks with fewer trainable parameters and improved generalization, but require a known structure beforehand. When no structure is available, the covariance matrix provides a principled substitute, and coVariance Neural Networks (VNNs) have shown that using it yields models that are theoretically grounded in Principal Component Analysis, stable to finite-sample estimation errors and parameter-efficient. This framework, however, is limited to single-view data, whereas many applications observe the same phenomenon through multiple views, each with its own in-view covariance and a cross-view covariance linking them. To incorporate these matrices as inductive biases in a principled way, we introduce CAnonical correlation NEural networks (CANEs), architectures that use in-view and cross-view covariances as distinct relational biases to solve downstream tasks. Via spectral analysis, CANEs draw analogies with Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA), a classical technique to project two-view data onto a correlated subspace. However, while CCA's objective is to compute shared view representations, CANEs are trained to solve a downstream task directly. We bound their output error under finite-sample covariance perturbations and show that it depends on controllable Lipschitz constants of the learned filters in place of the singular-value gap that governs the stability of CCA, thus guaranteeing better robustness. Experiments on seven datasets from brain imaging and motion sensing show that CANEs match or outperform single-view, two-view and CCA-based baselines, often with fewer parameters, and retain their performance when covariances are re-estimated from fewer samples.

</details>


### [27] [Fractional physics-informed neural networks to model social media virality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07215)
*Alex Koong,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分数阶物理信息神经网络（fPINN）结合符号回归，同时学习社媒参与度的时间记忆与非线性增长动态，并用全白化残差解决噪声下的分数阶估计偏差，分段建模显著提升重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社媒病毒式传播模型要么预设参与度增长的函数形式，要么假定过去活动影响未来互动的固定机制，缺乏对时间记忆与非线性增长的联合、可解释学习。

Method: 构建分数阶物理信息神经网络，联合学习时间记忆（分数阶阶数）与反应增长律，并用符号回归将学到的动力学转化为可解释方程；针对观测噪声，提出将观测噪声通过分数算子传播的全白化残差以替代标准逐点物理残差；在348条X平台帖子的24小时窗口上，将其划分为四个教师强制分段进行分段参数估计。

Result: 24小时分辨率的合成实验表明，干净数据下分数阶阶数与反应律可被联合恢复；噪声下标准逐点残差因尺度伪影使阶数趋向下界，全白化残差可纠正该失效。真实数据中单窗口模型估计出强记忆依赖但样本外重建不佳；分段模型各段分数阶从0.23升至0.99，64%的留出轨迹MSE≤0.01，优于九种经典基线中最强者的44%。

Conclusion: 分数阶物理信息方程发现可揭示可解释的参与度动力学，但需重视噪声感知估计与时间分段建模。

Abstract: Existing models of social media virality either prescribe the functional form of engagement growth or assume a fixed mechanism by which past activity influences future engagement. We present a fractional physics-informed neural network that learns both the temporal memory and nonlinear growth dynamics of social media engagement, using symbolic regression to translate the learned dynamics into interpretable equations. Synthetic experiments at the deployment resolution of 24 hourly observations demonstrate that the fractional order and reaction law can be jointly recovered from clean data. Under observation noise, however, the standard pointwise physics residual drives the fractional order toward its lower bound through a scale artifact. We address this failure using a fully whitened residual that propagates observation noise through the fractional operator. Applying the framework to 348 posts from X (formerly Twitter), we find that a single-window model estimates strongly memory-dependent dynamics but fails to accurately reconstruct held-out trajectories. Partitioning the 24-hour window into four teacher-forced segments substantially improves performance, yielding segment-specific fractional orders that increase from 0.23 to 0.99 and interpretable growth laws for each phase. The segmented model reconstructs 64% of held-out trajectories within a mean squared error of 0.01, compared with 44% for the strongest of nine classical baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of fractional physics-informed equation discovery to uncover interpretable engagement dynamics while highlighting the importance of noise-aware estimation and temporal segmentation.

</details>


### [28] [Compositional Unalignment: Auditing Safety in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07216)
*Hong kyu Lee,Kai Shu,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CMaG——一种免训练的反对齐方法，通过单次反向传播提取方向使模型从"拒绝组合性危害"单调转向"合规"，揭示组合性安全在行为上表现强、但内部鲁棒性脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法仅在组合性安全基准上依赖LLM评判器得分，只能度量"可观测行为"，无法反映安全机制被整合进模型的鲁棒程度；且细小SFT即可达到与多阶段RL流水线相当的分值，说明高分本身不能证明鲁棒性，因此需要一种审计对齐鲁棒性的互补手段。

Method: 以"反对齐"作为审计工具：先对比最小化SFT与多阶段RL基准分值；再检验9种标准方向发现方法识别组合性危害（AUC最高0.99）但消融方向后要么拒绝不变、要么破坏连贯性，说明识别与拒绝解耦。据此提出CMaG：用拒绝-合规对数概率边际度量拒绝意图，对中层激活求梯度，并与部分良性配对对比以剥离跨模态交互项；80个样本一次反向传播即可得到方向，沿该方向引导可单调地将拒绝转为合规。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B与LLaVA-1.5-7B上，CMaG使组合性安全分数下降最多65分，同时超98%输出保持连贯，优于基于梯度的基线；所得方向可迁移到文本显式危害，并能对经过防御性训练加固的模型实现反对齐。

Conclusion: 组合性安全在行为层面可能表现强劲，却对定向内部干预依然脆弱；反对齐可作为审计LVLM对齐鲁棒性的有效补充手段，提醒仅凭基准分值不足以评估安全整合程度。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) are vulnerable to compositional harm: an image and a prompt that are benign in isolation become harmful through the model's cross-modal reasoning. Recent alignment methods report strong scores on compositional safety benchmarks, but an LLM judge only measures observed behavior and cannot reveal how robustly safety is integrated into the model. We argue that un-alignment provides a complementary audit of alignment robustness. We first show that minimal supervised fine-tuning reaches an LLM-judge score comparable to a multi-stage reinforcement-learning pipeline, so similar benchmark scores cannot establish robustness by themselves. We then show that recognition and refusal of compositional harm are decoupled: nine standard direction-finding methods detect compositional harm with up to AUC=0.99, yet ablating the recovered directions either leaves refusal intact or destroys model coherence. We therefore propose CMaG, a training-free un-alignment method for compositional safety. CMaG measures refusal intention with a refusal-versus-compliance log-probability margin, takes its gradient with respect to mid-layer activations, and contrasts it against partially benign pairs to isolate the cross-modal interaction term. A single backward pass over 80 examples yields a direction along which steering monotonically converts refusal into compliance. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.5-7B, CMaG reduces compositional safety scores by up to 65 points while over 98% of outputs remain coherent, outperforming gradient-based baselines; the direction also transfers to text-explicit harm and un-aligns models hardened by defensive training. Compositional safety can thus appear behaviorally strong while remaining fragile to targeted internal intervention. Warning: this paper contains unsafe model outputs, including references to self-harm.

</details>


### [29] [TileQuant: Tile as the Precision-Allocation Unit for LLM Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07217)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TileQuant：免训练后量化框架，以硬件对齐的2D权重瓦片（tile）为单位分配精度——离线标定按预算将高分瓦片保留16-bit、其余8-bit，并配合核函数复用紧凑权重缓冲，实现原生整数/16-bit局部累加，生成吞吐达原模型2.66x，且多任务质量基本无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ难以平衡"不对称权重敏感度"与部署效率：统一位宽PTQ忽略敏感度差异；importance-aware PTQ仅在同一宽度内调整scale或重构权重；列级混合精度保留整列高精度，会误保护同列内的低敏感权重；运行时outlier检测又引入解码开销。因此需要一个既细粒度贴合敏感度、又对硬件友好的精度分配粒度。

Method: 1) 精度单元改为硬件对齐的2D权重瓦片而非整列/整层；2) 免训练标定产生两类打分：前向统计得到的input-activation-aware打分（标定成本低）与梯度/损失敏感度得到的gradient-informed打分（保真更强）；3) 在每个投影层的保留预算内，得分最高的瓦片保持16-bit，其余量化到8-bit；4) 依据该map将权重重构进紧凑缓冲，kernel复用缓冲执行原生整数与16-bit计算，并采用local accumulation降低访存流量。

Result: 生成吞吐达到原始模型的2.66x；在语言建模、推理、长上下文检索、问答等任务上保持质量，并在多项任务上超过量化模型乃至原模型。

Conclusion: 结果支持"以能捕捉局部敏感度、同时便于高效硬件执行的单元作为精度分配粒度"这一设计原则；瓦片级混合精度在质量与部署效率之间取得更优折中。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces large language model (LLM) deployment costs, but preserving quality requires accounting for uneven weight sensitivity. Uniform PTQ uses one weight bit-width, importance-aware PTQ adjusts scales or reconstructs weights within that width, and column-wise mixed precision retains selected columns at higher precision. However, sensitivity varies within columns, so whole-column retention also protects less sensitive weights, while runtime outlier detection adds decoding overhead. We therefore introduce TileQuant, a training-free framework that assigns precision to hardware-aligned 2D weight tiles. Offline calibration provides input-activation-aware scoring from forward statistics for lower calibration cost and gradient-informed scoring from loss sensitivity for stronger quality preservation. Within each projection's retention budget, the highest-scoring tiles remain at 16-bit and the rest use 8-bit. The resulting map guides reconstruction into compact weight buffers, which the kernel reuses for native integer and 16-bit computation with local accumulation to reduce memory traffic. Experiments show that TileQuant reaches 2.66x the original model's generation throughput. Across language modeling, reasoning, long-context retrieval, and question answering, it preserves quality and surpasses quantized and original models on multiple tasks. These results support selecting precision units that capture local sensitivity while enabling efficient hardware execution. The implementation is: https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileQuant

</details>


### [30] [Loopback Transformer: Towards a Unified View of Residual Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07226)
*Xuan Ouyang,Zefan Cai,Chenlong Zhang,Cheng Luo,Ke Wan,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Loopback Transformer：将残差连接视为深度维记忆，把共享块的输入与增量更新分离读取，用几何衰减控制更新总权重、用注意力学习分配，在更少参数下取得更低验证损失与更高下游平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环Transformer仅通过共享参数压缩模型规模，但"参数共享"本身并不决定重复计算结果如何被复用；对整块输出加权会连带重新加权输入中已携带的历史，缺乏对输入强度、更新总权重及更新间分配的独立控制。

Method: 从残差连接的深度-记忆视角出发，分析每次块应用后存储何种表示及后续读取的加权方式。将共享堆栈的输入保留在独立通路上，仅读取各次增量更新，从而解耦输入强度、更新总权重与更新间分配三个控制量。Loopback Transformer据此设计：由几何衰减设定更新总权重并提供近因先验，由注意力学习如何分配该权重。

Result: 在ClimbMix上，参考规模191M至3B、训练token数与执行深度对齐的条件下，仅用基线40-75%的Transformer参数（不含token嵌入与输出头）即达到更低验证损失；1.4B与3B规模下十三项下游任务平均准确率更高；在191M、374M、778M规模上，于标准Transformer及七种循环残差设计中取得最低验证损失与最高十三任务均值。

Conclusion: 结果表明循环结构的收益取决于重复计算的复用方式而非单纯参数共享；显式解耦输入通路与更新读取、并结合学习的权重分配，可在显著减少参数的同时提升语言建模与下游泛化性能。

Abstract: Looped Transformers reduce parameter count by repeatedly applying shared blocks, but parameter sharing alone does not determine how the results of repeated computation are reused. We take the depth-memory view of residual connections and analyze which representation is stored after each block application and how later reads weight it. A full block output contains both its input and its new update, so weighting that output also reweights the history already carried by the input. Reading individual updates instead, with the shared stack's input kept on a separate path, gives separate controls over input strength, total update weight, and allocation across updates. The Loopback Transformer implements these controls: geometric decay sets the total update weight and supplies a recency prior, and attention learns how to allocate that weight. On ClimbMix, Loopback reaches lower validation loss than standard Transformer baselines with 40-75% of their Transformer parameters (excluding token embeddings and the output head) at reference scales from 191M to 3B, with training tokens and executed depth matched. It also has higher mean accuracy over thirteen downstream tasks at 1.4B and 3B. At 191M, 374M, and 778M, it has the lowest validation loss and the highest thirteen-task mean among the standard Transformer and seven looped residual designs.

</details>


### [31] [Neural Compression over Equivalence Classes: Exact Gauges and Functional Rate-Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07233)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用神经网络参数化的表示自由度（精确保持函数的变换与受控扰动），在物理压缩器不变的前提下搜索更易压缩的等价参数，实现最高23.4%的压缩率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络存在大量产生相同或几乎相同函数的参数化，但部署时通常只保存训练恰好得到的那个参数向量，未能利用这种表示自由度作为压缩资源。

Method: 不重新设计编解码器，而是在精确保持函数的规范（gauge）变换族与受控函数扰动空间内进行搜索，并用未修改的物理压缩器（Zstandard、gzip、Brotli、ZipNN）本身作为选择更廉价表示的目标函数；精确变换族在严格审计下提供零失真基准，允许微小函数变化则带来更大率失真收益。

Result: 在SmolLM2与Qwen3上，Zstandard压缩后检查点体积最多减少23.4%，在gzip、Brotli、ZipNN下呈现相近的相对降幅。

Conclusion: 训练网络的表示自由度可作为一种实用的存储资源，与现有压缩器正交兼容。

Abstract: Neural networks admit many parameterizations of the same or nearly the same function, but deployment typically stores whichever parameter vector training happens to produce. We show that this representational freedom can itself be used for compression. Rather than redesigning the codec, we search over exact function-preserving transformations and controlled functional perturbations, using the unchanged physical compressor itself to select cheaper representations. Exact gauge families provide a zero-distortion reference under the strict audit; allowing small functional changes exposes much larger rate-distortion gains. Across SmolLM2 and Qwen3, our method reduces Zstandard-compressed checkpoint size by up to 23.4\%, with similar relative reductions under gzip, Brotli, and ZipNN, establishing trained-network representational freedom as a practical storage resource.

</details>


### [32] [$D^2$-OPD: Separating Direction and Distance in Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07241)
*Bowen Fang,Dingmin Wang,Hengzhi Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D²-OPD，将外推式 on-policy 蒸馏中的外推方向与外推距离解耦：用逐前缀的硬 KL 约束限制目标偏离教师模型的距离，用自适应组合教师后训练偏移与初始学生-教师差距来确定外推方向，并以 forward KL 优化学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外推式 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）方法将外推方向与外推距离耦合，使得教师对数概率偏移的幅度直接决定目标移动的远近，缺乏对这两个因素的独立控制。

Method: D²-OPD：1）逐前缀硬 KL 约束界定目标相对教师模型的距离；2）自适应地组合教师的 post-training shift 与初始 student-teacher gap 以确定外推方向；3）使用 forward KL 将学生模型优化至该目标，从而对低概率 token 也提供学习信号。评测覆盖三种设定：标准 OPD 表现良好、基座模型初始化学时表现不佳、以及无教师参考可用。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，D²-OPD 在三种设定中均取得最高分，相对最强基线最高提升 9.45 分；消融实验验证了各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 方向与距离的解耦是提升外推式 on-policy 蒸馏效果的关键，D²-OPD 在多种初始化与教师可用性条件下均具稳健优势。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model to match a teacher's next-token distributions on student-generated prefixes. Extrapolative OPD further extends the teacher's log-probability shift relative to a reference policy to construct stronger learning targets. However, existing methods couple extrapolation direction and distance, allowing the magnitude of the teacher's shift to dictate how far the target moves. We propose $D^2$-OPD, decoupling these two factors: a per-prefix hard KL constraint bounds the target's distance from the teacher, while an adaptive combination of the teacher's post-training shift and the initial student--teacher gap determines the extrapolation direction. We optimize the student toward this target using forward KL, providing learning signals even for low-probability tokens. We evaluate three settings: where standard OPD performs well, where it struggles with base-model initialization, and where no teacher reference is available. On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, $D^2$-OPD attains the highest score in all three settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 9.45 points. Ablations validate the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [33] [MAC-v2: Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07245)
*Kwanyoung Park,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAC-v2，通过无重建潜在世界模型与动作分块实现像素级离线RL的长时程基于模型的价值展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素环境下的长时程基于模型的价值展开比状态环境困难得多：智能体需同时学习表示与动力学，且两者的误差会随想象轨迹递归累积，阻碍有效价值展开。

Method: MAC-v2：训练无重建（reconstruction-free）潜在世界模型，表示同时由预测目标与控制目标学习，避免编码与控制无关的视觉细节；引入动作分块（action chunking），减少价值展开所需的递归模型预测步数，从而抑制误差累积。

Result: 在visual OGBench、MMBench2、Robomimic、LIBERO-10等视觉基准上，相较无模型与基于模型的离线RL基线取得强劲表现；消融分析表明长时程价值展开对性能至关重要，动作分块与潜在学习目标对可靠的长时程潜在想象均为必要。

Conclusion: 长时程基于模型的价值展开可作为像素级离线RL的有效框架；无重建潜在表示与动作分块是缓解表示与动力学误差递归累积的关键设计。

Abstract: We study whether \emph{long-horizon model-based value expansion} can serve as an effective framework in pixel-based offline reinforcement learning (RL). Long-horizon model-based value expansion from pixels is substantially harder than state-based environments, as the agent must learn both the representation and its dynamics, and errors from either can accumulate recursively over imagined trajectories. We introduce MAC-v2, which enables long-horizon model-based expansion from pixels by training a reconstruction-free latent world model with action chunking. By learning representations from prediction and control objectives, MAC-v2 does not need to encode control-irrelevant visual details that can hinder the dynamics prediction. Action chunking further mitigates the prediction error by reducing the number of recursive model predictions required for value expansion. Across diverse visual benchmarks including visual OGBench, MMBench2, Robomimic, and LIBERO-10, MAC-v2 achieves strong performance against model-free and model-based offline RL baselines. Our analyses show that long-horizon model-based value expansion is critical for the performance of MAC-v2, and that both action chunking and latent learning objectives are essential for reliable long-horizon latent imagination.

</details>


### [34] [KG-Neighbor: Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Construction for Embedding Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07246)
*Chang Won Kim,Jaewook Lee,Haeun Min,JunNyeong Jeong,Minwoo Sim,Barom Kang,Kyohoon Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KGN，用知识图谱三元组共享度重构嵌入的kNN邻域图以指导降维，并引入IDProfile诊断残余失配来源；在八种文本/图像条件下NMI与LT_2D均超越kNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入可视化中，邻近实例常被解读为语义相关，但从输入嵌入导出的邻域未必反映真实内容关系；基于邻域的降维方法以嵌入邻域图为优化目标，会把这一缺陷传播到布局中。作者希望在不使用类标签的前提下，用外部关系知识校正邻域结构。

Method: 1) Knowledge-Guided Neighborhood Graph Construction (KGN)：将每个实例的关系知识表示为一组知识图谱三元组，以“三元组共享度”作为成对语义证据来调整嵌入距离，并重建top-k邻域图；KG抽取与邻域构建均不使用类标签。2) 引入IDProfile (IDP)：针对残余失配，独立评估源内容是否支持分配的类标签以及是否支持邻域多数类，将案例区分为Placement Issue、Label Issue与Unresolved Case。3) 在八个文本与图像数据集-编码器条件下，对引导强度做全扫描并比较四种KG相似度度量。
