A reversible pair sampler preserves the learner's intended marginal at both played actions. The estimator's second-moment and stability bounds support online updates for both feedback models.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring the Marginal Value of Verifier-Based RL Beyond Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07639)
*Amit Sagtani,Saloni Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可控基准 gpu-train-debug 上直接度量 RLVR 相对 SFT 的贡献：SFT 解释了绝大部分可得提升，GRPO 在分布内无可检测增益（+0.22 分，CI [-0.06,+0.51]），仅在 OOD 上有小幅增益（+0.33 分，CI [+0.02,+0.68]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常把 RLVR 视为 SFT 的替代或后续阶段，隐含假设 RL 阶段本身带来独立增益，但这一贡献缺乏严格控制下的直接度量。作者希望在两阶段都能跑完、可精确对比的条件下量化 RLVR 的边际作用。

Method: 构建 gpu-train-debug：1,877 条合成 GPU/LLM 训练故障诊断任务，覆盖 30 个类型，由程序化验证器打分并给予部分分，难度校准至中档模型得分落在 0.60–0.75。先做试点，随后在书面预注册下修正研究问题，全文实验规模为 60 步 GRPO、学习率 1e-6，并设 60 步冷启动 GRPO 对照臂；SFT 使用 1,200 条黄金示范；评估含分布内与留出 OOD 划分。

Result: 试点中 SFT 在难分片上达 0.785，而基座模型仅 0.311、15 步 GRPO 后为 0.314。预注册规模下，分布内提升 +0.22 分（95% CI [-0.06, +0.51]，不可检测），OOD 提升 +0.33 分（CI [+0.02, +0.68]，可测但幅度有限）。冷启动 GRPO 全程复现试点结论。全研究算力成本 $8.69。

Conclusion: 在示范充分的设定下，SFT 占据绝大部分可得提升，验证器驱动的 RL 只带来有限的分布外增益。SFT 残留的错误多为系统性、跨样本高度一致，可能削弱 GRPO 的组内学习信号。研究依托归档生成结果、冻结配置与打标代码完全可复现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is usually evaluated as an alternative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT), or as a stage that follows it, with the implicit assumption that the RL stage contributes gains of its own. We measure that contribution directly, in a controlled setting where both stages can be run to completion and compared exactly. We built gpu-train-debug, a deterministic benchmark of 1,877 synthetic GPU and LLM training failure diagnosis tasks across 30 archetypes, graded by a programmatic verifier with partial credit, and calibrated so that a mid-tier model scores in a 0.60 to 0.75 band. A pilot produced a surprise: 1,200 gold demonstrations nearly solved the benchmark (SFT 0.785 on the hard split), while 15 steps of GRPO from the base model did not move it (0.314 vs 0.311 base). We therefore amended the study, in writing and before the full run, to ask the sharper question: what does verifier-based RL add beyond demonstrations? At the pre-registered scale (60 GRPO steps, learning rate 1e-6), the answer is: no detectable improvement in-distribution (+0.22 points, 95% CI [-0.06, +0.51]), and a modest but measurable improvement under distribution shift (+0.33 points on a held-out OOD split, CI [+0.02, +0.68]). SFT accounted for most of the available improvement, while verifier-based RL produced only modest additional gains under distribution shift. The residual errors SFT leaves are mostly systematic, identical across samples, which likely reduces the within-group learning signal available to GRPO. A full 60-step cold-start GRPO arm confirms the pilot at the full horizon. The whole study cost $8.69 of compute and is fully reproducible from archived generations, frozen configs, and tagged code.

</details>


### [70] [WaSP: Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline for Solver-Free Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07646)
*Ryan Liu,Chuwei Wang,Ricardo Baptista,Andrew Stuart,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WaSP：沿中间分布路径分阶段合成"输入-解"配对，随训练逐步缩小分布偏移，实现无求解器的神经算子训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子训练通常依赖昂贵的输入-解配对数据。两种无求解器替代方案各有缺陷：物理信息学习受残差范数权衡制约——范数需足够强才能控制解误差，但强范数会增大 mini-batch 方差、损害优化；制造数据（对人工解施加 PDE 算子生成配对）虽便宜，但其训练分布可能与目标分布互相奇异，导致目标分布上精度差。

Method: 提出 Wasserstein 数据合成流程（WaSP）：构造从制造数据分布收敛到目标输入分布的中间分布路径，分阶段训练。每阶段由上一代代理模型生成解，再用精确 PDE 算子反推对应输入，得到下一代理模型的训练对，从而在训练过程中逐步缓解分布偏移。

Result: 理论证明合成过程引入的分布偏移满足逐步误差传播界；在 Allen-Cahn、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、Ginzburg-Landau 等 PDE 上，相比现有无求解器方法解误差降低 3.9×–9.5×，并更好地复现两个混沌系统的统计可观测量。

Conclusion: 以分布路径上的迭代式数据合成替代直接训练，可兼顾无求解器的低成本与目标分布上的高精度，为神经算子的无监督/免求解训练提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Neural operators offer fast surrogates for solving PDEs, but training them typically requires expensive input-solution pairs. Two solver-free alternatives include physics-informed learning and training on manufactured data. Physics-informed learning approximates solutions by minimizing PDE residuals. While the residual norm must be strong enough to control solution error, stronger norms increase mini-batch variance and hinder optimization. Manufactured data constructs input-solution pairs for learning by applying the PDE operator to artificial solutions. However, the resulting training distribution can be mutually singular with the target distribution, yielding poor accuracy on the target distribution. We introduce the Wasserstein Data Synthesis Pipeline (WaSP), which trains on manufactured input-solution pairs while gradually mitigating the distribution shift. WaSP synthesizes new pairs by applying the learned operator to a path of intermediate distributions converging to the target input distribution. Training proceeds in stages along this path. At each stage, the previous surrogate generates solutions, and the exact PDE operator derives corresponding inputs, yielding training pairs for the next surrogate. Theoretically, we prove a stepwise error-propagation bound for the distribution shift introduced during synthesis. Empirically, WaSP reduces solution error by $3.9\times$ to $9.5\times$ compared to existing solver-free methods on PDEs such as Allen-Cahn, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, and Ginzburg-Landau, and better reproduces the two chaotic systems' statistical observables.

</details>


### [71] [Simple Meta-Learning for HPO and CASH Using Performance Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07659)
*Emiel Witting,Cheng Yan,Tom Julian Viering*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于历史性能的元学习表示替代显式超参数编码，将其与标准代理模型及MMR多样性重排序结合，用于AutoML的迭代配置选择，在TabArena上以约一半试验次数达到同等调优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在HPO与CASH中通常依赖显式超参数编码作为代理模型输入，但在高维搜索空间中，这类表示使标准代理模型难以学到有效的性能模型。作者希望找到一种与搜索空间维度解耦的替代表示。

Method: 将每个配置表示为它在先前任务上的历史性能（performance-based representation），直接与标准代理模型（如线性Bayesian ridge回归）配对；同时将该表示用于最大边际相关性（MMR）这一来自推荐系统的多样性重排序准则，作为无后验的采集规则。研究范围限定为AutoML的迭代选择组件，即在固定候选集中决定下一个评估配置，不涉及运行时估计、成本感知预算与集成等完整框架功能。

Result: 在TabArena上进行多模型选择与优化时，性能表示+线性Bayesian ridge回归优于专用元学习基线，以约一半的试验次数达到同等调优性能；在单模型HPO中位列第二，仍具竞争力。两个基准上该方法在远低复杂度与成本下接近或改善regret，无需预训练或调参，且扩展性与搜索空间维度无关。

Conclusion: 以历史性能为元特征表示的简单方法可有效替代显式超参数编码，配合标准代理模型和MMR采集规则即可在高维搜索空间中实现高效、低成本的配置选择，并保持与维度无关的可扩展性。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization for Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) and Combined Algorithm Selection and Hyperparameter Optimization (CASH) often relies on explicit hyperparameter encodings as surrogate inputs. In high-dimensional search spaces, however, such representations can make it difficult for standard surrogates to learn useful performance models. We instead study an alternative meta-learning representation that describes each configuration directly as its historical performance across prior tasks. This representation can be paired directly with standard surrogate models. Our scope is the iterative selection component of AutoML, deciding which configuration to evaluate next from a fixed candidate set, rather than a complete framework with features such as runtime estimation, cost-aware budgeting and ensembling. We further apply this representation to maximal marginal relevance (MMR), a diversity-based re-ranking criterion from recommender systems, as a posterior-free acquisition rule. When selecting and optimising across multiple models on the TabArena benchmark, the performance-based representation paired with a linear Bayesian ridge regression model outperforms dedicated meta-learning baselines, achieving the same tuned performance in roughly half the number of trials. For single-model HPO, it remains a competitive second. Across both benchmarks this method closely matches or improves on regret at a fraction of the complexity and cost, requiring no pre-training or tuning and scaling independently of search space dimensionality. Code for reproducing our results is available at https://github.com/EWitting/simple-metalearning.

</details>


### [72] [An Information Bottleneck for Post-hoc Memorization Mitigation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07669)
*Dina EL Zein,Shashi Kumar,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在冻结的LLM骨干与语言建模头之间插入非参数变分信息瓶颈(NVIB)模块，通过压缩末层隐表示来减少训练数据记忆的提取，同时保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可记忆并复现受版权保护的训练数据，带来隐私与知识产权风险；作者希望在无需修改或重训底层模型的前提下，以信息瓶颈方式缓解记忆问题。

Method: 提出模块化、即插即用框架：冻结LLM骨干和LM头不变，在token预测前的最终隐表示上加入非参数变分信息瓶颈(NVIB)进行压缩，力图保留通用语言知识而过滤高度具体的记忆序列；在书籍段落记忆提取任务与标准语言建模基准上评估记忆减少与下游效用的权衡。

Result: NVIB层显著降低记忆数据的可提取性，同时在基准任务上保持有竞争力的性能，表明记忆性与实用性之间存在可控权衡。

Conclusion: 添加信息瓶颈为预训练LLM的事后记忆缓解提供了一种实用且与架构无关的方向，无需修改或重训原模型。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce copyrighted training data, raising serious privacy and intellectual property concerns. In this work, we investigate whether memorization can be mitigated without modifying or retraining the underlying model through an information bottleneck. We propose a modular plug-and-play framework that inserts a nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) between a frozen LLM backbone and its language modeling head. By compressing the final hidden representations before token prediction, the proposed bottleneck aims to preserve general linguistic knowledge while filtering out highly specific memorized sequences. We evaluate our approach on memorization extraction tasks involving book passages and on standard language modeling benchmarks to measure the trade-off between memorization reduction and downstream utility. Our experiments show that the NVIB layer substantially reduces extractability while maintaining competitive performance on benchmark tasks. These results suggest that adding an information bottleneck offers a practical and architecture agnostic direction for post-hoc memorization mitigation in pretrained LLMs.

</details>


### [73] [Calibrated Target Noise Recovers Curvature from the Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07671)
*Arash Jamshidi,Katsiaryna Haitsiukevich,Aristides Gionis,Kai Puolamäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种仅利用（小批量聚合）梯度即可恢复曲率信息的简单方法：向目标注入方差按局部输出曲率校准的零均值噪声，再从噪声梯度的协方差中恢复实用的曲率矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从梯度中估计曲率是机器学习的基础问题，而在仅有聚合小批量梯度的场景下尤为困难，但预条件优化、采样与早停等应用仍需要曲率信息。现有方法通常依赖逐样本梯度、二阶导数或网络内部量，代价高、集成难。

Method: 采用目标扰动（target-perturbation）方案：向训练目标注入零均值噪声，其方差按局部输出曲率校准；对所得含噪批量梯度的协方差进行估计，从而得到实用曲率矩阵，无需逐样本梯度、二阶导或其他内部网络量。理论上证明对一大类非线性模型可恢复广义高斯–牛顿（GGN）矩阵，对前馈 ReLU 网络还可恢复总体 Hessian 的对角块，并证明该估计器简单、轻量且可证最优。

Result: 实验表明曲率恢复准确；将估计器用于预条件时优化收敛更快；估计的 GGN 对早停具有实用价值。

Conclusion: 简单的目标扰动加噪声梯度协方差即可从聚合梯度中有效恢复曲率，方法轻量、易集成到现有训练流程，并可支持预条件优化与早停等下游应用。

Abstract: Estimating curvature information from gradients alone is a fundamental problem in machine learning. The challenge becomes particularly acute in settings where only aggregate mini-batch gradients are available, yet curvature information is still required for applications such as preconditioning in optimisation, sampling, and early stopping. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that a simple target-perturbation scheme suffices to recover such curvature information from gradients alone. Our method injects zero-mean noise into the targets, with variance calibrated to the local output curvature. The covariance of the resulting noisy batch gradients then recovers practical curvature matrices, without requiring per-sample gradients, second-order derivatives, or other internal network quantities. For a broad class of non-linear models, our scheme recovers the Generalised Gauss--Newton (GGN) matrix; for feed-forward ReLU networks, it additionally recovers the diagonal blocks of the population Hessian. We demonstrate that the resulting estimator is simple, lightweight, provably optimal, and easy to integrate into existing training pipelines. Experiments demonstrate accurate curvature recovery, faster optimisation when the estimator is used for preconditioning, and the utility of the estimated GGN for early stopping.

</details>


### [74] [Instrumental Variables in Continuous-Time Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07674)
*Lars Pennig,Søren Wengel Mogensen,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出连续时间随机过程（SDE）下的工具变量（IV）框架，以路径值工具变量导矩条件，实现混杂下因果效应的识别与一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IV方法仅在静态或离散时间设定下识别因果效应，尚不能推广到连续时间动态系统，而此类系统中的因果问题十分常见。

Method: 针对由SDE参数化的平稳连续时间随机过程，将结构稀疏性与因果效应直接编码在漂移项的无穷小层面，并与同一过程离散化后的IV假设作对比；基于这些结构假设，利用路径值工具变量推导矩约束；对Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程给出矩约束的简化形式、识别条件及一致估计方法；进一步借助签名核（signature kernel）将路径矩约束推广至非线性因果效应。

Result: 在存在混杂的连续时间系统中，所提方法能够恢复因果效应，而基于极大似然的估计与离散时间IV方法会出现系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 将IV识别从离散/静态设定推广到连续时间SDE动力学，为混杂下连续时间因果推断提供了有理论保证且可实用的估计框架。

Abstract: Causal questions often arise in systems that evolve in continuous time. While instrumental variable (IV) methods provide a principled approach to identify causal effects in static or discrete-time settings, even in the presence of unobserved confounding, they do not yet extend to continuous-time dynamics. In this work, we develop an IV framework for stationary continuous-time stochastic processes parameterized by stochastic differential equations (SDE), where the instrument itself takes the form of a stochastic process. We encode the structural sparsity and causal effects directly at the infinitesimal level of the drift, and contrast these assumptions with the IV assumptions on the discretized version of the same process. Based on these structural assumptions, we derive moment restrictions using path-valued instruments. We show how these restrictions simplify for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes and present an identification condition together with a corresponding consistent estimation method. Finally, we extend our estimation framework to nonlinear causal effects by expressing the path-based moment restrictions leveraging the signature kernel. Empirically, our method recovers causal effects in confounded continuous-time systems where maximum-likelihood-based estimation and discrete-time IV methods are systematically biased.

</details>


### [75] [$O(1/n)$ Convergence Rate for RVI $Q$-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07676)
*Avik Kar,Siddharth Chandak,Rahul Singh,Shalabh Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文为弱通信有限MDP上同步RVI Q-learning给出有限时间收敛保证：一般非扩张线性正则映射的Krasnosel'skiĭ–Mann迭代得到$O(n^{-a})$末迭代均方残差界，应用于RVI Q-learning得到$O(n^{-1})$的Bellman span残差与期望次优性界，即$O(\varepsilon^{-1})$次迭代可达$\varepsilon$-最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVI Q-learning（相对值迭代Q学习）是求解平均奖励/弱通信MDP的经典算法，但此前缺乏有限时间（非渐近）收敛速率保证；作者希望为同步RVI Q-learning建立显式的末迭代（last-iterate）收敛界，并刻画其对实例参数的依赖。

Method: 先建立一般性理论结果：对有限维赋范空间中由非扩张、线性正则（linearly regular）映射驱动的随机Krasnosel'skiĭ–Mann迭代，在鞅差噪声（条件二阶矩至多随残差平方线性增长）下，用$n^{-a}$（$0<a\le1$）多项式步长得到末迭代均方残差$O(n^{-a})$界。再验证弱通信MDP的中心化Bellman算子满足线性正则性与噪声条件，从而将一般结果用于同步RVI Q-learning。

Result: 对同步RVI Q-learning，使用合适步长序列时得到均方Bellman span残差的$O(n^{-1})$界；利用最优与次优平稳确定性策略之间的正间隙，得到贪心策略期望次优性的$O(n^{-1})$界，因此$O(\varepsilon^{-1})$次同步迭代即足以获得期望意义下的$\varepsilon$-最优策略。常数依赖于命中时间参数与次优策略（第二优策略）的次优性间隙，具有实例依赖性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为弱通信MDP上的同步RVI Q-learning提供了有限时间、末迭代、实例相关的收敛速率保证，填补了渐近收敛与实用复杂度之间的空白；所提的随机Krasnosel'skiĭ–Mann迭代分析框架也可推广到其他非扩张算子驱动的强化学习算法。

Abstract: We establish finite-time convergence guarantee for synchronous relative value iteration (RVI) $Q$-learning for finite weakly communicating Markov decision processes (MDPs). For stochastic Krasnosel'skiĭ--Mann iterations driven by a non-expansive, linearly regular map on a finite-dimensional normed space, we establish last-iterate mean-squared residual bounds under martingale-difference noise whose conditional second moment grows at most linearly with the squared residual. Suitable polynomial stepsizes of order $n^{-a}$ yield an $O(n^{-a})$ rate for $0<a\leq1$. We verify linear regularity and the noise condition for the centered Bellman operator for finite weakly communicating MDPs. Applying the general result gives an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the mean-squared Bellman span residual of RVI $Q$-learning iterates when a suitable stepsize sequence is used. The positive gap between optimal and suboptimal stationary deterministic policies then yields an $O(n^{-1})$ bound on the expected suboptimality of the greedy policy. Consequently, $O(ε^{-1})$ synchronous iterations suffice for an $ε$-optimal policy in expectation. These guarantees are instance-dependent, with constants that depend on the hitting-time parameters and the suboptimality gap of the second-best policy.

</details>


### [76] [Non-Parametric Mutual Information Estimation with Vine Copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07693)
*Sigurd Holmsen,Berit Omli Øksnes,Ingrid Hobæk Haff,Sylvia Richardson,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 VINE，一种基于简化 D-vine copula 的非参数互信息（MI）估计器：将联合密度用简化 D-vine 近似后，密度比化简为成对 copula 的乘积，从而可非参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互信息估计主流为神经 MI 估计器（SotA neural MI estimators），但其计算成本较高；作者希望获得灵活且计算高效的 MI 估计方法。

Method: 基于简化 D-vine copula 近似联合密度，使密度比分解为成对 copula 乘积，并以非参数方式估计各成对 copula；结构简化带来灵活性并降低计算开销。

Result: 在多个数据集上，分别在与简化 D-vine 假设一致和假设被违反两种设定下，与当前最优神经 MI 估计器对比：在低至中等维度数据集上以更低计算成本取得有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: VINE 是一种独特、计算高效且灵活性强的非参数 MI 估计器；其优势主要体现在低至中等维度场景，且在假设不完全满足时仍具竞争力。

Abstract: We introduce VINE: a non-parametric mutual information (MI) estimator based on simplified D-vine copulas. Approximating the joint density with a simplified D-vine allows the density ratio to simplify to a product of pair copulas, which we can estimate non-parametrically. This structural simplification yields VINE, a flexible and computationally efficient MI estimator. We compare VINE against SotA neural MI estimators on a range of datasets, both under settings where the simplified D-vine assumption holds and where it is violated, demonstrating competitive accuracy at a lower computational cost for low- to medium-dimensional datasets.

</details>


### [77] [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255)
*Hongeun Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeNO将奖励引导的初始噪声优化摊销为一次学习的残差输送，推理时单次噪声校正即可提升奖励，无需测试时迭代搜索，并保持生成器冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型的奖励对齐常建立在随机轨迹上，不适配少步确定性生成器；现有的无梯度初始噪声优化需在推理时逐样本昂贵搜索。

Method: 学习锚点条件的噪声修正分布（残差输送），用Local GRPO与标量奖励训练，生成器冻结；针对高斯初始噪声与保范投影，推导校正后噪声分布与原始先验间KL散度的可处理上界作为正则目标。

Result: 在少步文本到图像及蛋白质结构生成（含昂贵黑盒可设计性奖励）上，单次噪声校正即提升目标奖励，同时基本保持生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 以摊销学习替代推理时搜索，为少步确定性生成器提供了轨迹无关、高效的奖励对齐方案，并具理论正则保障。

Abstract: Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [78] [Equivariant Deep Learning in Direct-Product Space for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07695)
*Shi Yin,Zujian Dai,Xinyang Pan,Lixin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-Learner，一种直接在直积空间的Hamiltonian类算符块上进行消息传递的等变学习框架，避免显式Clebsch–Gordan分解与逆重构，并构建MP-HAM-110K大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有等变架构在不可约球张量直和空间中传播特征，而目标Hamiltonian本质上是局域轨道与自旋基上的直积表示算符块。这种表示失配割裂了连续Hamiltonian块、掩盖了轨道耦合的直接操作，并将特征组织为不规则角动量分量，导致大量变换、与硬件执行和内存管理不匹配。

Method: 在直积空间直接做消息传递：(1) 提出基于李生成元的方向嵌入；(2) 提出等变不变的左右乘积与收缩操作，保持对SO(3)、SU(2)及空间反演的等变性，无需显式Clebsch–Gordan分解或逆重构；(3) 实例化为Transformer架构，Hamiltonian类特征矩阵/张量通过直积空间等变算符演化，非线性的不变标量路由控制矩阵单元门控、方向传播、注意力及环境上下文交互。

Result: 构建MP-HAM-110K数据集，含逾11万个材料结构、覆盖68种元素，兼具Hamiltonian与波函数数据。在MP-HAM-110K与Materials-HAM-SOC上的实验表明，DP-Learner相较SOTA方法在Hamiltonian预测精度与推理效率上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将表示空间与目标Hamiltonian的直积结构对齐，DP-Learner在理论等变性、架构可行性与实验性能上统一解决了表示失配与计算瓶颈问题，并提供了大规模基准数据支撑。

Abstract: Most existing equivariant architectures for Hamiltonian prediction propagate features in direct-sum spaces of irreducible spherical tensors, whereas the target Hamiltonian is naturally an operator block in a direct-product representation over local orbital and spin bases. This representation mismatch fragments contiguous Hamiltonian blocks, obscures direct manipulation of orbital couplings, and organizes features into irregular angular-momentum components that require numerous transformations and are poorly aligned with efficient hardware execution and memory management. To address both the representation mismatch and computational bottleneck, we propose DP-Learner, an equivariant learning framework that performs message passing directly on Hamiltonian-like operator blocks in the direct-product space. Theoretically, we propose Lie-generator-based directional embedding and invariantly weighted left--right products and contractions that preserve equivariance to SO(3), SU(2), and spatial inversions, without explicit Clebsch--Gordan decomposition or inverse reconstruction. Methodologically, we instantiate this principle as a Transformer architecture, in which Hamiltonian-like feature matrices or tensors evolve through equivariant operators in the direct-product space, while non-linear invariant scalar routing controls structured gating over matrix units, directional propagation, attention, and environment-embedded context interactions. We further construct MP-HAM-110K, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset comprising more than 110,000 material structures covering 68 elements with both Hamiltonian and wavefunction data. Experiments on MP-HAM-110K and Materials-HAM-SOC demonstrate that DP-Learner dramatically improves Hamiltonian prediction accuracy and inference efficiency over the state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating Language Model Features for Zero-Shot Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Isolation Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07720)
*Romain Hermary,Nesryne Mejri,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM纯作为特征提取器，采用"每token一个时间步"的细粒度分词方案，直接分析LLM诱导的特征空间，并以无监督Isolation Forest评估其在多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）零样本场景下的可分性与判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTSAD本质困难：无监督、需高局部精度、标注稀缺。现有将LLM跨模态迁移至时间序列的工作不适配MTSAD——分词遮蔽了细粒度时序与变量结构，且多依赖预测/重建等辅助目标；本文认为模型容量增大时这些代理目标不再可靠，高表达能力模型可能把异常也重建/预测出来，从而破坏两类策略的基础。

Method: 摒弃代理目标，将LLM仅用作特征提取器并直接分析其特征空间；在零样本设定下通过分类考察LLM表征对MTSAD的有效性：(1) 研究提取特征的可分性，假设异常特征应与正常特征隔离；(2) 采用简单逐步分词（一步一token）以保留细粒度局部信息，分析其时序与上下文判别能力；(3) 用无监督Isolation Forest直接评估表征质量；(4) 设计对照实验，检验性能是否可归因于LLM的语言相关预训练，还是独立于该预训练而产生。

Result: 通过零样本分类与特征可分性分析，给出LLM表征多变量时序用于异常检测的实证结果；Isolation Forest上的表现直接反映所提取表征的质量，并区分出语言预训练贡献与表征本身固有能力的相对作用。

Conclusion: 结果提供了关于LLM能否表征多变量时序以支持异常检测的经验性洞见，凸显跨模态基础模型更广泛的应用潜力，为MTSAD中LLM的使用方式（特征提取而非代理目标优化）提供了依据。

Abstract: Time-Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD) is a fundamentally challenging task: it is unsupervised, requires high local precision capabilities, and suffers from limited labelled data. Recent work has explored adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to time-series via cross-modal transfer. However, existing approaches are poorly suited to Multivariate TSAD (MTSAD), as tokenisation often obscures fine-grained temporal and variables structures, and most methods optimise auxiliary objectives such as forecasting or reconstruction. We posit that these surrogate objectives become unreliable as model capacity increases. Highly expressive models risk reconstructing or forecasting anomalies, undermining the foundations of both strategies. Instead, we propose to use LLMs purely as feature extractors and directly analyse their induced feature spaces. In this work, investigate the ability of LLMs to provide useful representations for MTSADs in a zero-shot setting through classification. To this end, we study the separability of the extracted features, under the assumption that anomalous features should be isolated from normal ones. We analyse their temporal and contextual discriminative ability using simple step-wise tokenisation schemes, with one time-step per token, which preserve fine-grained local information. Their usability for MTSAD is assessed using an unsupervised Isolation Forest, enabling a direct evaluation of the resulting representation quality. We further investigate whether the observed performance can be attributed to the language-related pre-training of the LLMs or arises independently of it. Our results provide empirical insight into the ability of LLMs to represent multivariate time-series for anomaly detection and highlight broader applications of cross-modal foundation models.

</details>


### [80] [Reinforcing What the Student Has Learned: Teacher-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07722)
*Bozhen Fang,Linhao Zhong,Xuehao Wang,Huanyi Zheng,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SD-OPD以学生预训练基座为参照，用中心化的学生-基座对数概率差作为更新系数并按符号与师生反馈一致筛选，解决on-policy蒸馏中风格位置更新幅度过大问题，在Qwen3 1.7B–8B五个配置上数学平均pass@1提升7.76–10.37个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD依赖教师对学生自生成回答的稠密反馈，但作者观察到反馈存在幅度失衡：数学推理回答中，风格相关token位置的平均绝对师生对数概率差是数学内容位置的3.5倍，导致按该差异缩放更新时，大量更新强度被导向模仿教师的表达风格而非推理能力。

Method: 提出Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation（SD-OPD）：以学生自身预训练基座为参照，用中心化的学生-基座对数概率差刻画后训练获得的token偏好，构成不依赖学生与教师距离的监督信号；仅保留符号与中心化师生反馈方向一致的系数，并在每轮训练中重新计算学生信号。

Result: 在Qwen3 1.7B至8B学生模型、5组师生配置下，六项数学任务平均pass@1（avg@16）相较OPD提升7.76至10.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 以学生基座为参照、符号一致的delta式系数能缓解OPD中风格与推理更新幅度失衡，显著提升数学推理蒸馏效果；代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model reasoning through dense teacher feedback on student-generated responses.We first observe an amplitude disparity in this feedback: on math reasoning responses, style-related positions exhibit 3.5 times the mean absolute teacher--student log-probability difference of mathematical positions.Scaling OPD updates by these discrepancies thus directs much of the update strength toward matching the teacher's expression. We instead take the student's own pretrained base as a reference: the student--base difference records the token preferences acquired during post-training and provides supervision that does not depend on the student's distance from the teacher. Building on this reference, we propose Student-Delta On-Policy Distillation (SD-OPD), which uses centered student--base differences as update coefficients, keeps each coefficient only when its sign agrees with centered teacher--student feedback, and recomputes the student signal every training round. Across 5 teacher--student configurations with Qwen3 students of 1.7B to 8B parameters, SD-OPD improves six-task mathematics average pass@1 (avg@16) over OPD by 7.76 to 10.37 percentage points. Code is available at https://github.com/aim-uofa/sd-opd.

</details>


### [81] [Static Recovery Is Not Dynamic Stability: Dynamics-Aware Benchmarking of Protein Motif Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07723)
*Emile de Bruyn,Moritz Schäffler,Jannik Schneider,Sebastian R. Schmidt,Chanwoong Hwang,Aleena Siji,Utkarsh Upadhyay,Stefan Bauer,Alexander Schug,Stefan Kesselheim,Artur Yakimovich,Alina Bazarova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BILBY 提出一套蛋白质 motif 支架生成评测框架：BILBY-PRO 基于 PROSITE 系统构建 220 个结构保守功能 motif 基准，BILBY-MD 引入基于参考的全原子分子动力学判据。六种模型的覆盖度与独特解产量呈现分化，静态重折叠成功的 55 个设计对中仅 31 个满足 MD 判据、8 个通过全部检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 motif scaffolding 基准存在两重局限：一是 motif 集规模小、手工筛选，覆盖面有限；二是成功判据为静态重折叠，而蛋白功能常依赖构象系综与构象转变，静态指标无法刻画动力学保真度。

Method: 1) BILBY-PRO：从 PROSITE motif-pattern 关联的实验结构中系统提取结构保守的功能 motif，覆盖 174 个 PROSITE 条目、共 220 个案例，并用于评测 6 个近期 scaffold 生成模型的目标覆盖度与独特解产量。2) BILBY-MD：基于参考模拟的全原子 MD 评价框架，对 MotifBench 中静态可解设计比较 motif 漂移、支架漂移、motif 接触保持以及 motif 二面角自由能景观。

Result: 模型表现分化：RFDiffusion 与 Proteina 解出更多目标；ESM3 在其能解出的目标上产生更多独特成功支架。MD 评测中，55 个静态可解的模型-问题对里 31 个满足 MD 支持判据，仅 8 个通过全部检查。紧凑、自包含的 motif 更易获 MD 支持，界面型或依赖上下文的 motif 失败更频繁。

Conclusion: 静态重折叠成功可提示局部 motif 恢复，但不能确证完整支架的动力学保真度。motif scaffolding 评测应沿目标覆盖度、独特解产量与 MD 支持的动力学保真度三条互补维度展开。

Abstract: Protein motif scaffolding benchmarks assess whether generative models can build scaffolds around functionally important residues. Their conclusions depend on which motifs are tested and which criteria define success. Existing benchmarks typically use small hand-curated motif sets and static refolding criteria, but protein function often depends on conformational ensembles and transitions. Here, we address both benchmark scope and success criteria. We introduce BILBY-PRO, a systematically derived benchmark of structurally conserved functional motifs from PROSITE-linked experimental structures, comprising 220 cases from 174 PROSITE motif-pattern entries. Evaluating six recent scaffold-generation models reveals distinct patterns in target coverage and unique-solution yield: RFDiffusion and Proteina solve more targets, whereas ESM3 produces more unique successful scaffolds on the targets it solves. We further introduce BILBY-MD, a reference-based all-atom molecular dynamics evaluation framework. Applied to statically solved designs from the original MotifBench benchmark, it compares motif drift, scaffold drift, motif-contact preservation, and motif-dihedral free-energy landscapes against reference simulations. Of 55 statically solved model-problem pairs, 31 satisfy our MD-supported criterion and only 8 pass all checks. Compact, self-contained motifs are often MD-supported, whereas interface-like or context-dependent motifs fail more frequently. Static refolding success therefore provides useful evidence of local motif recovery but does not establish dynamical fidelity of the full scaffold. BILBY highlights three complementary axes of motif-scaffolding evaluation: target coverage, unique-solution yield, and MD-supported dynamical fidelity.

</details>


### [82] [Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741)
*Qiufu Li,Huibin Xiao,Linlin Shen,Weijia Fan,Xi Jia,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出分类精度反映的是样本级的局部性质，而特征学习追求数据集级的全局性质（类内紧凑、类间可分），据此从第一性原理重新设计BCE损失，并在归一化特征空间中分析BCE与CE损失的最小值及随尺度因子γ变化的收敛行为，揭示分类精度与特征质量随空间尺度的不同权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设"分类精度越高则特征质量越好"，并以训练分类模型作为监督特征学习的主要手段。但作者认为该假设存在偏差：分类精度刻画的是模型在数据集上的样本级局部性质，而特征学习的目标是获得具有良好数据集级全局性质（如类内紧凑性与类间可分性）的特征，二者并不等价，因此需要从特征学习的第一性原理出发重新审视分类损失的构造。

Method: 1）从特征学习的第一性原理出发，重新设计二分类交叉熵（BCE）损失，作为分类中CE损失的对偶形式；2）在归一化特征空间中解析求解BCE与CE损失的最小值，证明二者均可导致神经坍缩（neural collapse），即不同类别的样本特征在最大程度上紧凑且最大程度上分离；3）分析尺度因子γ变化时两种损失的收敛行为，刻画分类精度与特征质量在不同尺度空间中的差异化变化。

Result: 理论分析表明：在固定训练策略下，过大的γ可实现完美分类精度，但会减缓特征向理想几何结构收敛的速度；过小的γ则使分类结果对微小扰动高度敏感；只有适中的γ才能同时获得可靠的分类性能与结构良好的特征。大量实验验证了上述结论，并显示BCE损失在长尾识别与开集识别任务中具有优势，因为它更契合特征学习的全局性要求。

Conclusion: 分类精度并不等价于特征质量，二者分别对应局部与全局性质。BCE损失在损失设计与优化几何上更契合特征学习目标，适中的尺度因子γ是兼顾可靠分类与良好特征结构的关键；BCE在长尾与开集识别中表现更优，说明从特征学习第一性原理重构分类损失是一条有价值的路径。

Abstract: Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $γ$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $γ$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $γ$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $γ$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

</details>


### [83] [Ordering Multivariate Time Series by Volatility Memory: A Temporal Convolutional Approach to Volatility Heredity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07746)
*Kaoutar El Azaar,Mohamed Hamlich,Moncef Garouani,Franck Ravat,Asmae Chakir,Mohamed Tabaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QS-TCN方法，结合波动率持续性估计与时间卷积学习对多变量时间序列进行排序（非因果图识别），在合成波动率系统及Finance、fMRI基准上排序质量优于lead-lag基线及其平方输入变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动率动态蕴含条件均值中不可见的时间关系信息，但各变量的强持续性会掩盖其对他变量的依赖，导致这类信息难以提取。

Method: QS-TCN：用准极大似然（QML）估计波动率记忆的持续性，用Matrix Pencil方法提取时间模态，二者作为网络中的结构化滤波器；与可学习滤波器共同表征目标变量历史，从而支持变量间方向性证据的比较，并汇总为全局优先顺序（作者明确声明该顺序不等同于因果图）。

Result: 在具已知参考关系的线性/非线性波动率交互合成系统及Finance、fMRI基准上评估；四种合成波动率机制下，平均排序质量高于外部lead-lag排序及其平方输入变体，但在两种机制下相对该变体的优势不确定。另对货币、黄金与加密货币市场给出描述性应用结果。

Conclusion: 将统计波动率记忆估计与深度时序学习结合可有效提取持续性掩盖下的方向性时间依赖，提升多变量序列排序质量；但输出为优先顺序而非因果结构，部分情形下增益有限。

Abstract: Volatility dynamics can carry information about temporal relationships that is not apparent in the conditional mean. Extracting this information is difficult because the persistence of each variable can obscure its dependence on other variables. This paper presents QS-TCN, a method for ordering multivariate time series that combines statistical estimates of volatility memory with temporal convolutional learning. Persistence estimates obtained by quasi-maximum likelihood and temporal modes extracted by the Matrix Pencil method define structured filters within the network. Together with learned filters, they represent the history of the target and support comparisons of directional evidence between variables. These comparisons are combined into a global order of precedence, which does not identify a causal graph. The method is evaluated on synthetic systems with linear and nonlinear volatility interactions and on the Finance and fMRI benchmarks, whose reference relations are known. Across the four synthetic volatility regimes studied, QS-TCN reaches a higher mean ordering quality than an external lead--lag ordering and its squared-input adaptation, although the gain over the adaptation is uncertain in two regimes. Applications to currency, gold and cryptocurrency markets are reported descriptively.

</details>


### [84] [ConvPE: Convolutional Position Embedding Improves Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07749)
*Zehao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConvPE 通过对隐状态的低维投影做轻量因果卷积，向注意力分数注入窗口化、内容相关的位置偏置，提升 Transformer 在语言建模、长上下文扩展与视觉任务上的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码（如 RoPE、一维/二维基线）在表达内容相关性与窗口局部性上存在不足，且难以同时兼顾性能与效率；需要一个轻量、可插拔、兼容现有机制的通用位置编码方案。

Method: 对隐状态做低维投影后施加轻量因果卷积，生成窗口化、内容相关（content-dependent）的偏置并加到注意力分数上；作为插件与 RoPE、注意力掩码、KV 拼接兼容；并通过高维卷积支持高维位置嵌入。

Result: 在复现实验中，ConvPE 优于 5 个一维基线与 8 个二维基线；在语言建模、长上下文扩展和视觉任务上均提升性能与效率。

Conclusion: ConvPE 是一种高效、通用、易集成的位置编码插件，可作为现有位置编码的替代或补充；代码已开源（https://github.com/wzhagi/convpe）。

Abstract: ConvPE (Convolutional Position Embedding) improves both the performance and efficiency of Transformer models in language modeling, long-context extension, and vision. It injects a windowed, content-dependent bias into attention scores through a lightweight causal convolution over a low-dimensional projection of the hidden states. As a lightweight plug-in, ConvPE is compatible with RoPE, attention masks, and KV concatenation. It also supports high-dimensional position embeddings via high-dimensional convolutions. In a reproduction, ConvPE outperforms five 1D baselines and eight 2D baselines. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzhagi/convpe.

</details>


### [85] [Foresee to Trade: Forecast-Augmented Policy Optimization for Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07750)
*Zeyu Liu,Xuanzhi Feng,Haoran Xu,Lai Sing Kwong,Yang Li,Hualei Zhang,Hao Chen,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyi Pang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization（FRPO），通过将未来资产机会的监督信号融入账户层面的交易决策强化学习，在五个投资组合设置中取得最高累计与年化收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线投资服务需把资产机会转化为用户账户的投资结果，但同一市场机会对不同账户应引发的操作并不相同，因此需要能同时利用资产层面机会信息与账户层面结果反馈的监督机制。

Method: FRPO通过共享表征实现两条监督路径：Financial State Head（FSH）以未来相对收益为目标，提供资产层面的机会监督；Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization在同一市场路径与相同初始账户条件下比较交易轨迹，提供账户层面的相对结果监督；Adaptive Gate依据预测方差与梯度对齐程度协调辅助监督。FSH仅在训练阶段使用，推理阶段仅用编码器与策略网络进行交易。

Result: 在DJIA30、TOPIX Core30及三种投资风格组合共五个设置上，十随机种子评测显示FRPO在全部设置中取得最高的平均累计收益与年化收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合资产机会监督与交易结果监督可有效强化投资智能体的交易决策。

Abstract: Online investment services must translate asset opportunities into investment outcomes for users' accounts, yet the same market opportunity can warrant different account actions. We study how supervision from future asset opportunities can strengthen account-level trading decisions and propose Forecast-Augmented Relative Policy Optimization (FRPO). FRPO separately supervises asset opportunities and account decisions through a jointly learned representation: a Financial State Head (FSH) learns future relative-return targets to provide asset-level opportunity supervision, while Same-Path Group-Relative Optimization compares trading trajectories under the same market path and initial account to provide account-level relative-outcome supervision. An Adaptive Gate coordinates auxiliary supervision using predictive variance and gradient alignment. FSH is used only during training; inference uses the encoder and policy for trading. Across DJIA30, TOPIX Core30, and three investment-style portfolios, ten-seed evaluations show that FRPO achieves the highest mean cumulative and annualized returns among the compared methods in all five settings. These results support strengthening investment agents' trading decisions through joint supervision from asset opportunities and trading outcomes.

</details>


### [86] [Neural Collapse on Imbalanced Datasets and Open-Set Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07753)
*Qiufu Li,Huibing Xiao,Linlin Shen,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以"类平均损失"上的优化问题为出发点，从理论上重新刻画了不平衡数据集与开集设定下的神经坍缩（NC）几何结构，并证明按样本频率重平衡的对比损失在其极小值处同样呈现标准NC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经坍缩理论建立在全局平均损失之上，而类别不平衡会扭曲标准NC；同时现有理论未覆盖开集中"无训练样本但仍有分类器向量"的类别，因此需要在不平衡与开集条件下重新建立NC的理论表述。

Method: 将分析建立在类平均（class-wise averaged）损失而非全局平均损失之上的优化问题；分别针对交叉熵（CE）与二元交叉熵（BCE）推导极小值处的几何性质；进一步构造受开集启发的场景（部分类别无训练样本但仍被赋予分类器向量）；最后分析按样本频率倒数重平衡的 Softmax 与 Sigmoid 对比损失。

Result: 证明当类平均损失达到极小值时，无论平衡或不平衡数据集，标准NC均成立（CE与BCE皆然）；在开集场景下，已知类仍呈现标准NC，而未知类的分类器向量相互重合且与已知类向量正交；重平衡的 Softmax/Sigmoid 对比损失在其极小值处，不同类别样本特征同样形成标准NC。

Conclusion: 神经坍缩并非仅限平衡闭集设定：在类平均损失意义下，标准NC在不平衡与开集条件中依然成立，且对重平衡对比损失普遍适用，从而将NC理论推广到更现实的训练分布。

Abstract: Neural collapse (NC) describes the elegant geometric structure that sample features and classifier vectors exhibit when a classification model's loss reaches its minimum. Class imbalance, however, generally distorts standard NC. In this paper, we theoretically reformulate NC on imbalanced datasets and in open-set settings. Our analysis is based on an optimization problem over class-wise averaged losses, rather than the global averaged losses used in prior NC studies. We prove that when class-wise averaged losses attain their minima, standard NC is achieved on balanced or imbalanced datasets, for both cross-entropy (CE) and binary cross-entropy (BCE) losses. We further extend the analysis to an open-set-inspired scenario, where some classes contain no training samples yet are still assigned classifier vectors in the losses. We find that in this scenario, when class-wise averaged losses reach their minima, standard NC still emerges on the known classes with training samples, while the classifier vectors of unknown classes without training samples overlap with each other and become orthogonal to those of the known classes. Finally, we analyze Softmax- and Sigmoid-based contrastive losses re-balanced by inverse sample frequency, proving that at their minima on balanced or imbalanced datasets, sample features of different classes likewise form standard NC.

</details>


### [87] [Scaling Laws for SAE Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07756)
*Nikita Koriagin,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了固定训练预算下SAE所需唯一激活数量的权衡：质量随缓冲增长快速提升后进入平台期，此后重复回放几乎与全程使用新激活等效；给出有限数据的缩放律与实用训练规则，可减少8–64倍激活存储且质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）训练中激活存储常是瓶颈。作者希望回答：在总训练预算固定时，SAE究竟需要多少条唯一激活？缓冲规模与质量的关系如何，是否必须让缓冲随训练预算线性增长。

Method: 定义"多样性—重复"权衡，提出针对有限数据的缩放律（scaling law）刻画质量随唯一激活数的变化；在多种字典大小与token预算、Llama-3.1早/中/晚期层、不同Qwen3规模、BatchTopK/TopK/JumpReLU三类SAE上验证；并用数学文本与Python代码的扫描实验检验激活来源变化的影响。

Result: 质量随缓冲增大先快速提升后进入平台期，平台后回放缓冲与持续使用新激活效果几乎相同，额外唯一激活对重构与可解释性收益很小。评估涵盖解释方差（EV）、重构MSE、特征吸收、自动可解释性与稀疏探针。实验中激活存储降低8–64倍而质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 给出实用训练规则：选择能达到质量平台的最小缓冲，并在剩余预算中重复回放该缓冲，无需让缓冲随训练预算增长；该结论跨字典规模、模型层、模型规模、SAE变体与激活来源均成立。

Abstract: Activation storage is often a bottleneck in sparse autoencoder (SAE) training. We study how many unique activations an SAE needs when its total training budget is fixed. Quality improves rapidly as the buffer grows, then reaches a plateau where replaying the buffer is nearly as effective as using fresh activations throughout training. Beyond this point, additional unique activations bring little benefit to reconstruction or interpretability, so the buffer need not grow with the training budget. We describe this tradeoff between diversity and repetition with a scaling law for limited data. We validate it across dictionary sizes and token budgets, early, middle, and late layers of Llama-3.1, Qwen3 model sizes, and BatchTopK, TopK, and JumpReLU SAEs. Sweeps using mathematical text and Python code test the effect of changing the activation source. Evaluation covers explained variance (EV), reconstruction MSE, feature absorption, automated interpretability, and sparse probing. These results give a practical training rule: choose the smallest buffer that reaches the quality plateau and replay it for the remaining budget. In our experiments, this reduces activation storage by $8\text{--}64\times$ with negligible quality loss.

</details>


### [88] [Optimal Learning in Games with Arbitrary Delays: Best-of-Both-Worlds for Self-Play and Adversarial Opponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07773)
*Emmanuel Esposito,Andrew Jacobsen,Hao Qiu,Alexander Ryabchenko,Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAO-OMD算法，在带任意未知延迟的零和博弈中同时实现自博弈的光滑博弈快速率与对抗任意对手的极小极大最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带延迟反馈的零和博弈学习问题中，现有方法难以在同一算法里兼顾两类保证：自博弈下光滑博弈的快速收敛率，以及对抗任意对手时的极小极大最优个体遗憾。作者还指出标准"黑盒归约到无延迟设定"无法同时获得这两种最优率。

Method: 提出Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent（MAO-OMD），即多锚点乐观在线镜像下降。以σ_max（最大未完成梯度数）刻画延迟结构，在双方均使用该算法时分析社会遗憾与个体遗憾，并单独分析与任意对手对抗时的个体遗憾；同时证明黑盒归约路线的不可行性，并将结果推广到多人博弈及玩家相关延迟序列。

Result: 在带任意未知延迟的光滑零和博弈中，双方使用MAO-OMD可获得O(σ_max+1)的社会遗憾与个体遗憾；对抗任意对手时保证极小极大最优的O(√(T+d_tot))个体遗憾，其中d_tot为总延迟。同时证明这些最优率无法通过标准黑盒归约同时达到。

Conclusion: MAO-OMD以单一算法统一了延迟反馈零和博弈中的自博弈快速率与对抗鲁棒性，突破了黑盒归约的局限，并自然扩展到多人博弈与玩家相关延迟情形。

Abstract: We study learning in zero-sum games with delayed feedback. We introduce Multi-Anchor Optimistic Online Mirror Descent (MAO-OMD), a single algorithm which obtains optimal fast rates in self-play on smooth games, while simultaneously maintaining minimax optimal guarantees against arbitrary opponents. In particular, in smooth zero-sum games with arbitrary and unknown feedback delays, MAO-OMD obtains $O(σ_{\max}+1)$ social and individual regrets when used by both players, where $σ_{\max}$ denotes the maximum number of outstanding gradients, while also guaranteeing minimax optimal $O(\sqrt{T+d_{\mathrm{tot}}})$ individual regret against arbitrary opponents, where $d_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total delay. Moreover, we show that these optimal rates cannot be obtained simultaneously using the standard black-box reduction to the undelayed setting. We also extend our results to multi-player games, and to games with player-dependent delay sequences.

</details>


### [89] [Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775)
*Moritz Grävinghoff,Konstantin Wehmeyer,Aron Asefaw,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LoRA适配器视为一种"数据模态"，用Transformer自编码器学习其非线性潜空间，验证其在采样、属性编辑上的能力保持与更强的位移鲁棒性，并扩展到混合秩适配器的统一潜空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作发现LoRA适配器群体位于低维流形上，且线性PCA基已足以支撑采样、编辑、反演等操作。作者追问：在这种情况下，一个"学习得到的非线性潜空间"相比线性基还能带来什么额外价值；同时指出固定维度的权重空间表示无法原生容纳不同秩的适配器，限制了其作为通用模态的可能。

Method: 在同一批LoRA身份适配器群体上训练Transformer自编码器，将每个适配器映射到潜空间；对每项操作（采样、单属性编辑、位移/扰动等）与直接作用于原始适配器权重的同一操作进行对照评估；进一步将序列自编码器扩展为可在单一共享潜空间内表示混合秩适配器。

Result: 潜空间保留了权重空间中已有的能力：可采样出多样化的身份、可编辑单个属性；并额外表现出对位移（displacement/扰动）的更强鲁棒性——退化后仍生成扭曲但有效的面部，而非纹理噪声。扩展后的序列自编码器能够在同一潜空间内承载混合秩适配器。

Conclusion: 将LoRA适配器权重视为独立的数据模态是可行的；学习到的非线性潜空间不仅保留线性权重空间的操作能力，还在鲁棒性和跨秩泛化上占优。该工作为把不同架构/秩的适配器权重统一为单一通用模态提供了路径，并指向用非线性潜空间承载异构适配器群体的进一步推广。

Abstract: LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

</details>


### [90] [PBPO: Prefix-Block Policy Optimization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07783)
*Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Si Zhang,Hanqing Zeng,Tianxin Wei,Chenglin Wei,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Prefix-Block Policy Optimization (PBPO)，通过前缀块状态与单次模型评估构造块似然的因子化单步代理，实现块级策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 以"块内并行去噪、块间自回归"方式解码，块级策略优化是自然选择；但完成块的精确似然需对中间状态与揭示顺序边缘化，难以处理。

Method: 构造 prefix-block state，用单次模型评估定义因子化一步代理似然；块代理比为各 token 代理比之积，但裁剪与重要性加权分别施加于每个 token；另通过对 prompt 与已揭示前缀的 corrupted view 重采样来增强重复更新的多样性。

Result: 理论分析表明块乘积加权相对 token 局部加权会放大梯度贡献方差；在两个 LLaDA 家族初始化、六个基准、三种生成长度上，PBPO 与现有 dLLM RL 方法性能相当。

Conclusion: PBPO 在保持块级条件的同时实现了可行且有效的块级策略优化，为 dLLM 的强化学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) commonly decode responses by proceeding autoregressively across blocks while iteratively denoising multiple tokens within each block. This structure makes block-level policy optimization a natural adaptation of autoregressive policy optimization. However, the exact likelihood of a completed block is intractable because it marginalizes over the intermediate states and reveal orders of the denoising process. To realize the block-level formulation, we construct a prefix-block state and use a single model evaluation to define a factorized one-step surrogate for the block likelihood. The resulting block surrogate ratio is a product of its token surrogate ratios, allowing variation at individual positions to propagate across the entire block before clipping. We therefore introduce Prefix-Block Policy Optimization (PBPO), which preserves block-level conditioning but applies surrogate importance weighting and clipping separately to each token. PBPO further diversifies repeated updates by resampling corrupted views of the prompt and revealed prefix, a design motivated by masked-dLLM pre-training. Under our analysis assumptions, block-product weighting amplifies the variance of gradient contributions relative to token-local weighting. Across two LLaDA-family initializations, six benchmarks, and three generation lengths, PBPO achieves competitive performance against existing dLLM RL methods.

</details>


### [91] [EnergyBench: a live, gate-aligned benchmark of day-ahead forecasts for European power markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07784)
*Evgenii Genov,Ander Martinez Alonso,Christoph Bergmeir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出已上线运行的实时（live）基准 EnergyBench：每日在日前市场闭市前 30 分钟，在统一冻结信息集上由基准自身运行模型，预测次日 15 分钟粒度的电价、风、光与负荷，并用 Bradley-Terry/Elo 评分排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源预测模型依赖回测与固定公开测试集：回测可能使用预测时点尚不存在的信息（信息泄漏），固定测试集被反复复用甚至进入基础模型预训练数据，导致对基准的过拟合。需要一个不可被预先优化的实时评测平台。

Method: EnergyBench 每日 11:30 CET/CEST（SDAC 日前关门 30 分钟前）自行运行所有参赛模型，在截止时刻冻结并公开同一信息集（含三套数值天气预报），覆盖六个竞价区（BE、NL、FR、DE-LU、DK1、DK2）次日 15 分钟网格上的电价、风电、光伏与负荷预测；支持基础模型在同天气协变量下有无对照。误差按序列对季节朴素基线做池化，用 Elo 尺度上的 Bradley-Terry 评分排名并给出按日 bootstrap 区间。

Result: 参赛对象包括基线与线性/梯度提升模型、11 个家族共 18 个时间序列基础模型、集成模型及电网运营商自有预测。前 32 个实况日中，协变量使 10 个基础模型在配对比赛中误差降低 37–51%（风电 64–73%，光伏 48–61%）；零样本使用即匹敌或优于同协变量的 LightGBM；参数规模（3M–331M）影响小得多；协变量模型的中位数集成排名最高。电网运营商的关门前预测在风、光上仍领先，最佳单模型误差高 16–22%；负荷差距 4–6%，落在区间内。

Conclusion: 实时、自运行、信息集冻结的评测范式消除了回测泄漏与测试集复用带来的基准过拟合；结果表明天气协变量而非模型规模是性能主因，零样本基础模型已可与专用梯度提升模型竞争，但专业电网预测在波动性高的风、光上仍具优势。

Abstract: Energy forecasting models are usually compared on backtests and fixed public datasets. Backtests can use data that did not yet exist when the forecast would have been made, and a fixed test set, reused across studies and possibly contained in the pretraining data of foundation models, invites overfitting to the benchmark. EnergyBench evaluates every entry live instead. Each day at 11:30 CET/CEST, thirty minutes before the day-ahead (SDAC) gate closes, every model forecasts the next day's price, wind and solar generation and load on the 15-minute grid for six bidding zones (BE, NL, FR, DE-LU, DK1, DK2). Unlike existing live benchmarks, EnergyBench runs the models itself, on one information set that it freezes at the cut-off and publishes, including three numerical weather prediction models, so that foundation models can be compared with and without the same weather covariates under the actual market deadline. Errors are pooled per series against a seasonal-naive baseline, and models are ranked by a Bradley-Terry rating on the Elo scale with a day-bootstrap interval. The field spans baselines, linear and gradient-boosted models on the frozen weather, eighteen time-series foundation models from eleven families, ensembles, and the grid operators' own forecasts. Over the first thirty-two live days, the covariates reduced the error of each of the ten foundation models run with and without them by 37-51% on matched races (64-73% on wind, 48-61% on solar). Used zero-shot, these models match or outperform a LightGBM model trained on the same covariates. Model size, from 3M to 331M parameters, has a much smaller effect, and median ensembles of the covariate models rank highest. The grid operators' pre-gate forecasts remain ahead on wind and solar, where the best single foundation models have 16-22% more error; on load the gap is 4-6%, within the intervals.

</details>


### [92] [Computation, not weight access: large language models as auditors of clinical prediction networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07847)
*Zhiyu Liu,Xinyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过预注册的模拟实验分离"权重读取"与"代码执行"两种条件，证明LLM审计临床预测模型的成败取决于计算能力与提示引导，而非权重可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM据称能读取、编辑、写入小型神经网络权重，从而有望仅凭模型文件审计临床预测模型；但此前的公开演示混淆了直接读取原始权重与运行代码两种因素，其真实能力边界尚不明确。

Method: 采用内部预注册的模拟实验，训练含105至38,401个权重的Tanh网络，数据为模拟的蛛网膜下腔出血患者队列，分别植入真实关系或无关系，另训练一个在亚组中低估死亡的偏倚网络。由GPT-6 Astra及另外四个GPT模型在默认或显式系统指令下回答单一提示词，设置三种条件：无工具且权重置于提示中；计算专用工具暴露权重（白盒）；仅暴露预测值（黑盒）。

Result: 无工具时，Astra可恢复353权重网络中的植入关系，但在3,457权重时可靠性下降；默认指令下它在12项审计与修复任务中拒绝11项（称不可识别），显式指令消除了这些拒绝。使用工具后，在最多38,401权重的网络上29次运行均恢复植入关系，15次零假设网络运行无假警报，6/6次识别出偏倚亚组。黑盒与白盒表现相当，其他GPT-6模型读取同样准确；人工设计脚本在审计任务上匹配对照模型，修复任务则匹配最佳LLM。

Conclusion: 对此类网络的审计而言，决定性能的关键是计算与引导（elicitation），而非权重访问权限；工具化计算可突破规模瓶颈，提示指令可消除不当拒答。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have been reported to read, edit and write the weights of small neural networks, raising the prospect of auditing clinical prediction models from their files alone. Public demonstrations, however, mixed reading raw weights with running code. We separated these conditions in internally pre-specified simulation experiments. Tanh networks of 105 to 38,401 weights were trained on simulated cohorts of patients with subarachnoid haemorrhage, each with a planted relationship or none, and one network was trained on labels that under-recorded deaths in a subgroup. GPT-6 Astra and four other GPT models answered single prompts under a default or an explicit system instruction, either with the weights in the prompt and no tools, or with a calculation-only tool that exposed the weights (white box) or only the network's predictions (black box). Without tools, Astra recovered planted relationships in 353-weight networks but became unreliable at 3,457 weights, and under the default instruction it declined 11 of 12 audit and repair tasks as unidentifiable; the explicit instruction removed these refusals. With the tool, it recovered the planted relationship in all 29 runs on networks of up to 38,401 weights, raised no false alarm in 15 runs on null networks, and found the biased subgroup in 6 of 6 runs. Black-box access performed as well as white-box access, and other GPT-6 models read as accurately. A well-designed script matched the comparator models on the audit and the best LLM repair. For auditing these networks, what mattered was computation and elicitation, not access to the weights.

</details>


### [93] [When, Where, How: Active In-Context Learning for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07886)
*Christian M. M. Frey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结的表格基础模型（TFM）在主动上下文学习中，随着上下文增加，多样性、预测不确定性与目标感知信息增益的价值如何变化，提出TANN与EPIC两种采集规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFM仅通过上下文中的有标签行适应新任务，而真实场景中标签获取昂贵（需高度专业领域知识）。经典主动学习规则在此设置下需随每次新增标签而更新引导采集的模型，因此需厘清各类采集准则在TFM上下文增长过程中的相对价值。

Method: 提出目标感知最近邻（TANN）采集与上下文期望预测信息增益（EPIC），并与经典规则（如多样性、预测不确定性）对比；在20个基准上使用两种成熟TFM与经典GBDT进行评估。

Result: 三点发现：(i) 何时——基于多样性的采集在最初几轮迭代最优；(ii) 何处——通过简单的目标感知最近邻规则标注服务于目标输入的候选池行，效果显著；(iii) 如何——EPIC在标签预算下得到最佳模型，在八个最大池的基准上仅用5–10%的池标签即保留平均80–87%的经机会校正的全数据性能，且所选标签也能提升GBDT的预测性能。

Conclusion: 主动上下文学习中采集准则的价值随TFM上下文增长而变化：早期宜用多样性，目标感知选择至关重要，EPIC在有限预算下综合表现最佳，且其选出的标签具有跨模型迁移价值。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) adapt to a new task only through the labeled rows in their context. However, labels are expensive to acquire in real-world scenarios due to the need for highly specific domain knowledge to ensure high-quality data. Active in-context learning transfers classical acquisition rules to this setting, in which the model that guides acquisition changes with every label added. We ask how the value of diversity, predictive uncertainty and target-aware information gain changes as a frozen TFM acquires more context. For that, we study Target-Aware Nearest Neighbor (TANN) acquisition and the Expected Predictive Information Gain in Context (EPIC) and evaluate them against classical rules. Our study across twenty benchmarks with two well-established TFMs and classical GBDTs highlights three findings: (i) When: diversity-based acquisition is best during the first acquisition iterations; (ii) Where: labeling pool rows that serve the target inputs via a simple target-aware nearest-neighbor rule matters substantially; (iii) How: EPIC yields the best models at the label budget, retains on average 80--87% of the chance-corrected full-data performance with only 5-10% of the pool labeled on the eight benchmarks with the largest pools, and selects labels that also improve the predictive performance of gradient-boosted decision trees.

</details>


### [94] [DISCERN: Distributional Synthetic Replay for Retain-Free Incremental Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07892)
*Nicolás Segura-Kunsagi,Juan Luis Suárez-Díaz,Iván Sevillano-García,Francisco Herrera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISCERN用“按类拟合参数分布采样合成嵌入”替代retain集，实现免保留集的增量类遗忘，并通过分布族选择适配不同骨干的潜空间几何，在ViT上显著提升累积遗忘的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中删除请求随时间到达，需要增量类遗忘（ICU）：顺序删除类、已遗忘知识不得复活、剩余类性能需保持。同时隐私约束下往往无法访问retain集。此外，原型回放假设各类围绕质心的各向同性高斯，与transformer嵌入近似位于超球面、聚集于类方向的实际几何不符。

Method: 提出DISCERN，一种基于干预的类遗忘方法：以各类参数分布采样的合成嵌入替代retain集。对每类拟合欧氏族（Gaussian、Laplace、Student's t）与方向族（von Mises–Fisher、Fisher–Bingham），通过统计检验选择分布族；若均不被接受，则用交叉验证对数似然选择。在MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100上，配2个卷积骨干与2个transformer骨干，与7个免retain基线在单类、多类、增量遗忘设置下对比，并做配对消融。

Result: 一次性删除多类时DISCERN具竞争力；在transformer的ICU场景中最稳定：ViT-B/16上20次顺序删除后调和均值保持0.797，而同时设置下最强方法从0.918跌至0.048。配对消融显示按类分布选择显著减少已遗忘类的“复活”，其作用是稳定累积遗忘而非普遍性提升。

Conclusion: DISCERN表明，用架构自适应的参数分布合成回放可有效替代retain集，并在增量类遗忘中抑制遗忘知识的复活；其收益主要体现为对累积遗忘的稳定性，而非整体精度提升，分布族选择是关键稳定器。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. In real deployments, however, deletion requests arrive over time, calling for incremental class unlearning (ICU): classes are removed sequentially, previously forgotten knowledge must remain forgotten, and the remaining classes must be preserved. We present DIstributional SynthetiC rEplay for Retain-free iNcremental unlearning (DISCERN), an intervention-based class unlearning method that replaces access to the retain set with synthetic embeddings sampled from per-class parametric distributions. Unlike prototype-based replay, which assumes an isotropic Gaussian around each class centroid, DISCERN fits Euclidean (Gaussian, Laplace, Student's $t$) and directional (von Mises--Fisher, Fisher--Bingham) families to each class and selects among them through statistical tests and, when none of them accepts, cross-validated log-likelihood. This design is motivated by the architecture-dependent latent geometry: transformer embeddings lie approximately on a hypersphere and concentrate around class directions, unlike those of convolutional networks. We evaluate DISCERN against seven retain-free baselines on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 with two convolutional and two transformer backbones, under single-class, multi-class, and incremental unlearning. DISCERN is competitive when classes are removed at once and is the most stable method in ICU on transformers: on ViT-B/16, after 20 sequential deletions it retains a harmonic mean of $0.797$, whereas the strongest method in the simultaneous setting drops from $0.918$ to $0.048$. A paired ablation shows that per-class distribution selection substantially reduces the resurfacing of previously forgotten classes, acting as a stabilizer of accumulated forgetting rather than a uniform improvement.

</details>


### [95] [Same Answer, Different Future: Matched Predictions Do Not Imply Matched Belief Updating in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07913)
*Weihan Li,Junhao Wu,Xinlei Chen,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 相同预测概率不足以判定信念状态等价；证据格式决定哪类读出反映其内容，且不同途径所得信念的"有效证据权重"差异显著，后续更新动力学取决于信念的获取方式与读出方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可通过观察结果、阅读摘要、接收指令或激活干预等途径产生相似的概率预测，但当前预测的一致并不能说明模型面对后续证据时会如何反应，即这些途径是否诱导了等价的信念状态尚不清楚。

Method: 构建带解析贝叶斯参照的Bernoulli推断任务；在三个开放权重模型上，通过四种文本途径与两种激活层干预准备状态；同时测量下一结果续写与显式概率估计，并从每个预备状态追加相同的新证据；用"有效证据权重"（该初始信念相当于多少次新观测）量化信念的持续性，并进行单次steering实验。

Result: 证据格式决定内容由何种读出表达：结果序列主要影响续写，摘要与陈述比率主要影响显式估计；各途径有效权重差异巨大，摘要的权重随其陈述观测数增长很弱，诱发问题可使推断权重变化达数个数量级；在单次steering设置中，能复现自然记录即时预测的干预在后续证据下大部分效应消失。

Conclusion: 当前读出的一致性不足以确立信念更新的等价性；信念的获取方式与读出方式共同决定其后续动力学，因此评估模型"信念"需区分途径并检验其对后续证据的响应，而非仅比较单点预测。

Abstract: Language models can produce similar probabilistic predictions after observing outcomes, reading summaries, receiving instructions, or undergoing activation interventions. Whether these routes induce equivalent belief states remains unclear: agreement in current predictions does not establish how a model will respond to subsequent evidence. We investigate this distinction in a Bernoulli inference task with an analytic Bayesian reference. Across three open-weight language models, we prepare states through four textual routes and two activation-level interventions, measure both next-outcome continuations and explicit probabilistic estimates, and append identical new evidence from each prepared state. We quantify the persistence of the initial belief through its effective evidence weight, the number of new observations it is worth. We find that the format of evidence decides which readouts express its content: outcome sequences primarily affect continuations, whereas summaries and stated rates primarily affect explicit estimates. Moreover, effective weights differ substantially across routes, with summaries receiving weights that scale weakly with their stated observation counts and elicitation questions changing inferred weights by orders of magnitude. In a one-shot steering setting, an intervention that reproduces a natural record's immediate prediction loses most of its effect under subsequent evidence. These results show that agreement in current readouts is insufficient to establish equivalence in belief updating: how a belief is acquired and how it is read jointly determine its subsequent dynamics.

</details>


### [96] [Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929)
*Fuma Kimishima,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FluxC——以 Flux 为压缩先验的零样本编解码器，并系统分析编码路径、重建方式与低码率失真对率-失真-感知行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 DiffC 证明预训练扩散模型可作零样本编解码器，但尚不清楚编码路径选择与重建方法如何影响率-失真-感知权衡，本文由此展开分析。

Method: 沿三个维度分析 FluxC：(1) 编码代价与比特分配；(2) 重建方式（祖先采样、单步 ODE、多步 ODE）；(3) 低码率下的失真。在匹配 SNR 条件下考察路径选择对理想信息率与比特时间分配的作用，并在高斯源与自然图像上验证。

Result: 匹配 SNR 时，路径选择不改变理想信息率，但改变比特在各时间步上的分配；ODE 重建（尤其多步）比祖先采样失真更低，低码率区间优势更明显；实验在高斯源与自然图像上验证了该预测，FluxC 与近期神经图像压缩方法竞争力相当，部分情形下更优。

Conclusion: Flux 可作为有原理依据的零样本编解码器；ODE 式重建是低码率下降低失真的有效选择，编码路径主要影响比特分配而非理想信息率。

Abstract: We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. We analyze FluxC along three axes: coding cost and bit allocation, reconstruction method, and low rate distortion. At matched signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that the path choice does not affect the ideal information rate, but changes how bits are allocated across time steps. For reconstruction, we compare ancestral sampling, one-step ODE reconstruction, and many-step ODE reconstruction. Our analysis shows that ODE-based reconstruction can achieve lower distortion than ancestral sampling, especially in the low rate regime. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

</details>


### [97] [AttnSR: Attention Step Reward for Long-Horizon Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07932)
*Mengsong Wu,Tianchen Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhenhua Liu,Zhe Cui,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AttnSR，一种无需额外奖励模型或额外采样的步级奖励增强方法，利用训练回放中策略注意力回溯估计各步对最终回答的贡献，从而为长程LLM智能体提供步级信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体需进行多步决策，但仅基于结果奖励的强化学习难以为单步提供直接监督，存在步级信用分配监督不足的问题。

Method: AttnSR从训练回放时提取的策略注意力构建奖励分配：沿策略模型的注意力图反向追踪，估计早期步骤与最终响应的相关性；以终局步（相对滑动基线衡量）决定这些奖励是强化还是惩罚所选步骤；不依赖单独训练的奖励模型，也不增加额外rollout。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的受控实验中，ALFWorld上所有评估检查点均优于PPO基线，最终留出成功率由91.2%提升至97.1%；消融表明收益取决于中间奖励的分配方式而非步级奖励的简单累积；ScienceWorld在每个检查点均有提升，且环境交互减少29.9%。

Conclusion: 策略注意力可作为长程智能体强化学习中步级奖励分配的实用先验，AttnSR为步级信用分配提供了有效且低开销的方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents make multi-step decisions, yet outcome-reward-only reinforcement learning provides little direct supervision for individual steps. We introduce AttnSR, a step-level reward augmentation method that derives reward allocations from policy attention extracted during training replay, without a separately trained reward model or additional rollouts. AttnSR traces backward through the policy model attention graph to estimate the relevance of earlier steps to the final response. The terminal step, measured relative to a running baseline, determines whether these rewards reinforce or penalize the selected steps. In a controlled Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct study, AttnSR consistently outperforms the PPO baseline across all evaluated checkpoints on ALFWorld, raising final held-out success from 91.2% to 97.1%. The ablation experiment shows that the benefit depends on how intermediate rewards are assigned, rather than simple accumulation of step rewards over time. AttnSR also improves ScienceWorld at every checkpoint while cutting environment interactions by 29.9%. These results support policy attention as a practical prior for step-level reward allocation in long-horizon agentic RL. Code is available at https://github.com/fairyshine/AttnSR .

</details>


### [98] [The Blindfolded Controller: Decision Model-Guided Training for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07934)
*Muhammad Ibrahim Khan,Aniket Dixit,Faizan Ahmed,James Brusey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DMGT方法，用决策模型（DM）基于环境与任务描述初始化强化学习（RL）策略，使智能体首轮即达目标并获得奖励，再由RL在奖励驱动下持续改进，从而兼顾DM免训练可行动与RL可优化的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在稀疏奖励与探索困难任务中从零学习慢且样本效率低；DM无需训练即可在稀疏奖励任务上表现良好，但不从奖励中学习，无法逼近最优解。二者优势互补。

Method: DMGT：给定环境与任务的自然语言描述，让DM生成行为以初始化RL策略（替代从零探索），随后由RL基于环境奖励继续训练优化策略；并通过消融实验总结向DM描述环境与任务的规范。

Result: 在七个任务上，DMGT全部优于纯RL，并在其中五个任务上优于DM。

Conclusion: 以DM初始化RL可实现"免训练起步＋奖励驱动改进"，有效缓解稀疏奖励与探索难题；同时给出了向DM描述环境与任务的具体指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a framework for continuous improvement through interaction with an environment. However, RL agents struggle against tasks that offer exploration challenges and sparse rewards, because learning efficient policies from scratch is slow and sample-inefficient. In contrast, decision models (DMs), given a description of the environment and task, can perform well in sparse-reward tasks, but, as they do not learn from reward, they are not able to improve towards the optimal solution. In this paper, we present Decision Model-Guided Training (DMGT), a technique that uses a DM with a description of the environment and task to initialise an RL policy, combining the DM's ability to act without training with RL's ability to improve from reward. Instead of exploring from scratch, the agent reaches the goal and receives reward from its first episodes, and RL then improves its policy beyond what the DM achieves. We demonstrate through experiments on seven tasks that our approach outperforms RL in all seven and DM in five. In addition, we provide comprehensive guidelines, derived from ablation studies, for describing an environment and task to a DM.

</details>


### [99] [Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950)
*Dang-Khoa Bui,Quang-Huy Tran,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui,Duc-Tuan Ngo,Thanh-Trong Tran,Tuan-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对MIMIC-CXR+EHR多标签分类基准的审计发现：record-level切分导致表观性能被标签复制与EHR向量复制造成的泄漏严重高估；改用患者分离切分、与入院对齐并经规则修正的标签后，原基准的结论（不平衡策略收益、树集成优于逻辑回归、EHR变量有效）均不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态CXR+EHR类不平衡基准多在"记录级"划分上评估，但同一患者贡献多张影像，导致评估存在泄漏风险；作者希望系统审计此类基准的可信度，并厘清标签与EHR变量的真实信息量。

Method: 在MIMIC-CXR的29,083张影像、10,479名患者、8个诊断目标上：(1) 复现记录级划分基准，并用"查找表"（复制EHR向量相同的开发集影像标签）作泄漏探针；(2) 回溯MIMIC-IV，核查标签来源（单次入院的诊断码关键词匹配、按患者复制到全部影像、每患者仅一个EHR向量）；(3) 建立患者分离评估协议，采用入院对齐与规则修正标签；(4) 比较类不平衡策略、调优树集成vs逻辑回归、发布版EHR变量、影像时点可得诊断史与影像嵌入（DenseNet、CheXpert编码器、RAD-DINO）的表现；并做F2/AUROC显著性检验；同时核查预训练数据重叠（RAD-DINO自监督预训练见过96.6%影像）。

Result: 记录级基准具误导性：EHR向量查找表几乎追平最佳模型；在未见患者上所有模型退化到"全部预测为阳性"的水平。标签层面：约三分之一影像的标签与其自身入院诊断码不一致。患者分离+修正标签下：无任何不平衡策略使宏F2相对无增强提升超过0.01；调优树集成相对调优逻辑回归无稳定优势；发布版EHR变量停留在全阳性基线水平，而影像时点可得诊断史与影像嵌入超过该基线（含未见过这些影像的CheXpert编码器）；将任一编码器加入诊断史均无显著F2增益；探索性AUROC增益在RAD-DINO显著、DenseNet不显著。

Conclusion: 多模态CXR+EHR的类不平衡基准结论易受记录级切分、标签传播与EHR复制造成的泄漏污染而失真；应使用患者分离、入院对齐、规则修正的标签与泄漏感知评估协议，并核查预训练数据重叠。论文提供可复用的诊断工具、泄漏感知协议与检查清单。

Abstract: Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

</details>


### [100] [Invariance before structure: two modes of emergent compression in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07952)
*Hardik Rajpal,Dan Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EARL框架，将学习表征中的"涌现"区分为状态空间压缩与参数空间压缩，并用Fisher敏感性及集合级信息论度量加以形式化；在带训练瓶颈的回声状态网络中发现二者分阶段出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人工神经网络中学习到的表征呈现显著的跨基质不变性，且这种不变性在漂移、异质性和初始化差异下依然成立；作者希望理解这种不变性从何而来，即表征"涌现"的内在机制。

Method: 提出Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning（EARL）框架，区分两种涌现模式：(1) 状态空间压缩——表征对其自身未来获得超越单个单元独立预测能力的预测力；(2) 参数空间压缩——微观参数不同的网络收敛到相同表征。二者分别形式化为Fisher敏感性，并引入集合级信息论度量；在带训练瓶颈的循环储层（reservoir）上进行实证。

Result: 参数空间压缩在训练最初几个epoch内即上升，且部分独立于具体任务；状态空间压缩约晚30个epoch才出现，且仅在网络开始学到任务的时间结构时发生。

Conclusion: 对基质的不变性（参数空间压缩）与任务特定的涌现结构（状态空间压缩）是在学习的不同阶段获得的，二者可解耦。

Abstract: Learned representations show striking invariance across biological and artificial neural networks, with different substrates and variations driven by drift, heterogeneity and initialisation. To understand this invariance, we propose Emergent Adaptive Representation Learning (EARL), a framework that distinguishes two modes of emergence in learned representations: state-space compression, where a learned representation gains predictive power over its own future beyond what the network's units can predict individually, and parameter-space compression, where networks with different micro-level parameters converge to the same representation. We formalise both as Fisher sensitivities and introduce ensemble-level information-theoretic measures for each. In a recurrent reservoir with a trained bottleneck, parameter-space compression rises within the first few epochs of training and partly independently of the task, whereas state-space compression rises about 30 epochs later and only when the network begins to learn the temporal structure of the task. These results suggest that invariance to the substrate and task-specific emergent structure are acquired in distinct phases of learning.

</details>


### [101] [CoRD: Coupled Rollouts Fill the Blind Spot of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07959)
*Guhan Chen,Bohan Li,Jun Rao,Hejin Wang,Songtao Tian,Yexin Xie,Xiaojun Meng,Peng Yuan,Jiansheng Wei,Xuebo Liu,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoRD（耦合 rollout 蒸馏），在保留on-policy损失的同时，通过推测解码的最大耦合对学生与教师分布做几何插值来采样训练状态，使训练状态分布偏向教师且干预稀疏；在Qwen3/Qwen3.5六个学生模型上，数学基准pass@1提升0.9–2.0分、pass@8提升1.2–3.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏只用学生自身rollout采样，教师在自身轨迹上会到达、但学生不会到达的状态几乎得不到监督，导致训练状态覆盖不足。

Method: CoRD保留原有on-policy损失，仅改变训练状态的分布：利用推测解码的最大耦合（maximal coupling）在学生与教师分布间做几何插值采样。当两分布一致时保留学生token，否则从教师相对学生低估计的目标部分替换一个token，保证干预频率达到精确采样器下界，并随学生能力提升而自然减少。

Result: 在Qwen3与Qwen3.5两个模型族、0.6B–4B的六个基座学生上，等步数等数据条件下，CoRD相对on-policy蒸馏在五个公开数学基准上平均pass@1提升0.9–2.0分、pass@8提升1.2–3.4分；在冻结的教师可达状态上，4B学生用CoRD 16步达到的差距收敛幅度，on-policy蒸馏80步仍未达到；其他方向同等或更高干预频率的替换规则均不及CoRD。

Conclusion: 以最小必要的分布干预将训练状态分布向教师偏移，是一种有效且干预可渐退的蒸馏状态分布修正方案，优于单纯on-policy蒸馏与更强干预的替换规则。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a student on its own rollouts, so states the teacher would reach but the student does not are seldom supervised. We introduce CoRD (coupled-rollout distillation), which keeps the on-policy loss and moves only the distribution of training states toward the teacher, drawing rollouts from a geometric interpolation of student and teacher through the maximal coupling of speculative decoding. The coupling keeps the student's token wherever the two distributions agree and otherwise substitutes a token from the part of the target the student underweights, so interventions are as rare as any exact sampler allows and recede as the student learns. Across six base students from two model families (Qwen3 and Qwen3.5, 0.6B to 4B), CoRD raises average pass@1 over on-policy distillation by 0.9 to 2.0 points and pass@8 by 1.2 to 3.4 points on five public math benchmarks at equal steps and data. On frozen teacher-reached states, a 4B student trained with CoRD reaches in 16 steps a gap closure that on-policy distillation does not reach in 80, and substitution rules intervening at least as often in other directions fall short of CoRD.

</details>


### [102] [Tight Asymptotic Bounds on the Maximum Number of Linear Regions of Piecewise-Linear Kolmogorov--Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07973)
*Ye Su,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文确定 CPWL KAN 线性区域数的紧致增长阶 Θ_n(k^{Σρ_{ℓ-1}})，改进已有上界，并证明在同等参数预算下 KAN 相对 ReLU 网络具有 Λ^{d⌊T/2⌋} 量级的多项式优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schoots et al. (AISTATS 2025) 仅给出 CPWL KAN 线性区域数的一个普遍偏松的上界 k^{Σ n_{ℓ-1}n_ℓ}，且未提供下界，无法刻画其可达几何复杂度的真实量级，也难以与 ReLU 网络的表达能力做严格比较。

Method: 考虑 T 层、宽度向量 n、输入维 d、输出宽度 1、每条边至多 k≥2 个仿射段的 CPWL KAN；通过构造匹配的上界与下界，得到关于 k 的紧致阶 Θ_n(k^{Σ_{ℓ=1}^{T}ρ_{ℓ-1}})，其中 ρ_{ℓ-1}=min_{0≤s≤ℓ-1} n_s；并在固定非线性深度 T、每模型至多 Λ 个参数的稠密参数化设定下，通过计数可训练结点位置并优化宽度与 k，比较 KAN 与 ReLU 网络的最大区域数之比。

Result: 上下界匹配，得到紧致阶 Θ_n(k^{Σρ_{ℓ-1}})；当所有隐藏层宽度不小于 d 时简化为 Θ_n(k^{dT})；相较 k^{Σ n_{ℓ-1}n_ℓ} 的旧上界改进因子为 Θ_n(k^{Σ n_{ℓ-1}n_ℓ − Σρ_{ℓ-1}})，只要存在宽度大于 1 的隐藏层即为严格渐近改进；参数预算 Λ→∞ 时，KAN 与 ReLU 最大区域数之比为 Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d⌊T/2⌋})。

Conclusion: 这些紧致界刻画了 CPWL KAN 可达到的几何复杂度，为理解其表达能力提供理论基础，并为架构设计（宽度、分段数 k、深度 T 的权衡）提供指导；T≥2 时该多项式优势表明 KAN 在高维下具备更强的区域划分能力。

Abstract: Schoots et al.~\citep{schoots2025relating} (AISTATS 2025) established a generally loose upper bound on the number of linear regions of continuous piecewise-linear (CPWL) Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs), without providing a lower bound. We consider a $T$-layer network with width vector $\mathbf n=(n_0,\ldots,n_T)$, input dimension $n_0=d$, output width $n_T=1$, and at most $k\geq2$ affine segments per edge. Our matching upper and lower bounds determine the tight growth order $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}})$ in $k$, where $ρ_{\ell-1}:=\min_{0\leq s\leq\ell-1}n_s$. When every hidden width is at least $d$, the tight order simplifies to $Θ_{\mathbf n}(k^{dT})$. The upper bound of \citep{schoots2025relating} is $k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell}$. Our upper bound improves it by a factor of $Θ_{\mathbf n}\!\left(k^{\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}n_{\ell-1}n_\ell-\sum_{\ell=1}^{T}ρ_{\ell-1}} \right)$, with strict asymptotic improvement whenever a hidden layer has width greater than one. We further compare CPWL KANs with rectified linear unit (ReLU) networks using dense parameterizations with at most $Λ$ parameters per model and the same fixed nonlinear depth $T$. Counting trainable knot locations and optimizing the widths and $k$, we prove that the maximum region count of CPWL KANs divided by that of ReLU networks is $Θ_{d,T}(Λ^{d\lfloor T/2\rfloor})$ as $Λ\to\infty$, yielding a polynomial advantage for KANs when $T\geq2$. These tight bounds characterize the attainable geometric complexity of CPWL KANs, providing a foundation for understanding their expressive power and guiding architectural design.

</details>


### [103] [QRAE: Query-Relevant Attention Entropy for Context Compression in DeepSearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07976)
*Yingjie Tan,Haiquan Qiu,Quanming Yao,Manyi Zhong,Xianzhi Yu,Yaqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QRAE在线上下文压缩方法，用稀疏证据评估头通过配对预填充打分，决定哪些完整检索块应跨推理步骤保留，在DeepSearch基准上提升成功率2.35个百分点并大幅降低延迟与token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSearch智能体反复检索并把来源带入后续推理，导致上下文膨胀、token消耗与延迟上升，而许多检索结果对后续推理并无贡献，因此需要在线压缩上下文。

Method: QRAE利用稀疏证据评估头，对候选批次做配对预填充：无查询对照预填充抑制共享注意力响应，再用归一化熵度量每块内正向响应的广度；高分块进入持久证据白名单并纳入推理上下文，不确定块留在候选缓存以便后续重评估。无需额外生成式压缩或修改注意力算子。

Result: 在多个DeepSearch基准与推理模型上，任务成功率平均提升2.35个百分点，端到端延迟与上报token用量分别平均降低30%和45%，并在接近100B参数的模型上同样有效。

Conclusion: QRAE是一种轻量、无需改模型结构、可泛化到大模型的在线上下文压缩方法，能同时提升性能与效率。

Abstract: DeepSearch LLM agents repeatedly search and carry retrieved sources into subsequent reasoning steps. This expanding context increases token consumption and latency, although many results do not contribute to later reasoning. We propose Query-Relevant Attention Entropy (QRAE), an online context compression method that uses sparse evidence-assessment heads to decide which intact retrieved chunks should persist across reasoning steps. It scores bounded candidate batches through paired prefills: a query-free control suppresses shared attention responses, while normalized entropy measures the breadth of the positive response within each chunk.
