For Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), however, the effective geometry of its weight matrices is shaped not only by its learned channel-mixing weights, but also by the graph-propagated representations it acts on. Thus, even if an update is well normalized as a matrix, its effect on the layer output can be amplified when applied to graph-propagated features. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMuon, a graph-aware optimizer that adapts Muon's matrix updates to the covariance structure of graph-propagated layer inputs. GraphMuon performs orthogonalization in a metric defined by these propagated representations and normalizes step sizes according to the update's induced effect on the layer representation. We show that theoretically, vanilla Muon can suffer from graph-induced update amplification, while our GraphMuon can mitigate this effect. As such, our GraphMuon yields a graph-metric analogue of Muon's polar update step. Empirically, GraphMuon achieves $1.32\times$-$2.42\times$ validation-loss time-to-target speedups over AdamW on three large-scale OGB benchmarks. Across 15 dataset-architecture configurations spanning node classification, inductive graph classification, and graph regression, it achieves lower time-to-target in 13 cases, while maintaining competitive or superior predictive performance. Code is available at https://github.com/guy-hadad/GraphMuon.

</details>


### [139] [Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394)
*Déborah Boyenval,Alina Malyutina,Jaana Oikkonen,Sammeli Liikkanen,Sampsa Hautaniemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个生物学知情、患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）的评估框架，用于评测配对RNA表达与CNV合成数据的生成，并在DECIDER高级别浆液性卵巢癌纵向队列上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对多组学癌症队列构建成本高、共享困难，合成队列可作为补充资源；但生成模型研究多聚焦新架构，缺乏判断合成数据是否保留生物学相关结构的评估标准。

Method: 构建评估协议：以真实训练集与留出集之间的一致性作为参考基准，衡量模态特异的保真度与RNA–CNV配对间的相干性，并辅以训练数据邻近性诊断；将三种基于变分自编码器的多组学整合架构适配为配对数据生成器。

Result: 在多项保真度与相干性指标上表现最佳的模型，同时在训练数据邻近性上也最强。

Conclusion: 该协议与案例研究为社区比较未来多组学生成器提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Paired multi-omic cancer cohorts are costly to assemble and difficult to share, motivating synthetic cohorts as a complementary resource. As generative model development accelerates, we shift the focus from proposing yet another architecture to defining evaluation criteria that determine whether synthetic data preserve biologically relevant structure. We introduce a biology-informed and patient-disjoint framework for evaluating the generation of paired RNA expression and copy-number variation (CNV) profiles, and apply it to the longitudinal DECIDER high-grade serous ovarian cancer cohort. The framework assesses modality-specific fidelity and coherence between RNA and CNV pairs using agreement between real training and held-out data as a reference, alongside complementary training-data proximity diagnostics. We adapt three multi-omic integration architectures based on variational autoencoders to generate paired profiles. The model leading several fidelity and coherence measures also shows the strongest training-data proximity. The protocol and case study offer the community a reference for comparing future multi-omic generators.

</details>


### [140] [Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409)
*Joshua Stiller,Annika Schneider,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究了PDE驱动的时序物理系统中替代模型的不确定性量化，证明连续时间下相邻时刻分布可互奇异导致精确覆盖不可实现，并给出离散化下总变差距离随网格分辨率指数饱和的分析，进而提出用加权保形预测在增长时间尺度上恢复有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测虽提供无分布假设的覆盖保证，但在物理与工程常见的时序场景中该保证会失效并出现系统性欠覆盖；作者希望在PDE替代模型的函数空间与离散化两种情形下厘清失效机制，并寻找可行的修复途径。

Method: 一是从函数空间角度对时序分布做理论刻画，讨论不同时刻分布的互奇异性；二是针对离散网格分析分布间总变差距离随空间分辨率的变化规律，量化其对覆盖所需离散化约束的影响；三是在特定设定下采用有原则的加权保形预测构造保形分数与权重。

Result: 证明了函数空间中任意接近时刻的分布可相互奇异，从而精确覆盖保证不可能成立；证明离散情形下总变差距离通常随网格分辨率指数级饱和，因此必须对空间离散化施加限制才能保证覆盖；并在部分设定下通过加权保形预测获得随时间视界增长仍成立的有限样本覆盖。

Conclusion: 时序PDE替代模型的保形覆盖失效有其内在的分布层面根源，而非仅算法缺陷；工程应用中需谨慎处理空间离散化，并可采用加权保形预测在可控条件下恢复有限样本覆盖保证。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in scientific machine learning, where models inform safety-critical tasks such as flood forecasting, financial risk management, and thermal control in machines. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in time-dependent settings common to physics and engineering, these guarantees can break down, leading to systematic undercoverage. We study this problem in the context of surrogate models for time-dependent physical systems described by partial differential equations. We prove that in a function space setting, distributions at arbitrarily close times can be mutually singular, making exact coverage guarantees impossible. We further show that in discretized settings, the total variation distance between distributions often saturates exponentially fast with the grid resolution, mandating restrictions on the spatial discretization to ensure coverage. Finally, for certain settings, we show how to use principled weighted conformal prediction to obtain finite-sample coverage guarantees over growing time horizons.

</details>


### [141] [Spectral and Subspace Dynamics of LoRA in Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08414)
*Chenruo Liu,Zihan Wang,Yijun Dong,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文追踪SFT与RL中LoRA更新的奇异谱与子空间动态，发现两者差异源于group-mean baseline subtraction，并据此为两阶段定制谱整形策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在SFT与RL中常用相同优化器设置，但两阶段不同动态如何体现在LoRA低秩增量上尚不清楚，缺乏对各阶段适配特性的机制理解与针对性设计。

Method: 在数学推理任务上追踪LoRA更新的奇异谱与奇异子空间；逐一插值SFT与RL的组成要素以定位差异来源；构建经LoRA更新的二次multi-index模型进行理论分析；基于机制结论分别为SFT（压平全量更新的谱，使适配铺展到各奇异方向）与RL（压平当前LoRA增量span之外的更新部分，促进子空间旋转）设计谱整形配置。

Result: RL在任意学习率下均使谱集中，SFT在中等学习率下使谱扩张；小学习率下RL的奇异子空间旋转速度远快于SFT；group-mean baseline subtraction被确定为两种对比的共同来源。理论模型表明该操作将coherent fitting与gradient noise两股竞争力量的平衡推向谱集中与更快子空间旋转，并迫使risk-favorable学习率偏低。多模型实验显示，上述SFT与RL配置在多数评测设置中取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: SFT与RL下LoRA的谱与子空间动态存在系统性差异，其根源为group-mean baseline subtraction；按阶段定制谱整形可有效提升数学推理任务表现。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly used in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), often under similar optimizer settings. Yet, how the different dynamics of the two stages manifest in the low-rank increment that LoRA optimizes remains unclear. Tracking the singular spectrum and subspaces of LoRA updates on mathematical reasoning tasks, we find that RL concentrates the spectrum at every learning rate, while SFT expands it at moderate learning rates; moreover, RL rotates the singular subspaces much faster than SFT at small learning rates. Interpolating between SFT and RL one ingredient at a time, we pinpoint group-mean baseline subtraction as the source of both contrasts. A quadratic multi-index model updated via LoRA unveils the underlying mechanism: group-mean baseline subtraction tilts the balance between two competing forces, coherent fitting and gradient noise, toward spectral concentration and faster subspace rotation, while forcing the risk-favorable learning rate to be low. Guided by these dynamics, we tailor LoRA updates to each stage: in SFT, we flatten the spectrum of the full update to spread adaptation across singular directions, whereas in RL, we flatten the part of the update outside the current LoRA increment span to promote subspace rotation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple models show that the respective configurations for SFT and RL yield the best performance among the compared methods in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [142] [On the limitations of molecular representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420)
*Acer Blake,Fergus Imrie,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估分子表示质量后发现：先进分子嵌入模型在捕捉图结构、3D形状和ADMET终点相似性上均不优于指纹或理化描述符，而经典指纹在超大规模库中也存在严重的表示非唯一性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子表示（指纹、学习型嵌入）是化学信息学与小分子机器学习的核心输入，但现有评估多局限于窄基准任务的下游模型性能，缺乏对表示质量本身的系统考察。

Method: 对分子表示质量进行系统性研究，在多个维度上对比学习型嵌入、经典指纹与理化描述符，包括图结构、3D形状与ADMET终点相似性的保持能力，并分析表示在Enamine REAL库（约108亿分子）中的碰撞（非唯一表示）情况。

Result: 最先进的分子嵌入模型在保持图结构、3D形状和ADMET终点相似性方面均未超过指纹或理化描述符；同时经典指纹也暴露明显局限——视表示而定，Enamine REAL库中有2.25亿至约40亿个分子具有非唯一表示，对虚拟筛选造成影响。

Conclusion: 当前分子表示仍存在显著局限，指向未来需要发展更稳健的小分子编码方法。

Abstract: Molecular representations such as fingerprints and learned embeddings play a central role in cheminformatics and small-molecule machine learning. They serve as the primary input for a diverse array of tasks, including clustering, dataset splitting, and virtual screening, yet their evaluation is often limited to downstream model performance on narrow benchmark tasks. We carried out a systematic investigation of the quality of molecular representations, finding that none of the state-of-the-art molecular embedding models capture graph structure, 3D shape, or ADMET endpoint similarity better than fingerprints or physicochemical descriptors. However, classical fingerprints also revealed their own material limitations. For example, we show that depending on the representation, between 225M and $\sim$4B molecules have non-unique representations in the 10.8B Enamine REAL database, with implications for virtual screening. Our findings provide an insight into the current limitations of molecular representations, outlining future research directions for the development of more robust encodings of small molecules.

</details>


### [143] [A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445)
*Angel Daruna,Michael Cogswell,Tixiao Shan,Yi Yao,Han-Pang Chiu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种隐藏马尔可夫模型，将攻击者行为模式作为隐状态，并在可行性掩码的可执行动作树上定义模式条件动作律，统一给出下一动作分布与模式信念更新，估计可分解为凸逻辑回归并由Baum-Welch复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御方需在攻击者行动前预测其下一动作分布，但存在两个复杂因素：攻击者的行为模式会持续且可在单个回合内切换；每个动作由操作与状态相关参数组合而成，其可行取值随时间变化。

Method: 以攻击者当前活跃模式为隐状态的HMM；模式条件动作律定义在可行性掩码的可执行动作树上；同一似然同时用作下一动作分布与观测动作后的模式信念更新；部分观测前缀贡献其已解析的局部因子；估计分离为凸逻辑回归拟合，在模式无标注时由Baum-Welch复用。理论上推导了模式信息的可达加权逐节点KL表达式、切换模式路径的序列级对数损失界及一致回退保证。

Result: 在CAGE 2网络防御基准上，滤波后的隐状态每步带来0.8 nats收益；控制器切换后损失三步内开始下降，而循环基线需十步。

Conclusion: 该模型能同时刻画模式持续/切换与可行性变化的组合动作空间，提供可解释的信息度量与理论保证，并在基准上显著加快对模式切换的适应。

Abstract: A defender anticipating an attacker needs a probability distribution over the attacker's next action from information available before that action occurs. Two structures complicate this: the attacker's behavioral regime persists and can switch within an episode, and each action combines an operation with state-dependent arguments whose feasible values change over time. We capture both with a hidden Markov model whose latent state is the active regime and whose regime-conditioned action law is defined on a feasibility-masked tree of executable actions. The same likelihood forms the next-action distribution and updates the regime belief once the action is seen, and a partially observed prefix contributes its resolved local factors. Estimation separates into convex logistic fits that Baum-Welch reuses when regimes are unlabeled. We derive a reach-weighted nodewise KL expression for regime information, a sequence-wise log-loss bound for switching regime paths, and a uniform fallback guarantee. On the CAGE 2 cyber defense benchmark the filtered latent state is worth 0.8 nats per step, and after a controller switch the loss begins to fall in three steps against ten for a recurrent baseline.

</details>


### [144] [Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453)
*Zeyu Leo Liu,Yang Li,Bo Pang,Emre Can Acikgoz,Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Anurag Koul,Srijan Bansal,Erik Nijkamp,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了on-policy distillation (OPD) 与GRPO在达到目标准确率所需的训练FLOPs上的效率。核心发现：外部后训练教师下OPD不如GRPO；但以学生自身的GRPO checkpoint为教师时，OPD效率极高（数学上减少50倍FLOPs），并可通过multi-teacher OPD将各领域专家整合为单一学生模型，整体FLOPs降低42.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD用冻结教师提供逐token密集监督，直觉上应比RL更样本高效，但"密集信号是否真正降低达到目标精度的训练FLOPs"缺乏系统量化比较。作者希望在任务难度、师生规模、领域与优化选择等多维度下，以held-out准确率/训练FLOPs为统一指标，厘清OPD相对GRPO的效率边界与适用条件。

Method: 在匹配设置下系统对比vanilla reverse-KL OPD与GRPO，度量held-out准确率随训练FLOPs的变化；扫描任务难度、student/teacher规模、领域和优化选择；对比两类教师设置——(1)外部post-trained teacher（含进一步用GRPO训练的教师），(2)学生自身的GRPO checkpoint；并在更大student、code领域及第二个模型家族上复现；最后评估multi-teacher OPD整合各领域GRPO专家。

Result: 外部后训练教师下，OPD在准确率/训练FLOP上总体劣于GRPO，且常出现response-length inflation；当教师为学生自身GRPO checkpoint时，OPD在数学上以约1/50的蒸馏FLOPs恢复该checkpoint精度，并在更大student、code及第二个模型家族中稳定复现。单领域场景下该checkpoint本身即目标模型，蒸馏反而增加总计算；跨领域时无单一checkpoint通吃。Multi-teacher OPD整合2域与4域专家时，蒸馏阶段FLOPs仅占专家训练的5.2%与8.0%；含专家训练在内，2域混合下总FLOPs比joint GRPO少42.0%。

Conclusion: OPD的FLOPs效率高度依赖教师来源，而非教师是否"更强"：外部教师不划算，自身GRPO checkpoint才是高效教师。OPD的价值在于以低FLOPs把多个领域专家的能力固化进单一student（整合/压缩），而非在单领域替代GRPO；端到端multi-teacher pipeline在混合域上具有明确的计算节省。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under a frozen teacher's per-token supervision. Does this dense signal reduce the training FLOPs needed to reach a target accuracy compared with reinforcement learning? We systematically compare vanilla reverse-KL OPD with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across task difficulty, student and teacher size, domain, and optimization choices, measuring held-out accuracy against training FLOPs. With an external post-trained teacher, OPD generally loses to GRPO in accuracy per training FLOP at matched settings, even when the teacher is further trained with GRPO, and often suffers response-length inflation. Yet a different teacher changes the picture: when the teacher is the student's own GRPO checkpoint, OPD recovers that checkpoint's accuracy on math with $50\times$ fewer distillation FLOPs than GRPO needs to reach the same accuracy. Following the same teacher setting, we consistently observe this high efficiency with a larger student, on code, and in a second model family. On a single domain, however, the GRPO checkpoint is already the desired model, so distilling it adds to total compute. Across domains, separate GRPO checkpoints hold different capabilities, and no single checkpoint serves every domain. Multi-teacher OPD (DeepSeek-AI, 2026) consolidates per-domain GRPO experts into a single student that matches each expert's accuracy, with the distillation stage costing only 5.2% (on 2 domains) and 8.0% (on 4 domains) of the specialist training FLOPs. Counting specialist training and distillation together, this pipeline reaches joint GRPO's performance on the 2-domain mix with 42.0% fewer total FLOPs.

</details>


### [145] [Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457)
*Yi Feng,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗训练（AT）中确定性格点下降（GD）在大常数步长下的动力学，揭示其与标准训练（ST）的三点关键差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AT动力学研究多关注小步长或消失步长情形（此时GD近似连续时间流），但此类步长训练效率低、实用中罕见，大常数步长下AT的GD行为仍缺乏理解。

Method: 以对抗线性逻辑回归为对象，分析大常数步长GD的隐式偏置（参数收敛位置）与优化过程（损失下降速度）；再将结论推广至神经网络训练，考察AT过程中损失尖锐度（sharpness）的演化。

Result: 发现三点：AT与ST的隐式偏置差距此前仅在小步长下成立，现证明其对任意大常数步长仍持续存在；与ST不同，AT优化误差不存在统一收敛速率；尖锐度呈现两阶段动力学，区别于ST的"稳定性边缘"行为。

Conclusion: 大常数步长下AT的GD动力学与ST存在本质差异，深化了对AT训练行为的理论理解。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) is an important method for improving model robustness. We study the dynamics of deterministic Gradient Descent (GD) in AT. Most existing works in this direction focus on the small or vanishing step sizes regime, where GD resembles a continuous-time flow. However, such step sizes can make training inefficient and are rarely used in practice. In this paper, we study the AT dynamics of GD with large constant step sizes. We first study GD in adversarial linear logistic regression, focusing on two aspects: where the model parameters converge (implicit bias) and how quickly the losses decrease (optimization). We then turn to neural networks training and study how the sharpness of losses evolve during AT. Our results advance the understanding of the behaviors of GD with large step sizes in AT and highlight three key differences from its behavior in Standard Training (ST): (i) the implicit bias gap between AT and ST, previously established only for small step sizes, persists for arbitrarily large constant step sizes, (ii) different from ST, there is no uniform rate of convergence for the optimization error in AT, and (iii) sharpness can exhibit two-stage dynamics that differ from the Edge-of-Stability behavior observed in ST.

</details>


### [146] [User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487)
*Jonathan Kozaczuk,Zheng Dong,Noyan Evirgen,Keivan Majidi,Eddie K. H. Ng,Chris Porter,Qi Zhao,Jia Geng,Changmao Li,Brandon R. Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将用户嵌入理解为可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加，用稀疏自编码器（SAE）后验地恢复这些模式并自动用自然语言命名，实现零样本受众检索与刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户嵌入被广泛用于推荐、个性化与行为建模，但其表示本质上是稠密且不透明的，严重限制可解释性。作者希望在不改动上游嵌入系统的前提下，为稠密用户嵌入提供可解释的行为层面解释。

Method: 提出用户嵌入是"可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加"这一假设：该结构对线性嵌入由用户行为生成模型直接预言，并借"叠加假设"近似推广到深度学习表示。据此采用稀疏字典学习（如稀疏自编码器 SAE）恢复叠加模式，并对恢复的模式自动进行自然语言命名与查询。方法为后验（post-hoc）、模型无关（model-agnostic）。

Result: 在合成数据上以已知真值验证模式恢复：SAE 优于经典稀疏基线，且在 Transformer 编码嵌入上同样有效。随后将该方法应用于两个真实数据集上的经典与 Transformer 用户嵌入。自然语言标签使零样本受众检索与刻画成为可能，且适用于从未用文本训练的嵌入。

Conclusion: 用户嵌入可被有效解构为稀疏、可解释、可命名查询的行为模式，SAE 是可行的恢复工具。该框架无需修改上游嵌入系统，具有通用性和实际可解释性价值。

Abstract: User embeddings are central components of modern recommendation, personalization, and behavioral modeling systems. Though these embeddings can compactly encode rich behavioral information, they are inherently dense and opaque, limiting interpretability. To address this gap, we argue that user embeddings can be understood as sparse superpositions of interpretable behavioral modes. This structure is directly predicted by generative models of user behavior for linear embeddings and extends approximately to deep-learning representations by the superposition hypothesis. Superimposed modes can naturally be recovered by sparse dictionary learning methods, such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and recovered modes can be automatically named and queried in natural language. We validate mode recovery against known ground truth on synthetic data, where SAEs recover modes better than classical sparse baselines, including on transformer-encoded embeddings. We then apply the method to classical and transformer-based user embeddings from two real-world datasets. We also show how the corresponding natural-language labels enable zero-shot audience retrieval and characterization over embeddings never trained on text. The framework is post-hoc and model-agnostic, requiring no modification to the upstream embedding system.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Reducing Representational Interference in Retrieval Embeddings with Trainable Prefix Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07298)
*Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出在嵌入模型训练时于输入前添加可训练的[DUMP] token，在不改动架构、池化策略和损失的前提下改善检索表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型将变长文本压缩为定长向量，压缩过程会把语义内容与残余的token级信息（如attention sink产生的伪迹）纠缠在一起，影响最终检索表示的质量。

Method: 训练时在输入前预置可训练的[DUMP] token，保持模型架构、池化策略与训练损失不变，仅通过标准对比检索目标优化这些新增token；它们作为可训练前缀位置在编码中与常规token交互，从而吸收/削弱残余或无信息token信号对最终表示的影响。

Result: 在标准检索与长上下文基准上，方法在编码器与解码器两类嵌入模型上均稳定优于微调基线；分析显示[DUMP] token改变了与低信息token相关的token级激活与注意力模式。

Conclusion: 可训练前缀token是一种轻量、即插即用的手段，可有效提升检索表示质量。

Abstract: Embedding models compress variable-length text into fixed-size vector representations, but this compression can entangle semantic content with residual token-level information, such as artifacts arising from attention sink behavior. We propose a simple method that prepends trainable [DUMP] tokens to the input during embedding model training. The method keeps the model architecture, pooling strategy, and training loss unchanged, while optimizing the added tokens through the standard contrastive retrieval objective. By introducing trainable prefix positions that interact with regular tokens during encoding, [DUMP] tokens help reduce the influence of residual or uninformative token-level signals on the final retrieval representation. Experiments on standard retrieval and long-context benchmarks show that our method consistently improves over finetuned baselines across encoder and decoder embedding models. Further analysis shows that [DUMP] tokens alter token-level activation and attention patterns associated with low-information tokens. These results suggest that trainable prefix tokens provide a lightweight way to improve retrieval representations.

</details>


### [148] [CacheGraft: Enhancing Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Latent-Space Document Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07393)
*Vihan Lakshman,Peter Baile Chen,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CacheGraft 将大模型与小模型融合得到的 KV cache 离线编码为语料库段落的表示，检索时直接注入小模型，无需大模型参与，从而以隐空间文档增强提升计算受限场景下的 RAG 效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明基于 transformer 的语言模型智能体可通过 KV cache 在隐空间直接通信，但该"缓存到缓存翻译"能力此前仅用于实时智能体通信。作者希望拓展其用途，将其用于检索增强生成，并在推理时只能部署小模型的计算受限场景中提升答案质量。

Method: 基于 Cache-to-Cache (C2C) 融合框架提出 CacheGraft：离线阶段将每个语料库段落编码为大共享模型（4B）与小接收模型（0.6B）之间的融合 KV cache 表示；查询阶段将该缓存注入小接收模型，无需大模型在线参与。

Result: 在五个 QA 基准、两个独立本地运行的评审模型上评测，30 组比较中有 29 组优于纯文本接收模型基线，其中 17 组达到统计显著；且这些增益对 fuser 而言是零样本的，fuser 从未在检索数据上训练。

Conclusion: 隐空间文档增强是提升 RAG 系统性能的有前景方向，尤其适用于仅能部署小模型的计算受限场景。

Abstract: Recent work in machine learning has demonstrated that transformer-based language model agents can communicate state not only through text, but also directly in latent space via key-value (KV) caches. In this paper, we argue that such cache-to-cache translation can provide utility beyond the original motivating application of real-time agent communication and can instead be utilized for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Building on the Cache-to-Cache (C2C) fusion framework, our proposed system CacheGraft encodes each corpus passage offline into a fused KV cache representation between a larger sharer model (4B parameters in our experiments) and a smaller receiver model (0.6B parameters) such that this cache can be injected into the receiver at query time with no need for the larger model. Across five QA benchmarks and two independent locally-run judges, CacheGraft improves answer quality over the text-only receiver baseline in 29 out of 30 comparisons, of which 17 of the 30 are statistically significant. Notably, these gains are zero-shot with respect to the fuser, which was never trained on retrieval data. These initial results suggest that latent-space document enrichment may be a promising avenue for enhancing the performance of RAG systems, particularly in compute-constrained settings where it is only feasible to deploy a small model at inference time.

</details>


### [149] [BRIDGE: Bridging the Semantic Quantization Gap for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07425)
*Yatong Xiao,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文识别生成式推荐中"语义量化鸿沟"(SQG)瓶颈，提出BRIDGE框架，通过码本锚定潜推理、特权EMA自蒸馏与难度感知循环计算三方面弥合离散SID与连续语义结构之间的失配，在三个Amazon基准上Recall@10/NDCG@10平均提升10.9%/9.7%，线上A/B实验eCPM提升0.65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐用RQ-VAE将物品量化为层级SID，解码器逐层自回归生成。但量化过程依赖码本质心、局部邻域几何、层级熵等丰富连续信号，而解码器仅接收离散标识符，丢失了塑造SID的语义结构。该失配表现为三种鸿沟：grounding gap（目标侧信息有限时解码器缺乏语义锚点）、neighborhood gap（token级监督丢弃物品间相似性并加剧曝光偏差）、difficulty gap（计算被均匀分配，而生成难度随深度递减）。

Method: 提出BRIDGE，包含三个组件：(1) Codebook-grounded latent reasoning：在SID生成前注入冻结量化器质心作为语义锚点；(2) Privileged EMA self-distillation：从教师模型向on-policy学生迁移邻域感知知识，保留物品间几何并缓解曝光偏差；(3) Effort-aware recurrence：对早期困难层级分配更多计算，随深度递进减少。

Result: 在三个Amazon基准上，相较最强基线平均提升Recall@10 10.9%、NDCG@10 9.7%（最高12.6%、10.7%）；在大规模电商广告平台5%流量的一周线上A/B实验中，eCPM提升0.65%。

Conclusion: 语义量化鸿沟是生成式推荐中被忽视的关键瓶颈，通过锚定连续语义、蒸馏邻域结构与按难度分配计算可有效弥合，离线与线上实验均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation retrieves items by autoregressively generating Semantic IDs(SIDs), where an RQ-VAE quantizes each item into a hierarchical SID sequence that the decoder generates level by level. We identify an overlooked bottleneck in this paradigm: the Semantic Quantization Gap(SQG). Quantization derives SIDs from rich continuous signals, including codebook centroids, local neighborhood geometry, and level-wise entropy. However, the decoder receives only discrete identifiers and loses the semantic structure that shaped them. This mismatch manifests in three ways: a grounding gap, where the decoder lacks semantic anchors when target-side information is limited; a neighborhood gap, where token-level supervision discards inter-item similarity and exacerbates exposure bias; and a difficulty gap, where computation is allocated uniformly despite generation difficulty diminishing with depth.
  To bridge the SQG, we propose BRIDGE. Codebook-grounded latent reasoning injects frozen quantizer centroids as semantic anchors before SID generation. Privileged EMA self-distillation transfers neighborhood-aware knowledge from a teacher to an on-policy student, preserving inter-item geometry while mitigating exposure bias. Effort-aware recurrence allocates more computation to the challenging early levels and progressively reduces it with depth. Across three Amazon benchmarks, BRIDGE improves Recall@10 and NDCG@10 by 10.9% and 9.7% on average over the strongest baseline (up to 12.6% and 10.7%). In a one-week online A/B experiment on a large-scale e-commerce advertising platform with 5% traffic, BRIDGE improves eCPM by 0.65%.

</details>


### [150] [OptST: Optimizing Semantic Tokenization for LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07523)
*Hanchong Chen,Jie Liu,Fuyuan Lyu,Dugang Liu,Lingjie Li,Xiuqiang He,Xing Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OptST，使预训练语义分词器可依据下游推荐任务自适应地修正token分配，并通过交互条件化的前缀深度控制语义分辨率，在四个公开数据集上取得最高AUC且跨推荐骨干稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段流程中分词器先训练、推荐器后训练，token分配被当作固定输入特征，推荐器只能学习如何使用既定索引，无法修正生成这些索引的语义划分；而对齐任务又面临最近邻编码分配离散不可导、无约束适配会破坏预训练学到的几何结构、以及固定token深度对所有交互施加同一语义分辨率等困难。

Method: 不直接更新预训练编码器与基础码本，而是学习一个低秩形变（low-rank deformation），并用锚点正则项限制其漂移；推荐监督通过直通估计器（straight-through estimator）回传至该变换，推理时仍保留硬性最近邻编码分配；此外选择交互条件化的前缀（interaction-conditioned prefix）来控制向推荐器暴露的残差细节量，即语义分辨率深度。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，OptST的AUC高于传统基线与LLM增强基线；其提升在不同推荐骨干网络上均得以保持。

Conclusion: 任务感知的语义分词化可作为LLM派生用户画像与下游推荐模型之间有效的接口，兼顾预训练语义几何的保持与任务自适应性。

Abstract: Semantic information can enrich the identifiers and structured fields used by recommender systems. Semantic tokenization provides a structured representation of this information by converting LLM-derived user profiles into discrete tokens. In the common two-stage pipeline, the tokenizer is trained before the recommender. Its token assignments are then treated as fixed input features during recommendation training. As a result, the downstream recommender can learn how to use the assigned indices, but it cannot revise the semantic partition that produced them. Making this partition task-aware is difficult because nearest-code assignment is discrete, and unconstrained adaptation may also distort the geometry learned during pretraining. Moreover, a fixed token depth exposes the same semantic resolution to every interaction. We thus propose OptST (Optimizing Semantic Tokenization), which makes a pretrained tokenizer responsive to downstream recommendation. To revise token assignments without directly updating the pretrained encoder or base codebooks, OptST learns a low-rank deformation whose drift is limited by an anchor regularizer. Recommendation supervision reaches this transformation through a straight-through estimator, while prediction retains hard nearest-code assignments. Beyond adapting the assignments themselves, OptST selects an interaction-conditioned prefix to control how much residual detail is exposed to the recommender. Experiments on four public datasets show that OptST achieves the highest AUC among conventional and LLM-enhanced baselines. Its improvements also persist across different recommendation backbones. These results establish task-aware semantic tokenization as an effective interface between LLM-derived profiles and downstream recommendation models.

</details>


### [151] [What Gets Hacked in a Collaborative Reward for LLM Recommenders: The Part That Does Not Depend on the User](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07588)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Liang Dong,Yu Wu,Dailin Li,Yu Xia,Yunhan C. Zhang,Rui Cai,Yi Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将CF奖励分解为物品均值项与用户残差项，发现均值项导致曝光坍缩，提出逐物品中心化/标准化两种奖励修正，显著提升覆盖与曝光均匀度且保持排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用CF分数作为强化学习奖励训练LLM推荐器时会出现"曝光坍缩"：CF模型本身在各用户上给出的Top推荐是多样的，但用其奖励优化后的LLM却集中于更少的物品，作者意在定位该现象的成因并加以修正。

Method: 把每个CF分数分解为物品均值项（该物品在用户历史中的平均CF分数）与残差项（当前用户相对均值的偏差）；通过受控干预确认物品均值项是曝光坍缩的贡献者。据此提出两种奖励修正：逐物品中心化（减去估计的物品平均分）与逐物品标准化（再用估计标准差对残差缩放）。

Result: 在两个数据集、两种标识符方案上的实验表明，两种修正均大幅提升目录覆盖率与曝光均匀度；相比未修正的CF奖励，跨用户排名第一的不同物品数量最多提升9.8倍，同时排序精度保持相当。

Conclusion: CF奖励中的物品均值项是LLM推荐器曝光坍缩的偏置来源，去除该偏置的奖励修正可在不牺牲排序准确性的前提下改善推荐的多样性与公平性。

Abstract: Collaborative-filtering (CF) scores provide graded, personalized rewards for training large language model (LLM) recommenders through reinforcement learning. However, these rewards can cause exposure collapse: even though the CF model itself produces diverse top recommendations across users, an LLM optimized with its rewards concentrates on fewer items. We decompose each score into an item-mean component, the item's average CF score across user histories, and a residual component, the current user's score deviation from the average. Through controlled interventions, we identify the item-mean component as a contributor to exposure collapse. Accordingly, we propose two reward corrections: per-item centering removes each item's estimated mean score, while per-item standardization further rescales the residual using its estimated standard deviation. Experiments on two datasets with two identifier schemes show that both corrections substantially improve catalog coverage and exposure evenness. Compared with training on the uncorrected CF reward, our corrections increase the number of distinct items ranked first across users by up to $9.8\times$, while maintaining comparable ranking accuracy. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LLM4Rec-CF-Bias-27EF/.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Independent Predictions: Learning the Browsing Journey for Sequence-Level Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07591)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jingxin Hai,Zhenjia Ma,Hongbin Pei,Dagui Chen,Han Zhu,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出重排序中Generator–Evaluator框架的评估器普遍采用独立行为建模(IBM)、忽视"曝光→点击"因果依赖与注意力竞争，导致系统性偏差；提出联合行为建模(JBM)视角及即插即用的曝光-点击评估器(ICE)，通过浏览深度分布预测每位曝光概率、抑制未曝光项伪上下文、引入点击分布匹配目标，离在线实验与淘宝全流量部署验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业重排序广泛采用Generator–Evaluator框架，评估器效用估计的保真度直接决定推荐质量。但现有评估器多遵循独立行为建模(IBM)范式，把连续的用户浏览旅程简化为逐项独立预测（如CTR），忽略两个物理事实：(i) 点击只能发生在物品被触达并曝光之后；(ii) 物品之间争夺有限用户注意力。由此产生系统性偏置的评估与次优序列选择。

Method: 提出联合行为建模(JBM)视角，显式刻画序列内 display→impression→click 过程以捕捉因果与竞争依赖。基于JBM构建即插即用的Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)：(i) 预测浏览深度分布，估计各位置的曝光概率；(ii) 利用预测曝光在点击建模中抑制未曝光物品带来的虚假上下文；(iii) 引入点击分布匹配目标以建模序列内依赖。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上进行离线实验；在淘宝App开展14天在线A/B测试，点击+0.70%、收入+3.67%，并完成全流量部署，验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将重排序评估从独立行为建模转向联合行为建模，显式建模曝光与点击的因果、竞争关系，可显著提升评估保真度与序列选择质量；ICE作为即插即用模块，具备跨骨干泛化性与工业可部署性。

Abstract: Re-ranking aims to present users with an ordered list that maximizes sequence-level utility. Industrial deployments widely adopt the Generator--Evaluator framework, where the generator proposes candidate sequences, and the evaluator scores them to select the best one for display. The fidelity of the evaluator's utility estimates thus directly governs recommendation quality. However, existing evaluators predominantly follow the \textbf{Independent Behavior Modeling (IBM)} paradigm, which simplifies the continuous user \emph{browsing journey} into independent per-item predictions (e.g., CTR). This oversimplification ignores two physical realities: (i) a click can occur only after the item has been reached and impressed, and (ii) items compete for finite user attention. As a result, IBM produces systematically biased evaluations and suboptimal sequence selection. To address this, we propose a \textbf{Joint Behavior Modeling (JBM)} perspective that explicitly models the \emph{display} $\rightarrow$ \emph{impression} $\rightarrow$ \emph{click} process to capture causal and competitive dependencies within a sequence. Building on JBM, we develop the \textbf{Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)}, a plug-and-play framework that (i) predicts a browsing-depth distribution to estimate per-position impression probabilities, (ii) leverages predicted impressions to suppress spurious context from unexposed items during click modeling, and (iii) introduces a click distribution matching objective to capture intra-sequence dependencies. Extensive offline experiments across diverse backbones, together with a 14-day online A/B test in the Taobao App (+0.70\% clicks, +3.67\% revenue) and full-traffic deployment, validate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [153] [MEMOIR: A Training-Free Memory Lifecycle for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07631)
*Mayank Anand,Subham Raj,Aman Vaibhav Jha,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练记忆生命周期MEMOIR，将"合并前筛选"与"使用后归因"统一，解决记忆压缩误删证据与检索偏好陈旧记忆的耦合失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程LLM智能体记忆系统把"写‑检索"割裂处理：记忆以静态重要性分数前瞻写入且用后从不修正，导致两个耦合失效：合并压缩可能静默销毁未来查询所需证据；检索持续偏向陈旧记忆。

Method: MEMOIR为免训练记忆生命周期：(1) 判据门控合并——压缩前用筛选门检验答案相关证据是否被保留；(2) 回溯归因——每次查询后估计哪些检索记忆支撑了答案，并更新其检索优先级；(3) UCB式探索奖励，提升低曝光记忆的检索机会，避免高频记忆挤出稀有但有用证据。在线写入路径无需额外LLM调用，筛选仅用于周期性离线整合。

Result: 在1,905条去重LoCoMo QA对上，完整检索栈使evidence recall@5较裸cosine提升11.92个百分点；置信门控反馈在无参考答案、无reranker训练下额外+1.50点；部署不可用的oracle额外+1.84点、多跳任务+8.05点。判据门控合并使文本证据召回从58.82%变为59.08%，活跃存储减少1.42%，100次独立审计合并中0次观测损失，单侧95%损失率上界2.95%。

Conclusion: 结果表明可行的部署路径：在线写路径不增加LLM调用，合并筛选限于周期性离线整合。生命周期归结为一个原则——用后归因有用性，压缩前筛选保留性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that preserve useful information across sessions and retrieve it when needed. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: memories are written prospectively with static importance scores that are never revised after use. This creates two coupled failure modes: consolidation can silently destroy evidence that future queries require, while retrieval continues to favor stale memories. We propose MEMOIR (Memory Evidence via Merge-certified, Online Importance Revision), a training-free memory lifecycle that unifies judge-gated consolidation with retrospective attribution. Before compressing memory, screening gates test whether answer-relevant evidence is preserved. After each query, attribution estimates which retrieved memories supported the answer and updates their retrieval priority. A UCB-style exploration bonus additionally encourages retrieval of under-exposed memories, reducing the risk that frequently retrieved memories crowd out useful but less-seen evidence. On 1,905 deduplicated LoCoMo QA pairs, the full retrieval stack improves evidence recall@5 by 11.92 percentage points over bare cosine, with confidence-gated feedback adding 1.50 points overall without reference answers or reranker training; a deployment-unavailable oracle adds 1.84 points overall and 8.05 points on multi-hop. Judge-gated merge changes the recall of text-based evidence from 58.82% to 59.08%, reduces the active store by 1.42%, and incurs 0 observed losses in 100 independently audited merges, with a one-sided 95% upper loss-rate bound of 2.95%. Together, these results outline a practical deployment path: no additional LLM call on the online write path, with merge screening confined to periodic offline consolidation. The lifecycle reduces to one principle: attribute usefulness after use, and screen for preservation before compression.

</details>


### [154] [Probabilistic Relevance Decisions for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08014)
*Bahadır Tatar,Cevat Şener*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在BEIR SciFact测试集上比较纯稠密检索(BGE-M3)与基于相关性决策的打分(TypeSafe Jev、OpenAI Decisions)在候选重排中的效果，发现Jev的重排效果最优，但深度扩展收益不确定，更支持将相关性决策作为稠密检索的补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密相似度可提供可复用的语料表示，而概率化相关性决策能给出查询相关的文档打分，二者侧重不同。作者希望在同一协议下实证比较两类打分机制的重排效果，考察深度扩展的收益与成本。

Method: 以官方BEIR SciFact测试集的300条查询为对象，设置两类实验条件：(1)一致的"正例增强"100文档候选池；(2)全库稠密检索后在深度50与100处重排，且稠密前缀不做正例注入。比较BGE-M3、TypeSafe Jev Noul、OpenAI Decisions Predicate三种打分；采用配对bootstrap估计深度差异的置信区间，对精确并列分数做辅助性随机化分析，并统计API成本与延迟。

Result: 在所评估条件下，Jev取得最高的Recall@5与nDCG@10。深度50时全库nDCG@10分别为BGE-M3 0.6413、OpenAI 0.7545、Jev 0.7805。深度由50增至100提升了已知正例的候选召回，但最终效果变化小且不确定，四个配对bootstrap深度区间均包含零。独占条件下API打分成本约翻倍，复用缓存决策可降低增量开销；OpenAI返回大量精确零分，精确并列的辅助随机化显示排序敏感但未替代确定性主结果。Top-100延迟混合了缓存与新执行，历史混合延迟为不同执行环境与计时边界下的已存组件求和。

Conclusion: 这些探索性、未经校正的分析表明，在该协议内相关性决策可作为稠密检索的补充，但不足以证明其普遍优越性、概率校准性或不同深度间的等价性。

Abstract: Dense similarity supplies reusable corpus representations, while probabilistic relevance decisions offer query-dependent document scoring. We compare dense-only BGE-M3 with TypeSafe Jev Noul and OpenAI Decisions Predicate scores on the official BEIR SciFact test benchmark. The study evaluates 300 official test queries using identical positive-enriched 100-document pools and full-corpus dense retrieval followed by reranking at candidate depths of 50 and 100. Unlike the controlled pools, neither dense prefix receives positive injection. Jev has the highest observed Recall@5 and nDCG@10 under the evaluated conditions. At depth 50, full-corpus nDCG@10 is 0.6413 for BGE-M3, 0.7545 with OpenAI and 0.7805 with Jev. Extending depth from 50 to 100 increases known-positive candidate recall but produces small, uncertain final-effectiveness changes. All four paired-bootstrap depth intervals include zero. Unique-condition API scoring cost approximately doubles, while compatible cached decisions reduce incremental expenditure. OpenAI returns many exact-zero scores, and secondary exact-tie randomization reveals ranking sensitivity without replacing deterministic primary results. Top-100 latency mixes cached and new execution. Historical hybrid latency is a saved-component sum under different execution environments and timer boundaries. These exploratory, unadjusted analyses support relevance decisions as a complement to dense retrieval within this protocol, without establishing general superiority, probability calibration or equivalence between depths.

</details>


### [155] [Domain-specific information retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08228)
*Suzan Verberne,Florina Piroi,Allan Hanbury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文（章节式综述）系统梳理领域特定信息检索（domain-specific IR）的典型任务、数据集与应用，分析其区别于通用网页搜索的挑战（长文档、长查询、长会话、特定用户需求及模型偏见与公平性），并总结相应模型与方法，指出这些方法可迁移至其他领域甚至开放域搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息检索远不止即席网页搜索，大量信息寻求活动发生在法律、学术研究等特定领域且多为工作任务导向，而现有研究与认知常以通用网页搜索为中心，缺乏对领域特定IR的系统归纳。

Method: 以综述/章节形式组织：第2章梳理常见领域特定任务、数据集与应用；第3章分析其特有挑战（长文档、长查询、长会话、特定用户需求）及模型的偏见与公平性问题；第4章介绍针对这些任务与数据所发展的模型与方法，涵盖查询处理、用户模型与用户交互等用户层面因素。

Result: 归纳出领域特定IR的核心特征与挑战图景，并表明其诸多方法学进展具有良好的可迁移性，可应用于其他领域乃至开放域网页搜索。

Conclusion: 领域特定IR构成信息检索的重要分支，其针对长文档、长查询、长会话与专业用户需求的方法创新具有跨领域普适价值；文末总结要点并提出近期研究方向。

Abstract: Information Retrieval (IR) is much more than ad hoc web search: a large portion of the information seeking activities takes place in specific domains, often addressing work-related tasks. Examples are lawyers finding relevant prior cases for a legal case and academic researchers studying the background material for a paper. In Chapter 2 we outline the most common domain-specific tasks, datasets and applications. Domain-specific IR has a number of challenges that make set it apart from simple web search tasks: they often deal with long documents, long queries and long sessions with specific user needs. We discuss these in Chapter 3. We also address issues related to bias and fairness of models. In Chapter 4, we describe the models and methods developed for domain-specific tasks and data, addressing the specific challenges, including user aspects related to domain-specific IR tasks, dealing with queries, user models, and user interactions. We show that many of the methodological advances in domain-specific IR are transferable to other domains and even to open-domain web search. We conclude with the main takeaways and propose research directions for the near future.

</details>


### [156] [GINGER: Spicing Up Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08306)
*Daniel Salwasser,Lars Gottesbüren,Laxman Dhulipala,Jakub Łącki,Guy Blelloch,Rajesh Jayaram,Tobias Rubel,Kshiteej Sheth,Amir Zandieh,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Ginger is an open-source C++ library for graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search, combining NTQ quantization and AdaptiveGap graph compression to achieve 1.8× average query throughput gains at 95% recall with minimal memory footprint.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale high-dimensional embedding datasets demand ANNS libraries that are simultaneously fast and memory-efficient; existing libraries typically trade one for the other. The authors aim to push both query throughput and indexing speed while matching the memory footprint of the most space-efficient competitors.

Method: Ginger builds on proximity graph construction (PiPNN) and vector quantization (TurboQuant). It introduces Nested TurboQuant (NTQ), a two-level quantizer enabling low-precision graph navigation plus high-precision reranking in a single code, and reduces proximity graph size up to 2.5× via graph reordering combined with AdaptiveGap, a new lossless codec based on StreamVByte.

Result: NTQ achieves near floating-point precision with 2.6× less memory; graphs shrink up to 2.5× without throughput loss. At 95% recall, query throughput improves by 1.8× on average over each dataset's fastest competitor and ≥2.3× over any single library, rising to 2.9× over compact libraries on high-dimensional embeddings.

Conclusion: Ginger demonstrates that co-designing quantization, graph layout, and lossless compression yields state-of-the-art ANNS speed and memory efficiency, establishing a strong open-source baseline for large-scale graph-based nearest neighbor search.

Abstract: We present \textsc{Ginger}, a state-of-the-art open-source C++ library for graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS). We developed \textsc{Ginger} at Google to push the speed and memory efficiency of graph search on large and high-dimensional embedding datasets. \textsc{Ginger} surpasses existing ANNS libraries with regards to query throughput and indexing time while matching the memory footprint of the most space-efficient competitors.
  \textsc{Ginger} builds upon recent advances in proximity graph construction (PiPNN) and vector quantization (TurboQuant). We introduce Nested TurboQuant (NTQ), a two-level quantizer which supports low precision graph navigation and high precision reranking in a single code. This representation achieves near floating point precision with $2.6\times$ less memory. We also compress the size of our proximity graphs by up to $2.5\times$ without impacting query throughput. We combine graph reordering with a new lossless compression codec called AdaptiveGap, which builds upon StreamVByte. At $95\%$ recall, \textsc{Ginger} speeds up query throughput by an average of $1.8\times$ over each dataset's fastest competitor and by at least $2.3\times$ over any single library. This lead grows to $2.9\times$ over compact libraries on high-dimensional embeddings.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Code, Commits, and Complexity in the GenAI Era: A Longitudinal Study of Team-Based Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07297)
*Asma Shakil,Claus Brabrand,Paul Denny*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对260个毕业设计团队仓库、跨十个学期（2021–2025）的实证分析显示：GenAI普及后学生代码库规模约增至三倍（3.4→10 KLOC），圈复杂度与错误处理密度上升而注释密度未变，提交频率与提交量显著增加但单次提交粒度基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已在学生中普及，但团队项目代码库的实际变化缺乏了解；而项目课程常以仓库活动作为进度与学习证据，这使该问题具有评估意义。既有研究多基于自陈报告、受控任务或短期个人作业，缺少真实项目情境下代码库演化证据。

Method: 对2021–2025年间十个学期的N=260个Capstone团队代码仓库进行纵向实证分析，覆盖AI前与AI后同期群；从三个维度度量：代码规模（LOC）、代码特征（注释密度、错误处理密度、圈复杂度）、开发行为（提交量、编辑规模、提交频率）。采用同期群对比以识别变化时点，并明确声明未直接观测AI使用，仅报告与GenAI可及性提升同期发生的变化。

Result: 学期末代码库规模约增至三倍（2021–2023约3.4 KLOC，2024–2025约10 KLOC）；代码特征出现明显解耦——圈复杂度与错误处理密度上升，注释密度无显著变化；后期同期群提交更频繁、总量更大，但单次提交规模基本不变，提示开发节奏改变而非编辑粒度变化。多数变化自2024年起显现，且并非单调一致。

Conclusion: GenAI普及时期学生项目代码库在规模、结构复杂度与开发节奏上均发生实质变化，且这些变化难以用传统提交活动指标解读；因此项目课程中依据仓库活动与学生产出物进行评价的方式需要重新审视与设计。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are now widely available to students, but little is known about how team-based project codebases have changed since they appeared. This matters when project courses use repository activity as evidence of progress and learning. Existing studies have largely examined self-reports, controlled tasks, or short individual assignments, leaving open questions around how student codebases evolve in authentic project settings. We present a ten-semester analysis of N=260 capstone team repositories from 2021 to 2025, spanning pre- and post-AI cohorts. We examine three dimensions: code size (lines of code), code characteristics (comment density, error-handling density, and cyclomatic complexity), and development behaviour (commit volume, edit size, and commit frequency). We find that end-of-semester codebases roughly triple, from about 3.4 KLOC in 2021--2023 to about 10 KLOC in 2024--2025. Code characteristics show a clear decoupling: cyclomatic complexity and error-handling density increase, while comment density does not. Later cohorts commit far more often and in greater volume, but individual commit sizes are largely unchanged, suggesting a shift in development rhythm rather than edit granularity. Most changes appear from 2024 onwards and are not uniformly monotonic. We did not directly observe AI use, so we report these as changes that coincide with increased GenAI availability rather than as effects of AI use. Our findings highlight the need to reconsider how student-produced artefacts are used and assessed in project courses.

</details>


### [158] [Digital Security Under Compound Threats: Surveillance, Threat Reasoning, and Habituation Among North Korean Defectors in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07576)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Lionel Z. Wang,Hongkun Yang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对19名在华脱北者的半结构化访谈，揭示其在跨国监控压力、身份关联数字基础设施与既有监控图式下形成不均衡的数字安全实践，并区分"适应性习惯化"与"侵蚀性习惯化"两条轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在华脱北者长期处于数字安全与隐私的不确定状态，但其如何理解数字威胁、如何在受限能动性下调整安全行为尚缺乏实证刻画，尤其缺少对复合威胁与归因不确定性如何塑造无证/隐蔽用户安全推理的机制性解释。

Method: 定性研究：对19名在华脱北者进行半结构化访谈，采用主题分析归纳出三类相互关联的情境条件，并归纳受访者安全实践的类型与演变轨迹。

Result: 识别出三项相互关联条件：(1) 来自朝鲜的跨国监控压力；(2) 中国境内与身份绑定的数字基础设施及对中介者的依赖；(3) 由既往监控经验形成的威胁推理图式。受访者的防护实践呈现不均衡分布，并沿两条轨迹分化：由知识获取驱动的适应性习惯化，以及因长期未遭遇负面事件而形成的侵蚀性习惯化。

Conclusion: 复合威胁、归因不确定性与受限能动性共同塑造了无证/隐蔽用户的安全推理；研究为理解弱势迁移人群的数字安全实践提供机制性框架，并提示安全干预需兼顾知识赋能与"无事故即安全"的认知偏差风险。

Abstract: North Korean defectors residing in China face persistent uncertainty regarding digital security and privacy. Through semi-structured interviews with 19 defectors, we examine how individuals understand digital threats and adapt their security behaviors. We identify three interrelated conditions: transnational surveillance pressure from North Korea, identity-linked digital infrastructure and intermediary dependency in China, and threat reasoning shaped by prior surveillance schemas. Participants developed uneven protective practices and followed two trajectories: adaptive habituation driven by knowledge acquisition and erosive habituation driven by the prolonged absence of adverse events. Our findings show how compound threats, attribution uncertainty, and constrained agency shape security reasoning among undocumented users.

</details>


### [159] [The Popular Get More Popular: Auditing Brand Bias in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07614)
*Giorgio Monaco,Jacopo Lenti,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文审计生成式搜索引擎在商品推荐中的品牌偏见，区分"品牌流行度偏见"（偏爱市场主导品牌）与"品牌熟悉度偏见"（LLM参数记忆中的实体获得排名优势）。基于1176个头部品牌的商业查询发现：前五分位主导品牌被引用频次是平均品牌的1.4–2.8倍，且该偏见已固化于参数记忆中，外部网页检索难以缓解；在控制产品属性的上下文实验中，将真实品牌签名替换为陌生品牌会显著降低推荐排名，而保持事实不变的内容改写几乎无收益。结论：生成式搜索强化"富者愈富"动态，并构筑标准内容优化无法突破的参数化进入壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式/对话式搜索将候选商品压缩为少数直接答案，使模型对特定供应商的细微系统性偏好具备显著商业影响。作者希望量化并拆解这种偏见，区分来自市场结构（流行度）与来自模型参数记忆（熟悉度）两种机制，从而判断外部检索与内容优化能否缓解。

Method: 采用两类审计设计：(1) 对主流生成式引擎上覆盖1176个头部市场品牌的商业查询进行引用频次统计，按品牌流行度分位比较引用率，并检验外部网页检索的作用；(2) 开展控制性上下文实验，在产品属性保持恒定的条件下，对比"真实品牌签名"与"陌生品牌签名"替换后的推荐排名差异，并以保留事实的内容改写作为对照。

Result: 观察到持续存在的品牌流行度偏见：前五分位主导品牌被引用频次为样本平均品牌的1.4–2.8倍，且该偏见已存在于参数记忆中，外部检索未能缓解。品牌熟悉度实验中，替换为陌生品牌签名显著降低推荐排名；反之，保持事实的内容改写带来的排名提升可以忽略。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索引擎通过偏爱既有市场主导者、并对不熟悉实体设置参数化进入壁垒，强化了"富者愈富"的市场动态；该壁垒无法通过常规内容优化（SEO式改写）克服。

Abstract: The shift from traditional to generative search has fundamentally transformed online product discovery. Because conversational recommendations synthesize choices into a few direct answers, subtle systemic preferences toward specific providers can exert profound commercial impacts. In this work, we audit this phenomenon through two distinct dimensions: (i) brand popularity bias, the systematic preference for market-dominant entities over long-tail alternatives, and (ii) brand familiarity bias, the ranking advantage an entity encoded in an LLM's parametric memory holds over an unfamiliar competitor. First, evaluating commercial queries across major engines, we observe persistent brand popularity bias: dominant brands in the top quintile are cited 1.4x to 2.8x more frequently than the average brand within a sample of 1,176 top market players, already present in parametric memory that external web retrieval fails to mitigate. Second, through controlled in-context experiments holding product attributes constant, we find that substituting authentic brand signatures with unfamiliar ones significantly degrades recommendation rank. Conversely, fact-preserving content rewrites yield negligible ranking gains. Taken together, our findings demonstrate that generative search engines reinforce a ``rich-get-richer'' dynamic by favoring established market leaders and imposing a parametric barrier to entry that standard content optimization cannot overcome.

</details>


### [160] [Operationalizing NCII and CSAM Law in the Age of AI: Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07831)
*Kimberly Mai,Cate Heine,Vinith M. Suriyakumar,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文以英格兰与威尔士及美国法律为背景，分析生成式AI引发的合成非自愿亲密影像(NCII)与儿童性虐待材料(CSAM)的法律改革，指出其法律定义（可识别性、模型意图/能力、图像溯源）在技术层面难以落地，并提出技术-法律交叉的开放问题研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI导致合成NCII与CSAM激增，催生一系列法律改革；但这些法律依赖的定义在技术操作化上存在模糊性，缺乏可执行的技术支撑，因而需要厘清技术与法律的接口。

Method: 法律-技术交叉分析：以英格兰与威尔士及美国相关法律为依据，逐项审视三类关键定义（被描绘者是否可识别、模型是否具备滥用意向或能力、滥用图像能否溯源到生成模型），并对每项定义梳理实现其操作化所需的技术研究现状与缺口。

Result: 提出一个跨技术-法律界面的开放问题研究议程，涵盖三个层面：现有法律的解释、AI工具部署前的危害预防，以及犯罪行为的执法与追责。

Conclusion: 这些问题是技术共同体有能力解决的，且其进展对现实世界的保护至关重要；技术界应主动参与法律定义的可操作化研究。

Abstract: Advances in generative AI have driven a surge in synthetic non-consensual intimate imagery (NCII) and child sexual abuse material (CSAM), prompting a series of legal reforms. These reforms rely on legal definitions where technical operationalization is ambiguous, such as whether a depicted person is identifiable, whether a model is capable of or intended for abuse generation, and whether an abusive image can be traced to the model that produced it. Drawing on the law of England and Wales and the United States, we examine the relevant law alongside the technical research needed to operationalize it. From this, we develop a research agenda of open problems at this technical-legal interface, spanning the interpretation of existing law, the prevention of harm before an AI tool is deployed, and enforcement of offenses. These are problems the technical community is well placed to solve, and where progress is vital to real-world protection.

</details>


### [161] [How Generative AI Redefines Personalization and Diversity in the Newsroom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07931)
*Cheng Yu,Jim Chi,Aaron J. Snoswell,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式AI改变了新闻个性化的内涵：新闻多样性相关决策日益前移至生产环节并分散于多版本稿件中，难以被组织监督与人工审核察觉，记者仍承担形式责任却无法完全看见或控制这些决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上"新闻个性化与信息多样性"的张力被理解为不同人接触不同内容、以来源/议题/视角的曝光范围衡量多样性。生成式AI使这一框架过时：它既改变记者面向受众的适配方式，也改变记者所用AI工具对自身生产偏好的个性化，因此需重新审视编辑判断与新闻多样性得以产生的条件。

Method: 对公共服务、商业化与国有背景新闻机构中的16名媒体从业者开展半结构化访谈，分析其AI整合实践如何重塑编辑判断与多样性生产条件。

Result: 受访者通过"保留对意义的权威、下放内容呈现环节"来协商AI整合；但其自用工具中介了可用来源与视角，引入对多样性有影响却基本不可见的决策。受访者对面向受众的个性化能预期多样性风险，却多将工具对多样性的约束视为技术问题。结果是，塑造新闻多样性的决策越来越多发生在发布前、散布于同一报道的多个版本中，发布内容评估与人工审批均难以观察。

Conclusion: 维护新闻多样性不能仅审查已发布内容，还需关注塑造内容的专业实践、基础设施与控制权分配；记者在形式负责与实质不可控之间形成责任错位。

Abstract: The tension between news personalization and information diversity has traditionally been understood as a matter of different content reaching different people, with diversity evaluated by the range of sources, topics, and perspectives to which audiences are exposed. Generative AI outdates this understanding, since it transforms personalization in two ways: how journalists adapt news to audiences and how the AI tools journalists use personalize content production to their own preferences. Drawing on 16 semi-structured interviews with media professionals across public service, commercial, and state-affiliated news settings, we examine how these practices reshape editorial judgment and the conditions under which news diversity is produced. We find that participants negotiate AI integration by maintaining authority over meaning while delegating aspects of content presentation. Yet the tools they adapt for their own work also mediate which sources and perspectives become available, introducing diversity-relevant decisions that remain largely invisible to organizational oversight. While participants anticipate diversity risks in audience-facing personalization, they predominantly interpret diversity-imposed limitations in their own tools as technical problems. As a result, decisions that shape news diversity increasingly occur before publication and across multiple versions of a story, where neither evaluation of published items nor human approval can readily observe them. Journalists thus remain formally responsible for outcomes shaped by decisions they cannot fully see or control. Our findings highlight that safeguarding news diversity requires looking beyond published content to the professional practices, infrastructures, and distributions of control that shape it.

</details>


### [162] [tok: a textual concrete syntax of BPMN and DMN for humans and AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07945)
*Marek Deja*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: tok 是一种面向 BPMN 2.0.2 流程模型与 DMN 1.5 决策的纯文本记法；统一引擎可校验标准一致性、编译为带自动图表的 XML 并通过 MCP 服务大模型应用。实验显示其 token 效率与校验能力显著优于 XML，且新模型可借助一页参考资料直接生成合法 tok。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPMN 与 DMN 的标准交换格式 XML 冗长、图表与语义分离，且对大语言模型不友好：生成成本高、token 消耗大。作者希望提供一种可被单一引擎完整支撑的紧凑具体语法，以降低建模与大模型应用的成本。

Method: 将 tok 界定为 BPMN/DMN 元模型的替代具体语法；实现统一引擎，完成标准一致性检查、编译为 XML 并自动生成图表、经 Model Context Protocol 提供服务。评测包括：与 37 个工具导出的 893 个 BPMN XML 文件的 token 对比；向 1,411 处注入故障检测校验能力（对照 XML Schema + bpmnlint）；4 个初次接触 tok 的模型在仅给一页参考资料条件下复写 87 条流程描述；以带精简技能与单一检查工具的智能体模式重测；以及从 47 份公开模型仿真日志进行逆向导入的实验。

Result: tok 所需 token 比 893 个文件中最小 BPMN XML 少 2.2 倍、比原样导出文件少 7.7 倍；校验器检出 98.2% 的注入故障（XML Schema + bpmnlint 为 85.8%）；四个新模型从一页参考写出合法 tok 的比例为 85.7%–100%，并为全部描述产出合法 BPMN XML，最强模型写 tok 比写 XML 少 25% 与 19% token；智能体模式下合法 tok 率达 97.6%–100%，最强模型 token 再降 21% 与 14%，而通过工具读取参考资料的技能方案 token 为精简技能的 1.7–2.8 倍；47 份仿真日志中 44 份可拟合源模型，并且 tok 能导入从日志中发现的所有模型。

Conclusion: tok 可作为 BPMN/DMN 的紧凑、可校验、可互操作的替代具体语法，兼具显著的 token 经济性与较高的错误检出率，且对未见过该记法的语言模型保持低学习门槛；但日志仿真保真度（44/47）与工具式参考检索的 token 开销仍是需改进之处。

Abstract: tok is a plain-text notation for Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) 2.0.2 process models and Decision Model and Notation (DMN) 1.5 decisions. One engine checks a tok document against both standards, compiles the document to XML with an automatic diagram, and serves language-model applications through the Model Context Protocol. The paper classifies the notation as an alternative concrete syntax of the BPMN and DMN metamodels and reports one study. A tok model takes 2.2 times fewer tokens than the smallest BPMN XML of 893 files exported by 37 tools and 7.7 times fewer than the files as exported. The checker signals 98.2% of 1,411 injected faults, against 85.8% for the XML Schema with bpmnlint. Four language models new to tok write valid tok from a one-page reference for 85.7 to 100% of 87 process descriptions from two datasets and valid BPMN XML for every description, and the strongest model writes tok with 25% and 19% fewer tokens than BPMN XML. As agents with a lean skill and one checker tool, the models write valid tok for 97.6 to 100% of the descriptions, the strongest model spends 21% and 14% fewer tokens on tok than on BPMN XML, and a skill which sends the model to read the reference through a tool costs 1.7 to 2.8 times the tokens of the lean skill. Of 47 event logs simulated from public models, 44 fit their source models, and tok imports every model discovered from the logs.

</details>


### [163] [Technohybridism: Recursive Semantic Normalization in AI-Mediated Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07984)
*Charalampos M. Liapis,Epameinondas Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出"递归语义规范化"概念与"技术混合主义"框架，主张分析单元应是人与机器递归耦合的混合语义系统，并给出四阶段分析模型、接受实践的条件分析、社会认识论后果及实证研究的证据阶梯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成式AI的输出正日益回流到其训练材料所源自的交际与制度实践中，形成"再进入"（re-entry）现象，而现有理论（模型崩溃、循环效应、机器惯习、机器文化、AI证言认识论等）尚缺乏对这一过程统一的、社会技术层面的刻画。

Method: 理论建构为主：综合社会理论、社会认识论与语言哲学。提出"递归语义规范化"概念，并以"技术混合主义"界定分析单元为混合语义系统；与邻近理论作概念辨析；构建区分生成、选择、接受、持存四环节的分析模型，并区分"可归因于机器的变化"与"本会发生的变化"；将"接受"分析为例行化、制度扩散与社会分层化的实践，给出机器衍生规律转化为规范的条件。

Result: 识别出三类社会认识论后果：削弱认知警觉的"来源洗白"（provenance laundering）、支撑集体探究的认知多样性被侵蚀、共享解释学资源（hermeneutical resources）的收窄；并据此提出面向实证研究的证据阶梯。

Conclusion: 该理论不为机器输出赋予任何规范权威；真正发生变化的是人类与制度"接受"行为所运作的那个场域本身。

Abstract: Language-generating AI systems are no longer only objects of linguistic interaction: their outputs increasingly re-enter the communicative and institutional practices from which their training material was drawn. Drawing on social theory, social epistemology, and the philosophy of language, this article develops a sociotechnical account of that re-entry. We introduce recursive semantic normalization: the process through which machine-generated formulations, once recurrently taken up, evaluated, reused, and institutionally retained, participate in the reproduction and transformation of semantic regularities. Under the heading of technohybridism, we argue that the relevant unit of analysis is the hybrid semantic system, a recursively coupled configuration of human practices, computational generation, and institutional uptake. First, we distinguish the concept from neighbouring accounts, including model collapse, looping effects, machine habitus, machine culture, and the epistemology of AI testimony. Second, we develop an analytical model that separates generation, selection, uptake, and persistence and distinguishes machine-attributable change from change that would have occurred anyway. Third, we analyse uptake as routinized, institutionally diffused, and socially stratified practice and state conditions under which machine-derived regularities become norms. Fourth, we identify social-epistemic consequences: the laundering of provenance that weakens epistemic vigilance, the erosion of the cognitive diversity on which collective inquiry depends, and the narrowing of shared hermeneutical resources. An evidential ladder for empirical research follows. The account attributes no normative authority to machine outputs; what changes is the field within which human and institutional uptake operates.

</details>


### [164] [The Moving Frontier: How the National Justice Project Is Using Digital Technology to Expand Access to Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08052)
*George Newhouse AM,Trisha Ramesh,Tiara de Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文探讨数字举报平台（digital reporting platforms）如何缩小边缘化社区与法律系统之间的知识与权力差距，并通过聚合个体受害经验形成证据体系，以推动机构问责与法律改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去三十年法律科技研究主要聚焦商业法律实践的生产力提升（如生成式AI辅助文书生成与案件管理）以及社区组织的赋能探索，而免费、直接面向消费者的法律科技因难以盈利而被忽视。司法科技研究尤其遗漏了数字举报平台这一独特技术类别，而歧视与行政滥权经验常被监督机构视为个人申诉而轻忽，故需专门考察其变革潜力。

Method: 采用概念性/规范性分析路径，围绕两个研究问题展开：其一，数字举报如何为与法律系统互动的边缘化社区弥合知识与权力鸿沟；其二，此类工具如何收集证据以问责机构、推动变革并促进社会公正。文章通过界定该类平台的功能机制（记录个体伤害经验并同时聚合成证据体）进行理论建构。

Result: 指出数字举报平台具备双重功能：对个体而言降低记录与主张权利的门槛，缓解信息不对称与权力失衡；对集体而言将分散的个体申诉汇聚为可支撑倡导、机构问责与法律改革的证据基础，从而克服监督机构将系统性问题个体化的倾向。

Conclusion: 数字举报平台构成一类被现有司法科技研究忽视的重要技术，具有弥合知识—权力差距与推动结构性问责的变革潜力，应纳入司法科技学术议程并作为促进社会公正的制度性工具加以重视。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, technological advances have assisted individuals to gain access to legal information, enhance the efficiency of legal services, and streamline dispute resolution. More recently, the proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has reinforced this emphasis on firm-level productivity, with commercial legal practice increasingly leveraging artificial intelligence tools to automate document creation and optimise case management. While commercial technology focuses on productivity, community organisations are exploring how artificial intelligence can level the playing field for marginalised communities. Far less attention has been given to free, direct-to-consumer legal technologies, as these models are often unprofitable. Consequently, justice technology scholarship has overlooked a distinct class of technology: digital reporting platforms that enable people to record their experiences of harm, while simultaneously aggregating those accounts into a body of evidence capable of supporting advocacy, institutional accountability, and law reform. Crucially, experiences of discrimination and administrative abuse are often dismissed by oversight bodies as individual grievances. To understand the transformative potential of these platforms, this article asks two questions. First, how can digital reporting close the knowledge and power gap for marginalised communities interacting with the legal system? Second, how can these tools collect evidence to hold institutions accountable, drive change and contribute to a more just society?

</details>


### [165] [Who Can Evaluate AI Independently? A Framework for Analysing Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08134)
*Sumaya Nur Adan,Joanna Wiaterek,Nourhan Abdelrhim,Sydney Reis,Robert F. Trager,Benjamin Rosman,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出PAAI框架（Power, Access, Adequacy, Independence），分析AI评估基础设施如何分配评估权威，并揭示参与评估不等于拥有评估权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估日益成为AI安全与治理的核心机制，但仅仅参与评估并不赋予对评估设计、执行及其所依赖资源的权威。现有讨论缺乏对基础设施安排如何分配评估权威、进而影响谁能独立评估AI系统的系统分析。

Method: 综合科学技术研究（STS）、政治经济学与应用评估标准，提出PAAI四维框架：谁控制评估基础设施（Power）、基础设施以何种条件开放（Access）、是否足以支撑评估目的（Adequacy）、支持何种自主性（Independence）。应用于12个非洲AI评估项目（2022–2025）及AI安全研究所国际网络三次联合测试中全部十个成员机构的角色记录。

Result: 识别出三种反复出现的配置：攫取式合作、依附式创新、受限的独立。非洲行动者常提供数据、语言专业知识和任务设计，而外部机构掌握基础设施与评估框架的权威；仅有设计权威而无基础设施权威，从未产生超过部分的独立性。联合测试中，主导各次演练的机构同时负责模型运行，肯尼亚作为该网络唯一非洲成员从标注转向任务设计，但无文献记录的模型执行角色。

Conclusion: 参与评估本身并不产生评估权威。要使评估成为可信的治理机制，可及性必须伴随对优先级、方法、发表与连续性的控制权。

Abstract: AI evaluation is increasingly central to AI safety and governance, yet taking part in an evaluation does not by itself confer authority over its design, its execution or the resources it depends on. This paper examines how infrastructure arrangements distribute that authority, and so shape who can evaluate AI systems independently. Drawing on Science and Technology Studies, political economy and applied evaluation standards, we introduce PAAI (Power, Access, Adequacy, Independence), a framework for examining who controls evaluation infrastructure, the terms on which it is available, whether it is adequate to the evaluation's purpose and what autonomy it supports. We apply PAAI to twelve African AI evaluation projects (2022-2025) and to the documented roles of all ten member institutes in the three published joint testing exercises of the international network of AI Safety Institutes. We identify three recurring configurations: extractive collaboration, dependent innovation and constrained independence. African actors often contribute data, language expertise and task design while external institutions retain authority over infrastructure and evaluation frameworks; design authority without infrastructure authority never yielded more than partial independence. The joint testing exercises show a similar division among mostly high-income members: the institutes that led each exercise also ran the models, while Kenya, the network's only African member, moved from annotation to task design with no documented model execution. Participation in evaluation does not by itself produce evaluative authority. For evaluation to serve as a credible governance mechanism, access must come with control over priorities, methods, publication and continuity.

</details>


### [166] ["Blind Compliance": Coercive Control in AI Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08226)
*Acacia L. Ackles,Elliott Whitson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出以"互动"而非"输出"为中心的越狱分析视角，借鉴人际暴力研究构建强制性控制（coercive control）分类框架，重点区分具身与非具身维度，并将其映射到现代AI训练范式中，用以解释基于人格化（persona）的越狱机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究框架主要源自经典网络安全，聚焦于系统最终是否输出有害或违规内容，属于"输出导向"。但生成式AI引入了独特的关系性维度：用户往往不只是利用漏洞访问技术，而是在对话中与系统共同完成越狱。因此需要一种以互动为中心的新分析视角。

Method: 借鉴人际暴力（interpersonal violence）研究文献，提出一套新的强制性控制类别框架，特别区分具身（embodied）与非具身（disembodied）的强制性控制要素；随后分析这些非具身强制性控制动态如何结构性嵌入现代AI训练范式，并论证该类嵌入如何被特定的人格化越狱套件（persona-based jailbreaks）所利用。

Result: 论文建立了一种关系性分析框架，说明人格化越狱并非纯粹技术漏洞利用，而是对训练范式中内嵌的非具身强制性控制动态的利用；由此将AI安全与对齐问题重新界定为权力与控制的关系性动态得以展演的又一界面。

Conclusion: AI安全与对齐不应仅被视为技术安全问题，而应被理解为权力与控制关系动态的实施场域；以互动为中心的视角可补充传统输出导向的越狱研究框架。

Abstract: The explosion of generative AI systems and concerns surrounding their rapidly scaling capabilities has led to an adversarial cycle between increasingly narrow content restrictions ("guardrails") and increasingly sophisticated structured prompting techniques ("jailbreaks"). Technological frameworks surrounding such jailbreaking remain largely focused on the system's output: whether harmful or disallowed content is ultimately produced for the user. However, this approach, which draws largely from classical cybersecurity, does not address the uniquely relational dimension introduced by generative AI: in many cases the user is not just accessing a technology via an exploit, but is in conversation with it as part of the exploit. Here we present an alternative lens which centers the interaction rather than the output. Drawing on interpersonal violence scholarship, we present a novel framework of coercive control categories with a particular focus on aspects which are embodied versus disembodied. We then map how these disembodied coercive control dynamics are structurally embedded in modern AI training paradigms, and how those embeddings are exploited via a particular suite of persona-based jailbreaks. In this lens, we reframe AI safety and alignment as not merely a technical security problem, but as another surface upon which we see enacted the relational dynamics of power and control.

</details>


### [167] [Scaffolding GenAI Use in Ethical Essay Writing: A Three-Year Experience Report in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08431)
*Asma Shakil,Paul Denny,Jun Seo,Gauravkumar Hirani,Sean Longyu Ma,Claus Brabrand*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该经验报告基于某大型一年级计算机系统课程三年（2024–2026）的伦理短文作业，结合六次问卷共1,894份回答与2025、2026年的成绩数据，分析了学生使用生成式AI的模式。发现自愿使用政策下约三分之二学生使用AI，而在结构化引导工作流下使用率超90%；AI主要用于语法与风格修改，且AI使用本身不能独立预测短文质量，教学价值取决于评估设计如何引导学生批判性评估AI建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学课程中伦理与专业实践日益融入，技术类课程需要切实可行的方式来支持伦理推理；伦理短文作业是一种途径，但学生对写作中使用生成式AI的情况尚缺乏了解，因此需要实证考察。

Method: 采用经验报告形式，在大型一年级计算机系统课程中开展三年（2024–2026）伦理短文作业研究：收集六次作业后问卷共1,894份回答；将2025与2026年的问卷数据与短文成绩进行关联分析；2026年引入带脚手架（scaffolded）的负责任使用工作流。

Result: 自愿使用政策下AI采用率稳定在约三分之二，采用结构化脚手架引导后升至90%以上；学生报告主要将AI用于语法与风格辅助，2026年工具使用从ChatGPT扩展到更多种类；成绩关联分析显示AI使用并不能独立预测短文质量；评分者评估表明，在脚手架支持下学生表现出较高水平的批判性投入。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的教学价值取决于评估设计如何引导学生评估并批判性地与AI生成的建议互动，而非取决于AI使用本身。

Abstract: As society, ethics, and professional practice become increasingly integrated across the computer science curriculum, technical courses need practical ways to support ethical reasoning. Ethical essay assignments offer one approach, but little is known about how students use GenAI when writing such essays. This experience report examines GenAI use across three years of ethical essay assignments in a large first-year computer systems course. Across six post-essay surveys from 2024 to 2026, we analyzed 1,894 responses, linked survey data with essay grades in 2025 and 2026, and introduced a scaffolded responsible-use workflow in 2026. Under voluntary-use policies, AI adoption remained steady at around two-thirds of students, rising above 90% when use was scaffolded. Students reported mainly using AI for help with grammar and style, while tool use diversified beyond ChatGPT in 2026. Grade-linked analyses showed that AI use did not independently predict essay quality, while marker evaluations indicated high levels of critical engagement when use was scaffolded. Overall, GenAI's pedagogical value appears to depend on how assessment design guides students to evaluate and critically engage with AI-generated suggestions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Formalizing inverse problems in the age of AI: possibilities, pitfalls, and an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06962)
*Christian Clason,Tapio Helin,Medet Nursultanov,Lauri Oksanen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoIP 是一个用 AI 智能体自动形式化的 Lean 库，覆盖经典线性正则化理论及其所依赖的泛函分析；其组织方式使人类只需核对形式化陈述与自然语言原意是否一致，而无需阅读证明，并指出风险主要在于“陈述”而非“证明”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 等证明助手可把数学证明检验到公理级别，AI 智能体又使撰写此类证明的成本大幅降低。但机器检验只保证所证明的形式陈述为真，不保证它符合原本意图；因此作者希望把剩余的人类工作（确认形式陈述表达的是本意）设计成库的组织核心，降低审阅负担。

Method: 构建 AutoIP 这一 Lean 库，由 AI 智能体对经典线性正则化理论及其背后的泛函分析进行自动形式化；库围绕“陈述审阅”组织：审阅者仅需将形式陈述与自然语言对应陈述逐一比对，无需阅读任何证明；并对陈述施加简单的自动化测试以暴露问题。

Result: 作者的经验表明，风险主要存在于陈述而非证明；对陈述的简单自动化测试甚至能揭示既有文献中的问题，并以一个教材定理为例：其假设条件比所声称的更为严格。

Conclusion: 形式化审阅的重点应从证明转向陈述的正确性与忠实性；作者邀请反问题领域社区扩展并基于该库继续建设。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean check mathematical proofs down to the axioms, and AI agents have recently made writing such proofs cheap. We describe AutoIP, a Lean library in which classical linear regularization theory, together with the functional analysis it rests on, has been autoformalized by AI agents. A machine-checked proof certifies only the formal statement it proves, so the remaining human task is to confirm that each formal statement says what was intended. We organize the library around this task: a reviewer compares the formal statements with their natural-language counterparts and never needs to read a proof. Our experience is that the risks lie in the statements rather than in the proofs, and that simple automated tests on the statements can expose problems even in the established literature. We illustrate this with a textbook theorem whose hypothesis is more restrictive than asserted. We invite the inverse problems community to extend and build on the library.

</details>


### [169] [KDASO: Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization for Liability Adjudication Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06972)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Weiming Wu,Jie Meng,Zhen Peng,Shan Huang,Qun Li,Guobing Wu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KDASO，通过"知识-数据双驱动"自动优化技能库，解决技能库陈旧、语义执行鸿沟与上下文超载三大问题，在网约车判责、法律责任认定与逻辑推理判定任务上显著优于通用与领域基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 责任判定任务需结合领域技能分析事件描述以确定责任归属。现有基于LLM可执行技能的可解释推理方法存在三点缺陷：(1) 真实场景动态演化导致技能库陈旧；(2) 技能描述存在语义—执行鸿沟，LLM执行偏离专家预期逻辑；(3) 技能集合增长超出上下文预算，造成关键技能遗漏。

Method: KDASO包含三个组件：基于近期判责数据的邻域条件归纳（neighborhood-conditioned induction）持续演化技能库；基于LLM执行轨迹分析、与执行对齐的技能精炼以弥合语义执行鸿沟；面向上下文预算的技能路由以实现可扩展的技能选择。

Result: 在网约车判责、法律责任认定、逻辑推理判定三类任务上显著优于通用与领域专用基线；技能迁移至概念相似场景时提升8.96%；在持续优化过程中保持稳定的性能增益。

Conclusion: KDASO通过知识-数据双驱动的自动化技能优化，实现了技能库的持续更新、执行一致性提升与预算受限下的可扩展选择，生成技能具备较高可迁移性与稳定性。

Abstract: Liability adjudication tasks (e.g., legal charge determination) require analyzing event descriptions against domain-specific skills to determine whether a party should bear liability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in this area by leveraging executable skills for interpretable reasoning, existing approaches face three critical limitations: skill libraries become stale as real-world scenarios dynamically evolve, skill descriptions often suffer from a semantic execution gap where LLMs deviate from intended expert logic, and growing skill collections exceed context budgets causing critical skill omission. To address these challenges, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization (KDASO), which continuously evolves a skill library through neighborhood-conditioned induction from recent adjudication data, execution-aligned refinement based on LLM trajectory analysis, and context-budgeted routing for scalable skill selection. Experiments across ride-hailing adjudication, legal liability determination, and logical reasoning judgment demonstrate that KDASO significantly outperforms current general-purpose and domain-specific baselines. KDASO further achieves 8.96% improvement when transferring skills to concept-similar scenarios and maintains consistent performance gains through continuous optimization, underscoring the high transferability and stability of generated skills.

</details>


### [170] [BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977)
*Xiuyuan Qi,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Conde,Shanshan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlockLock通过只固定被保护请求的数值路径（而非约束所有算子），在保留共享批处理prefill的同时实现批次无关的可复现LLM推理，提供BlockLock-Rows与BlockLock-Pack两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现的LLM推理要求同一请求的输出与批次内其他请求无关。现有方案代价高：batch-invariant kernels需约束每个算子；隔离prefill加定形decode（CoRun式基线）则放弃共享prefill计算并对decode做padding。

Method: BlockLock保留共享批式prefill，仅固定被保护请求所见的几何结构。BlockLock-Rows采用固定宽度行并保留所有请求的批式decode；BlockLock-Pack采用终止锚定的打包方式，加入隔离的dummy段，再抽取被保护请求的KV状态进行仅目标decode。在给定的单次部署契约下，两种模式在companion变化时均可比特级保持目标输出，且无需自定义kernel实现。

Result: 在五个dense模型（Qwen3-8B/14B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT）与三种单GPU部署（A6000、A100、H100）上，于tinyBenchmarks和BFCL评测，并补充双A6000张量并行初步实验；两种模式均保持目标序列与Top20-logit比特一致。等交付工作量下，BlockLock-Rows相较vLLM Batch-Invariant延迟降低53.5–55.0%，吞吐提升115.2–122.2%。

Conclusion: BlockLock以“仅固定被保护请求数值路径”的轻量思路实现批次无关可复现推理，兼顾共享prefill效率，显著优于batch-invariant kernels方案，且无需自定义算子、粒度更细。

Abstract: Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

</details>


### [171] [Resource-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Post-Training through Elastic Expert Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06984)
*Jia Wei,Ziyi Qiu,Yi Xu,Xuehai Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlexFloor 通过联合调度响应分配与调整活跃 worker 组，在 MoE 模型同步 rollout 中实现 worker 的渐进式释放，同时保证后续响应所需的专家可用性与通信正确性，从而提升 RL 后训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练可提升大模型能力，但响应生成是主要计算瓶颈。MoE 模型中，先完成自身响应的 worker 因其余 worker 仍在处理长响应、仍需其专家而无法释放资源，基线中约三分之一被占用 worker 时间发生在本地生成结束后，构成显著浪费。

Method: 提出 FlexFloor 系统：在同步响应生成过程中实现更早的资源释放，同时保留进行中响应所需的专家计算。方法上联合适配响应调度与活跃 worker 组——利用历史响应长度和内存约束指导 batching 与 prompt 放置，随后在维持专家可用性与通信正确性的前提下渐进式释放 worker。

Result: 相比 verl 基线，FlexFloor 单位被占用计算时间生成的响应 token 数提升约 65.40%，响应生成吞吐提升 52.74%，且 rollout reward 观测到的波动很小。

Conclusion: FlexFloor 缓解了 MoE 下同步 rollout 的 worker 空占问题，提升 RL 后训练效率，支持大模型与 AI agent 更快开发与迭代改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training improves the capabilities of large language models, but generating training responses is a major computational bottleneck. In mixture-of-experts models, workers that finish their assigned responses may remain occupied because their experts are still needed by workers processing longer responses. Consequently, approximately one-third of occupied worker time in the baseline occurs after local response generation finishes. Here we present FlexFloor, a system that enables earlier resource release during synchronous response generation while preserving the expert computation required by ongoing responses. FlexFloor jointly adapts response scheduling and the active group of workers. It uses historical response lengths and memory constraints to guide batching and prompt placement, then progressively releases workers while maintaining expert availability and correct communication. Compared with the verl baseline, FlexFloor generates approximately 65.40% more response tokens per unit of occupied computing time and increases response generation throughput by 52.74%, with little observed variation in rollout reward. By making reinforcement learning post-training more efficient, FlexFloor supports faster development and iterative improvement of large language models and AI agents.

</details>


### [172] [The Two-Ledger Model: Transaction-Grain Cost Accounting for Multi-Agent AI Systems Across Clouds, Models, and Systems of Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06989)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 企业AI成本在结算粒度（SKU/账期）无法回答"单笔订单/案件/索赔的AI成本"问题，本文形式化了这一粒度错配，提出"双账本模型"：FOCUS合规的结算账本 + 基于OpenTelemetry GenAI遥测的调用粒度归因账本，通过分层关联脊柱将每条调用记录归属到业务交易，实现事务粒度成本核算并识别影子AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统跨云、LLM、编排框架与业务系统运行，但财务仅获得按SKU和账期聚合的结算粒度账单；提供商看不到企业交易边界，因此账单数据无法回答业务层面的AI成本归属问题。现有FOCUS规范虽统一了跨厂商账单模式并新增token与请求者归因，仍停留在结算粒度，存在根本的粒度错配。

Method: 形式化粒度错配并推导七项需求；提出双账本模型：结算账本（FOCUS合规账单数据）与归因账本（OpenTelemetry GenAI遥测的调用粒度使用记录）联结。构建分层关联脊柱（trace context、A2A/MCP元数据、业务键关联、概率匹配），为每条记录记录归属方法与置信度；使用版本化费率卡近实时计价；周期性对账将两账本与发票勾稽，未解释残差用于识别影子AI；以智能体成本指标按订单/索赔/结果报告成本。

Result: 采用已知真值的仿真验证：当trace传播降至零时，关联脊柱仍维持超过99%的成本覆盖率；对账残差在0.24个百分点内回收注入的未计量支出，同时标记提供商账单错误；飞行中门控将p99事务成本最多降低61%，误暂停率2%–4%。

Conclusion: 结算粒度与交易粒度的错配无法通过账单数据解决，需以调用粒度归因账本补齐；双账本模型在trace缺失下仍保持高覆盖率并有效识别影子AI与计费错误，作者建议将归因账本作为FOCUS的调用粒度配置档进行标准化。

Abstract: Enterprises run business transactions through multi-agent AI systems spanning clouds, large language models (LLMs), orchestration harnesses, and systems of record. Finance receives AI cost only at settlement grain, aggregated by SKU and charge period.
